§0.5 `tp_unconstrained` cites S04-R-21; §9.1 (`billed_cum` is owned by stage 10, `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S01-R-19) and `RecognitionState.allocated`; §10.2.1 and S10-R-01 per-document attribution (POLICIES ALG-02 step 3; `_coverage/answer-keys-fx-entity-books:OQ-AK-03`); §10.5 `billed_cum` node; S13-R-11, S14-R-04 and S15-R-13 cite the published `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.5, §0.4 and S08-R-14; §16.4 adds D-25b, D-76 and D-77.<br>**Decisions taken in B3 (D-77):**<br>1. Rev 1.1 is not issued. The revision number moves to 1.2 to match the B3 fix pass of the other design documents.<br>2. A `DELIVERY_RECORDED` whose trigger the resolved POL-095 option does not permit is refused at submission. If such an event reaches the engine (for example through a replay of a stream recorded before the option was set), stage 09 counts the quantity as delivered without a control transfer and records trace params `reason = TRIGGER_NOT_PERMITTED`. No finding is raised.<br>3. Stage 10 emits the `billed_cum` node for every obligation. Stage 01 emits only `delivered_quantity_cum` and `returned_quantity_cum` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S01-R-19 governs the rev 1.0 wording of §9.1 and §10.5).<br>4. The unreferenced lines of one billing document are summed and apportioned once, with the sign applied after apportioning the absolute amount (ALG-02 step 3). A line with `obligation_key` is attributed directly.<br>5. Net JET-11 accretion is attributed to the obligations for which S04-R-10 requires a financing adjustment, weighted by posted allocation, or by resolved SSP when those allocations sum to 0.<br>6. In the impairment test, the costs of anticipated renewals and extensions are the costs included in the `EAC` version effective at the period end. The preparer includes them whenever the `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version in force includes renewals. No data member is added.<br>7. Monetary-liability layers follow POLICIES rev 1.2 decisions 3 to 5: one layer per balance role and source event, created at spot; JET-10d at recognition only when the recognition relieves contract-liability layers at historical carrying; settlement at spot on the cumulatively rounded carrying share.<br>8. 04 rev 1.2 holds no settlement event for consideration payable. A consideration-payable layer therefore stays open and is remeasured at every period end (POLICIES rev 1.2 decision 5).<br>9. S12-R-12 keeps its id and is amended, not retired, because its subject (movement-kind storage) persists; only the interim storage is withdrawn.<br>10. `RecognitionState.allocated` carries the `AllocatedState` consumed by stage 09, so that stage 15 can call `decompose_prior_period` (§9.1).<br>Change record: `docs/reviews/B3-fix-engine-spec.md` |

This file specifies stages 09 to 15 of the calculation pipeline. `docs/accounting/ENGINE_SPEC.md` holds the index of both files, the conventions of §0 and stages 01 to 08. Where §0 below restates a convention of `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0 and the two differ, `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0 governs and this file is corrected mechanically (OQ-B-01).

---

## 0. How to read this file

### 0.1 Scope

| Stage | Title | Engine or platform | 05 pipeline stage | 05 engine modules |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 09 | Recognition and schedules | Engine | STG-07, STG-08; the breakage and royalty parts of STG-09 | EMOD-02, EMOD-09, EMOD-10 (recognition of segments), EMOD-11 (exercise and expiry recognition), EMOD-15 (revenue target), EMOD-19, EMOD-20, EMOD-24 (sums the catch-up nodes of stages 06 and 08) |
| 10 | Billing and balances | Engine | STG-10 (position); refund-liability targets of STG-09 | EMOD-13, EMOD-15 (refund liability and return asset targets) |
| 11 | Contract costs and loss contracts | Engine | STG-07 (cost schedules), STG-09 | EMOD-16, EMOD-17 |
| 12 | Foreign currency and multi-entity | Engine | STG-10 (layers), STG-11 (intercompany pairs) | EMOD-14, EMOD-27 (JET-13 part) |
| 13 | Books | Engine | STG-02 to STG-12 (memoisation), LEGACY book fold | EMOD-26 |
| 14 | Journal derivation | Engine (posting intents) and platform (summarisation) | STG-11, STG-14 | EMOD-25, EMOD-27 |
| 15 | Close projections and disclosures | Engine (per-group disclosure measures) and platform (aggregation, snapshots, variance) | STG-12 (trace nodes), STG-15 | EMOD-24 (aggregates the prior-period targets of stage 08), EMOD-28 |

Stages 01 to 08 (`ENGINE_SPEC.md`) produce the canonical bundle and quantity ledger (stage 01), the Step 1 gate per book (02), the obligations (03), the transaction price, including VC estimates, expected returns as a targeted price reduction, consideration payable, financing, noncash consideration and taxes (04), SSP resolution and allocation (05), and the allocation effects of modifications (06), onboarding (07) and estimate changes and late events (08) (ALG-04, ALG-05, ALG-10; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.2-A). Stage 09 consumes their result through the contract of §0.5 and `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.11.

The measurement of warranty provisions (EMOD-21), customer incentive assets (EMOD-22), noncash consideration assets (EMOD-23), deposit liabilities (EMOD-12) and financing accretion (EMOD-18) is produced by stages 02 and 04 (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.11-A). Stage 14 consumes those targets and derives their journal lines (JET-01b, JET-11, JET-14, JET-16, JET-17).

### 0.2 Conventions restated

| Id | Convention | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| C-01 | **Numbers.** Exact values and ratios are `fractions.Fraction`. Posted amounts are `int` minor units of the currency's ISO 4217 exponent μ from the bundle currency table. `round(x)` means `erev_engine.money.round_half_up(x, μ)`. Every apportionment calls `largest_remainder`. Every posted cumulative amount calls `cumulative_posted` (ALG-01 §2.1.3 as confirmed by D-11a). No stage in this file quantises by any other path | D-11, D-11a; DG-KRN-MONEY-01 to 04; ENG-04; ENGINE_SPEC CV-30 to CV-36 |
| C-02 | **Signs.** Net position follows D-12 (positive means contract liability). Cumulative targets are signed. A posting intent carries a side (`D` or `C`) and a non-negative amount; a negative economic effect swaps sides (POLICIES §0.9, JET R4). The orchestrator converts intents to signed `subledger_line.amount_txn` (debit positive) | D-12; POLICIES §0.9; 04 §8.0; ENGINE_SPEC CV-37 |
| C-03 | **Order.** Events in ENG-06 order `(effective_date, record_seq, event_key)`. Periods ascending by start date. Obligations ascending by subject key (Unicode code point). Entities ascending by code. Books `ASC606`, `IFRS15`, `LEGACY` (RCP-11). Every iteration over a mapping or set is sorted by the stated key | ENG-05, ENG-06; DG-ENG-02; ENGINE_SPEC CV-20 to CV-24 |
| C-04 | **Periods.** The period of a date is taken from the calendar of the entity that owns the amount: the performing entity for revenue schedule lines (T-ENG-02), the contracting entity for billing and contract balances, the asset-owning entity for cost assets. Period keys are `FY<year>-P<nn>` (T-REF-05) | D-19; T-ENG-02; T-REF-05; ENGINE_SPEC CV-12 |
| C-05 | **Horizons.** The evaluation horizon of an (entity, book) is the last period whose bundle state is `open`, `closing` or `reopened` (RCP-03). Cumulative targets are evaluated at every period end from the group's inception period through the horizon. Deterministic schedules — those of the components S09-R-45 calls deterministic (rev 1.126) — extend to the period containing the last end date of the subject. **No period inside the horizon (rev 1.100; supervisor ruling R-107 (a) of 2026-09-30).** When the group's inception period lies after the horizon of a contracting entity — a contract booked ahead of the entity's open periods — the set of period ends is empty for that entity: nothing is evaluated, presented or posted for it, and the computation succeeds; it never raises (S10-R-22, S12-R-13). The set is per contracting entity, so an entity whose periods are behind does not stop another entity of the same group. The version's own measures (04 T-CON-08 and T-CON-11) remain those of the version date, whatever the horizon, and are not postings; the allocation and the deterministic schedules are those of any other contract | RCP-03; ENGINE_SPEC CV-13 |
| C-06 | **Targets, not movements.** Every stage returns cumulative targets per (book, entity, subject, measure, period end). Period amounts, catch-ups and posting intents are differences of cumulative targets. A repeated compute over an unchanged bundle emits no intent | RCP-06; PROP:P9; ENGINE_SPEC CV-14 |
| C-07 | **Trace.** Node id `<measure>:<subject key>:<period key or '-'>` (DG-KRN-EXP-02). Formula ids `<area>.<name>.v<n>` registered in `erev_engine/formulas.py` (RCP-25). A posted-amount node stores `format_money` and `rounding_residue = exact − posted` (DG-KRN-EXP-03). Measures that equal a 04 column use the column name (DG-OQ-05) | DG-KRN-EXP-01 to 04; B1 §6.2 obligation 2; ENGINE_SPEC CV-50 to CV-56 |
| C-08 | **Findings.** Codes are 04 §15.4 literals with finding severity `ERROR`, `WARNING` or `INFO` (D-73). A finding with severity `ERROR` makes the computation `QUARANTINED` (ENG-09). A code this file needs that 04 does not yet catalogue is written "proposed" with its open question; the build loop uses it only after the supervisor adopts it. Rev 1.2: D-76 adopted every identifier that rev 1.0 proposed, and 04 rev 1.2 or POLICIES rev 1.2 publishes each one (§16.3), so none remains proposed | D-73; D-76; 04 §15.4; ENG-09; ENGINE_SPEC CV-04, CV-15, CV-40 to CV-45 |
| C-09 | **Purity and failure.** Stages obey DG-ENG-01 to DG-ENG-12. A stage never returns partial output; an invariant violation raises `EngineError("ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED")` with the failing identity in `detail`, persisted as exception `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` (DG-ENG-05; 04 table 15.4-D) | DG-ENG-01 to 12; RCP-20; ENGINE_SPEC CV-46 |

### 0.3 Subject keys

| Subject | Subject key | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Obligation | `<contract external_id>/<obligation_key>` | `SF-ORD-10003/POB-01` |
| Contract and contracting entity | `<contract external_id>@<entity code>` | `SF-ORD-10003@AVM-US` |
| Combination group and entity | `<group code>@<entity code>` | `CG-CON-000012@AVM-US` |
| Loss unit | the contract subject key (POL-150 `CONTRACT`) or the obligation subject key (`POB`) | `SF-ORD-10003` |
| Contract cost asset | `<contract external_id>/COST/<local event key of the capitalising event>` | `SF-ORD-20417/COST/EV-000812` |
| FX layer | `<group code>@<entity code>/<layer_key>`; `layer_key` = `<balance role>:<global event key>` (T-CON-18; CV-22), percent-encoded as one component | `CG-CON-000012@AVM-US/CONTRACT_LIABILITY%3ASF-ORD-10003%2FEV-000455` |
| Estimate element | `<contract external_id>/<element_code>`; portfolio elements `PORTFOLIO:<portfolio code>/<element_code>` | `SF-ORD-10003/EAC` |
| Recognition component | `<obligation subject key>#<component code>` (§9.2.1) | `SF-ORD-10003/POB-01#FIXED` |

Every component of a subject key is percent-encoded for `%`, `/`, `@`, `#` and `:` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` CV-21). The global event key is `<contract external_id>/EV-<stream_version, 6 digits>`; the local form `EV-nnnnnn` appears only inside keys already prefixed by the contract (CV-22). Keys are natural keys, never generated UUIDs (DG-ENG-02).

### 0.4 Stage modules

DG-ENG-07 requires stage modules under `backend/erev_engine/stages/` to take the names and order this specification fixes. Each stage is a package whose `__init__.py` exports exactly one entry function. Submodules are private to their stage.

| Stage | Package | Entry function | Submodules |
|---|---|---|---|
| 09 | `erev_engine/stages/s09_recognition/` | `run(ctx: BookContext, st: AllocatedState, tb: TraceBuilder) -> RecognitionState` | `components.py`, `progress_time.py`, `progress_events.py`, `progress_inputs.py`, `usage.py`, `royalty.py`, `breakage.py`, `returns.py`, `holds.py`, `manual.py`, `decompose.py`, `schedule.py` |
| 10 | `erev_engine/stages/s10_billing_balances/` | `run(ctx, st: RecognitionState, tb) -> BalanceState` | `billing.py`, `classification.py`, `refund_liability.py`, `position.py`, `reclass.py`, `current_split.py` |
| 11 | `erev_engine/stages/s11_costs_loss/` | `run(ctx, st: BalanceState, tb) -> CostLossState` | `capitalise.py`, `amortise.py`, `impair.py`, `loss.py` |
| 12 | `erev_engine/stages/s12_fx_entities/` | `run(ctx, st: CostLossState, tb) -> FxState` | `rates.py`, `layers.py`, `intercompany.py` |
| 13 | `erev_engine/stages/s13_books/` | `run_books(cb: CanonicalBundle, stages: Sequence[StageSpec]) -> tuple[BookResult, ...]` | `memo.py` (constant `STAGE_POLICY_KEYS`), `legacy_book.py`, `switches.py` |
| 14 | `erev_engine/stages/s14_posting/` | `run(ctx, st: FxState, tb) -> PostingState` | `templates.py` (constants `JET_PARTS`, `COUNTER_ROLES`), `amount_classes.py` (constant `AMOUNT_CLASSES`), `targets.py`, `assign.py`, `accounts.py`, `intents.py` |
| 15 | `erev_engine/stages/s15_disclosures/` | `run(ctx, st: PostingState, tb) -> DisclosureState` | `rpo.py`, `rollforward.py`, `prior_period.py`, `disaggregation.py`, `cost_rollforward.py` |

Stages 09 and 11 use the shared exact-progress functions of `erev_engine/progress.py` and the period helpers of `erev_engine/dates.py` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.2). Stages 02 to 08 may export several entry functions (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.2-A). Two stages of this file call exports of stage 08, with the published signatures: stage 14 calls `s08_estimates_late_events.assign_posting_period(ctx, entity, effective_date) -> Assignment` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §8.2, S08-R-08), and stage 15 calls `s08_estimates_late_events.decompose_prior_period(ctx, st.allocated, period_key, tb) -> tuple[Target, ...]` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §8.5, S08-R-14). The member `allocated` is the `AllocatedState` consumed by stage 09; `RecognitionState`, `BalanceState`, `CostLossState`, `FxState` and `PostingState` carry it unchanged (§9.1).

Platform modules for the platform parts of stages 14 and 15 (DG-LAY-04): `erev_api/domain/journals/summarize.py`, `erev_api/domain/journals/chunking.py`, `erev_api/domain/reports/disclosures/` (one module per E-65 kind), `erev_api/domain/reports/waterfall.py`, `erev_api/domain/reports/variance.py`, `erev_api/domain/close/snapshots.py`.

05 §3.4 and §3.7 delegate three engine constants to ENGINE_SPEC. This file fixes their modules: `AMOUNT_CLASSES` (RCP-07) in `s14_posting/amount_classes.py`; `COUNTER_ROLES` (05 §3.6.1) in `s14_posting/templates.py`; `STAGE_POLICY_KEYS` (RCP-12) in `s13_books/memo.py`, which also holds the entries of stages 04 to 08 published by `ENGINE_SPEC.md`.

Every stage of this file runs once per book inside `compute` (RCP-11). Stage 13 is the orchestration of books and memoisation around the other stages, not a step between stages 12 and 14; it is numbered 13 for the brief's order.

### 0.5 State consumed from stages 01 to 08

The fields below are the contract this file relies on. `ENGINE_SPEC.md` is authoritative for how each field is produced; a field named here and absent there is OQ-B-02.

```python
# erev_engine/stages/state.py (shared frozen dataclasses; field names equal 04 columns where a column exists)
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BookContext:
    book_code: BookCode                         # E-02
    framework: BookCode                         # ASC606 | IFRS15 | LEGACY
    currencies: CurrencyTable                   # minor units μ per ISO code
    txn_currency: str                           # single currency per group (D-75, 03 Q3)
    entities: Mapping[str, EntityView]          # code -> functional currency, time zone, calendar periods, period states
    horizon: Mapping[str, str]                  # entity code -> horizon period key (C-05)
    policies: PolicyResolver                    # value(code, *, contract=, obligation=, entity=, period=); resolved(...) -> ResolvedPolicyInput (CV-17)
    mapping: AccountMappingView                 # T-REF-15 PUBLISHED version pinned for the computation
    trigger: ComputationTrigger                 # E-87 plus DRY_RUN (05 §3.2)
    tenant_preset: str                          # "DEFAULT" | "LEGACY_PARITY"

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AllocationSegment:                        # one per boundary; ENGINE_SPEC §0.11 adds base_revenue_exact, progress_measure,
                                                # unit_ssp, remaining_ssp, remaining_billing_plan, modification_boundary_no
    component: str                              # "FIXED" | "PERIOD_VC" | "ROYALTY" (§9.2.1)
    effective_date: date
    event_key: str | None                       # boundary event; None for inception
    cause: SegmentCause                         # INCEPTION | MODIFICATION | TP_CHANGE | ESTIMATE_CHANGE |
                                                # MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISE | TERMINATION | OPENING_BALANCE
    basis: str                                  # "INCEPTION" (inception basis, 25-13(b), 32-43) | "PROSPECTIVE" (25-13(a))
    x_exact: Fraction                           # X: exact allocation of the component (currency units)
    a_posted: int                               # A: posted allocation (minor units, largest remainder)
    base_revenue_posted: int                    # R_k: posted cumulative revenue at the boundary (PROSPECTIVE only, else 0)
    base_progress: ProgressBase                 # quantities at the boundary: units, costs, hours, date (PROSPECTIVE only)
    totals: ProgressTotals                      # contracted quantity, EAC element code, term start and end after the boundary
    estimate_pair: tuple[str | None, str | None]  # (v_old, v_new) version keys for ESTIMATE_CHANGE and TP_CHANGE causes

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ObligationState:
    subject_key: str                            # §0.3
    contract_key: str
    obligation_key: str
    obligation_kind: ObligationKind             # E-18
    distinctness: Distinctness                  # E-105
    satisfaction_pattern: SatisfactionPattern   # E-19
    recognition_method: RecognitionMethod       # E-11; one measure per obligation version (REQ-REC-018)
    ratable_convention: RatableConvention | None  # E-21
    principal_agent: PrincipalAgent             # E-89
    licence_nature: LicenceNature               # E-90
    scope_flag: ScopeFlag                       # E-77
    start_date: date | None
    end_date: date | None
    recognition_start_date: date | None         # earliest date revenue may begin (V5; POL-025), set by stage 03 (S03-R-10)
    contracting_entity: str
    performing_entity: str
    quantity: Fraction                          # Q after modifications
    resolved_ssp: Fraction                      # for reclass fallbacks (POL-077 makes the contract total positive)
    revenue_category: str | None
    dimensions: Mapping[str, str]
    account_overrides: Mapping[str, str]        # role literal or "BILLING_CLEARING:<purpose>" -> account code
    segments: tuple[AllocationSegment, ...]     # ascending by (effective_date, event order); first cause INCEPTION or OPENING_BALANCE
    opening: OpeningBalanceView | None          # OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED payload values (POL-210, PT-11)
    terminated_on: date | None                  # CONTRACT_TERMINATED effective date for this obligation

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AllocatedState:
    group_code: str
    inception_date: date
    contracts: tuple[ContractView, ...]         # header fields incl. termination terms, status_in_book per book
    obligations: tuple[ObligationState, ...]
    events: tuple[EventView, ...]               # CanonicalBundle.events: ENG-06 order, voids resolved (S01-R-12)
    measure_events: tuple[EventView, ...]       # CanonicalBundle.measure_events (CV-11)
    ledger: QuantityLedger                      # S01-R-16 cumulative quantities per obligation
    return_paths: Mapping[str, ReturnPath]      # obligation subject key -> expected-returns path of stage 04 (§9.2.7; OQ-B-02)
    estimates: EstimatePins                     # pin(estimate_key, at, before=None) -> EstimateVersionView | None (S01-R-18)
    tp_unconstrained: Mapping[str, TpView]      # contract key -> unconstrained, credit-adjusted TP build-up by date
                                                # (stage 04, ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-21 in §4.3; used by §11.2.5)
    specialist_targets: SpecialistTargets       # EMOD-12, 18, 21, 22, 23 cumulative targets per period (§0.1)
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Target:                                   # one cumulative target point

    period_key: str

    node_id: str                                # trace node (C-07)

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Finding:

```

### 0.7 Section pattern

Every stage section has the same parts: inputs and outputs; pseudocode; invariants (`S<nn>-INV-<nn>`); findings and codes; trace nodes and formula ids; references (POL, ALG, JET, CHK, answer-key families of POLICIES §0.7, DEVIATIONS ids); worked examples (`EX-<nn>-<letter>`). Rules carry ids `S<nn>-R-<nn>`. Ids are stable and never renumbered; a retired id is marked `Retired`.

Pseudocode is Python 3.12 over the types above. `money.*` is `erev_engine.money` (DG §5.9), `dates.*` is `erev_engine/dates.py`, and `tb.node(...)` is `TraceBuilder.node` (DG §5.16). A helper whose body is not shown is specified in the prose of the same rule.

---

## 9. Stage 09: recognition and schedules

Stage 09 turns allocations into cumulative revenue targets per obligation and period, decomposes each period amount by cause, and publishes the versioned revenue schedule. It never posts; stage 14 derives postings from its targets.

### 9.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Produced by | Use in stage 09 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObligationState.segments` (X, A, basis, base quantities, updated totals) | stages 05 to 08 (ALG-04, ALG-05, ALG-10; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` CV-60 to CV-63) | The allocation in force at every date (§9.2.2) |
| `DELIVERY_RECORDED` (payload `trigger`), `RETURN_RECORDED`, `PROGRESS_RECORDED`, `MILESTONE_ACHIEVED`, `COST_INCURRED` (`purpose = PROGRESS_INPUT`), `USAGE_REPORTED`, `BILLING_RECORDED`, `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRED`, `HOLD_APPLIED`, `HOLD_RELEASED`, `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_APPLIED`, `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` | stage 01 (`measure_events`, `ledger`; ENG-06 order) | Progress, realised variable amounts, holds, manual adjustments |
| Estimate pins of kinds `EAC`, `BREAKAGE`, `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL`, `RETURN_RATE` (E-09) | stage 01 pin index (S01-R-18), applied by stage 08 | Progress totals, breakage, royalty accruals, expected returns |
| Resolved POL-003, POL-025 (through `recognition_start_date`), POL-051 to POL-053, POL-055 to POL-057, POL-090 to POL-094, POL-233, POL-240, POL-241 | `BookContext.policies` | Measure selection and parameters |
| `ContractView.status_in_book` per date | stage 02 | V4 guard |
| Judgement records topic `REPURCHASE_CLASSIFICATION`; bill-and-hold criteria record (S09-R-12) | bundle | Point-in-time control rules |
| Periods, calendars, horizon | `BookContext.entities`, `horizon` | Period ends and schedule extent (C-04, C-05) |

| Output (`RecognitionState`) | Content | Consumers |
|---|---|---|
| `revenue_targets` | `Target(measure = "revenue_cum")` per obligation, performing entity and period end, with exact values | stages 10, 11, 12, 14, 15 |
| `revenue_by_cause` | `Target(measure = "revenue", cause = <E-28 line type>)` per obligation and period (§9.2.10) | stage 14 (amount classes), stage 15, `/explain` |
| `schedule_lines` | `ScheduleLineOut` of kind `REVENUE` (T-ENG-02) | persistence, waterfall, RPO |
| `obligation_measures` | T-CON-11 values at the version date: `progress_ratio`, `revenue_cum`, `remaining_allocation`, `remaining_quantity`, `scheduled_amount`, `awaiting_trigger_amount`, `catch_up_amount`, `catch_up_cum`, `catch_up_modification_cum`, `catch_up_tp_change_cum`, `catch_up_estimate_cum`, `satisfaction_status`, `satisfied_date`, `hold_types`, `delivered_quantity`, `revenue_amount`, `ssp_delivered`, `ssp_delivered_cum` | persistence (T-CON-11), parity runner (DG-PAR-05) |
| `return_states` | `ReturnState` per returnable obligation and period end (§9.2.7) | stage 10 (JET-04b, JET-07c, JET-07d targets) |
| `escheat_components` | Unclaimed-property amounts at expiry of redemption-pattern obligations (§9.2.8) | stage 10 (refund liability component `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY`) |
| `close_gate_facts` | Royalty coverage gaps (§9.2.9) and bill-and-hold criteria gaps (S09-R-12) | platform close step `INVARIANTS` |
| `findings` | `Finding` rows (§9.4) | orchestrator |
| `allocated` | The `AllocatedState` consumed by stage 09, unchanged; `BalanceState`, `CostLossState`, `FxState` and `PostingState` carry the same member (rev 1.2) | stage 15 (`decompose_prior_period`, §0.4) |

Stage 09 measures progress with the shared functions of `erev_engine/progress.py` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.2; OQ-A-01), which stages 06 and 08 also use to measure catch-ups, so allocation and recognition read one progress function. The version-state nodes `delivered_quantity_cum` and `returned_quantity_cum` come from stage 01 (S01-R-19); stage 09 cites them as inputs. The `billed_cum` node is emitted by stage 10 for every obligation (S10-R-03; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S01-R-19; OQ-B-17 resolved by D-76).

### 9.2 Pseudocode

#### 9.2.1 Recognition components

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-01 | An obligation's revenue target is the sum of the posted targets of its components. Component `FIXED` carries the allocated fixed consideration and every constrained VC amount that is not allocated to distinct increments. Component `PERIOD_VC` carries VC allocated entirely to the distinct increment in which it is earned under 606-10-32-40 (REQ-POB-005, REQ-ALC-005): usage fees under POL-240 `DERIVED` and right-to-invoice amounts. Component `ROYALTY` carries sales- or usage-based royalties under 606-10-55-65 (POL-056, POL-057) |
| S09-R-02 | `FIXED` is recognised by the obligation's measure over its allocation segments (§9.2.2). `PERIOD_VC` and `ROYALTY` are realised amounts: for them X = A = the realised cumulative amount in minor units and f = 1 once the increment is satisfied, so ALG-01 §2.1.3 returns the realised amount itself. Stage 04 includes the same realised amounts in the transaction price, so V1 of 04 DB-17 (Σ `allocated_amount` = `transaction_price` − `consideration_payable_amount`, rev 1.2) holds at every version (OQ-B-02 resolved by D-76) |
| S09-R-03 | Rounding happens once per component: C_p(d) = Σ_c C_c(d). Each C_c is produced by `money.cumulative_posted`; `PERIOD_VC` and `ROYALTY` amounts are already minor units |

#### 9.2.2 Cumulative target of a `FIXED` component

```python
def fixed_target(ob: ObligationState, d: date, mu: int, ev: EventIndex) -> tuple[int, Fraction, Fraction]:
    """Returns (C posted, E exact, f progress) for date d. ev holds events with ENG-06 position at or before d."""
    seg = active_segment(ob.segments, d, ev)          # last segment whose boundary event precedes d in ENG-06 order
    if seg.basis == "INCEPTION":                       # inception, 25-13(b), 32-43 TP change, 25-13(c) class N
        f = progress.full(ob, seg, d, ev)              # §9.2.3 to §9.2.6 over the updated totals
        E = seg.x_exact * f
    else:                                              # "PROSPECTIVE": 25-13(a) class D, series increments
        b_k = seg.base_revenue_exact                   # CV-62: posted C_before(d_k) natively; E_before(d_k) for parity templates
        g = progress.since_boundary(ob, seg, d, ev)    # progress over the remaining scope, 0 at the boundary
        E = b_k + (seg.x_exact - b_k) * g              # CV-63; x_exact = b_k + exact pool share (ALG-04 §2.5.5)
        f = g
    if seg.x_exact == 0:
        return 0, Fraction(0), f                       # CV-63: a_posted is 0 as well
    phi = Fraction(1) if f == 1 else E / seg.x_exact
    C = money.cumulative_posted(seg.x_exact, seg.a_posted, phi, mu)   # D-11a bound and completion alignment
    return C, E, f
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-04 | Segment selection uses ENG-06 order: an event on date d with a lower `record_seq` than the boundary event is measured in the previous segment. A catch-up is therefore C_after(d) − C_before(d), both evaluated at the same progress (§9.2.10). Exception (D-92 (1)/(1a)): an `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` version (`EAC` or `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION`) whose effective date equals the date d of a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` of the same contract, recorded in the same date's batch with a lower `record_seq`, and whose estimate the amendment's lines change or the class D / class N re-measurement of the modified obligation reads, is the modification's updated estimate: it is not measured in the previous segment; the pre-boundary state (C_before, the pool, the S06-R-11 weights) is measured without it and it applies at the boundary (ENGINE_SPEC S06-R-15, S06-R-17). An unrelated same-date estimate keeps this rule's ordering; with several `CONTRACT_AMENDED` on one date the version belongs to the first amendment after it in `record_seq`, never an earlier one; a version recorded on d after the amendment keeps today's reading (it is the modification's estimate by S06-R-15 already) |
| S09-R-05 | A `PROSPECTIVE` segment starts at E = b_k (CV-62, CV-63), so a native boundary never creates a catch-up (S09-INV-09). This is the 25-13(a) behaviour of ALG-04 §2.5.5: the remaining allocation is recognised prospectively by the obligation's measure |
| S09-R-06 | A terminated obligation receives a `TERMINATION` segment from stage 06 with basis `INCEPTION`, `x_exact` = E(d_T), `a_posted` = C(d_T) and totals that give f = 1 from d_T on. Stage 09 applies it like any segment |
| S09-R-07 | `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` (POL-210 `OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER`, PT-11) creates, in stage 07, a segment with cause `OPENING_BALANCE` and basis `PROSPECTIVE` whose base is the imported `revenue_cum` and whose `x_exact` is the imported `revenue_cum + remaining_allocation` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §7). No revenue is recognised before the cutover date |

#### 9.2.3 Progress: time elapsed

```python
def progress_time(ob, seg, d, *, since_boundary: bool) -> Fraction:
    conv = ob.ratable_convention                      # E-21, pinned (POL-090 Pin K)
    s = seg.effective_date if since_boundary else seg.totals.term_start
    s = max(s, ob.recognition_start_date or s)        # V5 and POL-025 licence renewal start
    e = seg.totals.term_end
    return progress.time_fraction(conv, s, e, d, calendar=entity_calendar(ob.performing_entity))   # ALG-11
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-08 | `TIME_ELAPSED` progress is ALG-11 exactly, with the convention pinned on the obligation. A 25-13(a) boundary restarts the convention on [boundary date, updated end] (ALG-11 rule 5). CHK-140 to CHK-145 are the unit tests |
| S09-R-09 | A custodial obligation (E-18 `CUSTODIAL`, POL-094) is `TIME_ELAPSED` over its booked holding period, and its `recognition_start_date` is the later of its `start_date` and the first bill-and-hold control transfer of an obligation of the same contract (S09-R-12) |

#### 9.2.4 Progress: point in time and output measures

| Method (E-11) | Exact cumulative progress f(d) on the inception basis | g(d) since a prospective boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `POINT_IN_TIME` | min(1, N_c(d) ÷ Q′), N_c = Σ quantities of control-transferring `DELIVERY_RECORDED` events (S09-R-11) with position ≤ d, less returns under §9.2.7 | (N_c(d) − N_k) ÷ (Q′ − N_k) |
| `UNITS_DELIVERED` | as `POINT_IN_TIME`, every trigger counted | as above |
| `OUTPUT_PERCENT` | latest `PROGRESS_RECORDED.cumulative_progress_ratio` with `measure = OUTPUT_PERCENT` at or before d; 0 before the first | (p(d) − p_k) ÷ (1 − p_k) |
| `MILESTONE` | latest `MILESTONE_ACHIEVED.cumulative_weight` at or before d | (w(d) − w_k) ÷ (1 − w_k) |
| `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` | min(1, R(d) ÷ P): R = the right to invoice for performance to date (S09-R-18 source order), P = the line's stated price (rev 1.20, D-98 candidate 28). **P = 0 (an X = 0 component: a D-87 rate line)**: the component carries no revenue and its progress is time elapsed over the service term [`start_date`, `end_date`] under the resolved POL-090 convention — the completion signal S09-R-46 reads; a line without an end date never completes (f = 0, `reason = OPEN_TERM`). The arithmetic convention f = 1 for an empty denominator is never read for the status or the satisfied date (rev 1.22, ENC6-R3) | min(1, (R(d) − R_k) ÷ (P − R_k)), R_k the right to invoice before the boundary; 1 when P − R_k ≤ 0 with P > 0; P = 0 as on the inception basis |
| `REDEMPTION_PATTERN` | §9.2.8 | §9.2.8 |
| `MANUAL` | 0; revenue arises only through `MANUAL_RELEASE` (§9.2.11) | not applicable |

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-10 | Q′ is `totals.quantity` of the active segment. A remaining total of 0 gives g = 1 (CV-62); stage 06 raises `MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED` when a boundary's catch-up divisor is 0 while cumulative revenue is not (DEV-059; S06-R-17) |
| S09-R-11 | **Control-transfer triggers.** `DELIVERY_RECORDED.trigger` ∈ {`DELIVERY`, `ACCEPTANCE`, `SELL_THROUGH`, `CONTROL_TRANSFER`} transfers control. The preparer records the trigger that transfers control under the terms: a consignment shipment is not recorded as a delivery, and the sell-through report is recorded with trigger `SELL_THROUGH` (REQ-REC-013, 606-10-55-79, 55-80); under a subjective acceptance clause the acceptance is recorded with trigger `ACCEPTANCE`, and a trial lapse with `CONTROL_TRANSFER` (REQ-REC-014, 606-10-55-86 to 55-88). The control indicators relied on are recorded in the event's `source_ref` and evidence (REQ-REC-001). POL-095 `recognition.control_trigger` (levels P, O; pin K; OQ-B-03 resolved by D-76) restricts the permitted triggers: `ANY_TRANSFER` (default) permits every trigger above; `ACCEPTANCE_ONLY` (subjective acceptance clause) permits only `ACCEPTANCE` and `CONTROL_TRANSFER`; `SELL_THROUGH_ONLY` (consignment) permits only `SELL_THROUGH` and `CONTROL_TRANSFER`. The platform refuses a `DELIVERY_RECORDED` with a trigger the resolved option does not permit (422 `validation-failed`). A non-permitted trigger that reaches the engine (for example in a replay of a stream recorded before the option was set) counts as delivered without a control transfer: the amount stays awaiting trigger, and trace params record `reason = TRIGGER_NOT_PERMITTED`; no finding is raised |
| S09-R-12 | **Bill-and-hold.** Trigger `BILL_AND_HOLD` transfers control only when a REVIEWED judgement record with E-56 topic `BILL_AND_HOLD` exists for the obligation and its four 606-10-55-83 members `reason_substantive`, `identified_as_customer_product`, `ready_for_physical_transfer` and `cannot_use_or_direct_to_another_customer` are all `true` (04 T-CON-19 questionnaire schema; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.4-A; OQ-B-04 resolved by D-76). Otherwise the quantity is recorded as delivered but not transferred, the amount stays awaiting trigger, stage 09 raises `BILL_AND_HOLD_CRITERIA_UNMET` (`WARNING`, 04 table 15.4-D) once per obligation and event, and the gap is listed in `close_gate_facts` |
| S09-R-13 | **Repurchase agreements (POL-233).** The REVIEWED judgement `REPURCHASE_CLASSIFICATION` of the obligation selects the outcome. `SALE`: normal rules. `RIGHT_OF_RETURN`: §9.2.7. `LEASE`: stage 03 sets `scope_flag = LEASE_842` (S03-R-12); stage 09 produces no targets and no schedule (REQ-CON-016). `FINANCING`: no `DELIVERY_RECORDED` transfers control while the option is open; the consideration stays in the contract liability and accretes through EMOD-18 (JET-11b); lapse of the option is recorded as `DELIVERY_RECORDED` with trigger `CONTROL_TRANSFER` at the lapse date, when revenue equals the accreted allocation (606-10-55-71); exercise is `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` with `refund_amount` = the repurchase price (§10.2.4) |

#### 9.2.5 Progress: input measures

```python
def progress_cost_to_cost(ob, seg, d, ev, *, since_boundary: bool) -> tuple[Fraction, Fraction]:
    """Returns (f, um_revenue_exact). f is the margin-bearing progress; um_revenue_exact the zero-margin part."""
    costs = [c for c in cost_events(ob, purpose="PROGRESS_INPUT", at=d)]    # flags from measure events; ledger costs_cum counts every flag
    K_prog = sum(c.amount for c in costs if not c.is_wasted and not c.is_uninstalled_material)
    UM_inc = sum(c.amount for c in costs if c.is_uninstalled_material)
    eac = ev.pin(ob, kind="EAC", at=d)                        # APPROVED version, greatest effective_date <= d
    if eac is None:
        raise_or_cost_recovery(ob)                             # §9.2.5 S09-R-16
    EAC_total = eac.expected_total_amount
    UM_exp = eac.parameters.get("uninstalled_materials_cost", UM_inc)   # 04 T-CON-13 EAC parameter; absent: materials known to date
    denom = EAC_total - UM_exp
    if denom <= 0:
        if K_prog > 0: finding("NON_FINITE_AMOUNT", ob, reason="EAC_NET_OF_MATERIALS_NOT_POSITIVE")
        return Fraction(0), UM_inc
    if since_boundary:
        K_k = seg.base_progress.costs_margin_bearing
        f = min(Fraction(1), (K_prog - K_k) / (denom - K_k))
    else:
        f = min(Fraction(1), K_prog / denom)
    return f, UM_inc
# FIXED target with uninstalled materials (inception basis):
#   E = f × (X − UM_exp) + UM_inc;   f_total = E ÷ X;   C = cumulative_posted(X, A, f_total)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-14 | `COST_TO_COST`: progress = margin-bearing costs incurred ÷ (EAC − expected uninstalled materials); `progress_ratio` (T-CON-11) holds this margin-bearing progress (REC-S5-EX19 asserts 0.20), while the posted target uses φ = E ÷ X. Costs flagged `is_wasted` are excluded from the numerator and are never in the EAC (606-10-55-21(a); REQ-REC-007). Costs flagged `is_uninstalled_material` that meet 606-10-55-21(b) earn revenue equal to their cost and are excluded from progress and from the margin-bearing allocation (POL-091, FIX). The expected total materials cost is the `EAC` parameter `uninstalled_materials_cost` (04 T-CON-13, rev 1.2; OQ-B-05 resolved by D-76), and absent it the materials cost incurred to date. The flag on a manual event is the preparer's 55-21(b) attestation, approved as a manual event. The ERP posts the materials cost. The engine posts only the revenue equal to that cost, through JET-02 (Dr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` / Cr `REVENUE`), and no `COST_OF_REVENUE` line (D-76 ruling on uninstalled materials; POLICIES POL-091 and the JET-02 row "Uninstalled materials"; REQ-REC-007 as amended) |
| S09-R-15 | `LABOUR_HOURS`: f = hours to date ÷ expected total hours, where hours to date is the latest `PROGRESS_RECORDED.hours_to_date` with `measure = LABOUR_HOURS` at or before d and expected hours is the `EAC` pin's `expected_quantity`. A pin without `expected_quantity` gives `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` when hours are positive |
| S09-R-16 | `COST_RECOVERY` (606-10-25-37): while no APPROVED `EAC` pin exists at d, E = min(X, all progress costs incurred to d) and f = E ÷ X; from the effective date of the first `EAC` pin the obligation is measured as `COST_TO_COST`, and the difference posts as a `CATCH_UP` (§9.2.10). A `COST_TO_COST` or `LABOUR_HOURS` obligation with costs and no pin gives f = 0, finding `LOSS_EAC_MISSING` when the contract is in 605-35 scope (§11.4), and the costs wait |
| S09-R-17 | An `EAC` version below costs incurred to date is refused at submission with problem `eac-below-costs-incurred` (04 §15.2; PRD J-10-ALT-1). When costs recorded after the version exceed the EAC net of materials, stage 09 caps f at 1, so the obligation stays fully recognised until a new `EAC` version is approved; no finding is raised |

#### 9.2.6 Right to invoice, usage and prepaid drawdown

```python
def period_vc_realised(ob, d, ev) -> int:
    usage = ev.usage_reported(ob, usage_period_end_on_or_before=d, royalty=False)   # S04-R-06
    if ob.recognition_method == "RIGHT_TO_INVOICE" and not usage:
        return (sum(b.amount - b.tax_amount for b in ev.billing(ob, at=d))
                - sum(cm.amount for cm in ev.credit_memos(ob, at=d)))   # invoiced performance to date
    total = 0
    for u in usage:
        if u.rated_amount is None:
            finding("NON_FINITE_AMOUNT", ob, reason="USAGE_RATED_AMOUNT_MISSING", event=u.key)
            continue
        total += u.rated_amount                                          # minor units (T-SRC-06 rated_amount)
    return total
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-18 | `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` (606-10-55-18; POL-092 guard applied by stage 03): revenue equals the amount the entity has a right to invoice for performance to date. The source is `USAGE_REPORTED.rated_amount` (approved timesheets or rated units); an obligation with no usage events uses invoiced amounts net of credit memos and taxes. Performance earned but not invoiced is an unbilled receivable (ALG-03 table 2.4-A; CHK-014). **Stated price and progress (rev 1.20, D-98 candidate 28; ENC-6).** The line's stated price P is the expected total of the arrangement; the right to invoice R for performance to date (the source order above, as completed by D-87 L6-5-Q-16: rated usage, else net delivered quantity × the line's `unit_price`, else invoiced net) is the output measure of progress of the `FIXED` component, f = min(1, R ÷ P) on the inception basis, formula `rec.progress.right_to_invoice.v1` (§9.2.4); with P = 0 (an X = 0 component, the D-87 rate line) the component carries no revenue and its progress — the completion signal of S09-R-46 — is time elapsed over the service term (rev 1.22, ENC6-R3: REC-FS-08 is `PARTIALLY_SATISFIED` through February and `SATISFIED` on 31 March, its term end, never at inception); R above P is a `PERIOD_VC` realised amount (S09-R-01, S09-R-02) that stage 04 carries in the transaction price (ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-06), mirroring the S09-R-33 guarantee pattern, so that revenue equals R whenever the `FIXED` allocation equals P (REC-FS-08: P = 0, TP = allocated = R; POS-CHK-014: P = R = 3,000.00; DISC-S10 EX-42: P 12,000.00, R 3,000.00, RPO P − R = 9,000.00 on the S15-R-08 basis, stated under the 606-10-50-14A expedient where the disclosure applies). **POL-092 guard (rev 1.21, D-98 candidate 37; ENC-6).** Stage 09 applies the guard the earlier text placed at stage 03: under `BLOCK_IF_NONLINEAR_OR_FIXED_COMPONENT` the expedient is refused with `RTI_EXPEDIENT_NOT_APPLICABLE` (04 table 15.4-C; ERROR) when a `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` element of type `VOLUME_TIER`, `REBATE` or `PRICE_PROTECTION` (tier, rebate, declining rate) or `USAGE` (a minimum commitment) targets the obligation, or when its booking line's `total_price` differs from `quantity × unit_price` while both are present and the total is not 0 (an upfront fee or a non-constant rate; a D-87 rate line books total 0); POL-092 `ALLOW` bypasses the guard and the same finding is recorded as a `WARNING` carrying the policy reason (`policy = ALLOW`), so the bypass is visible in the output and the explain trace. Flagged for the G12 accountant package as the expedient-applicability predicate |
| S09-R-19 | `USAGE` with POL-240 `DERIVED`: usage fees are `PERIOD_VC` realised amounts, counted at the end of their usage period (`usage_period_end` ≤ d, for events preceding the evaluation position), the rule stage 04 applies to the price (S04-R-06). A stand-ready fee or minimum is the `FIXED` component |
| S09-R-20 | `USAGE` with POL-240 `ESTIMATE_MEASUREMENT_PERIOD_TP` (PT-01): the measurement-period transaction price is max(minimum commitment, expected usage fees), constrained, from the transaction price of stage 04 over the VC estimate pin. The obligation is a `FIXED` component measured `TIME_ELAPSED`; each new estimate version is a `TP_CHANGE` segment from stage 08; overage and shortfall invoices are billing only (CHK-110; PRD WLD-X-19) |
| S09-R-21 | **Prepaid drawdown.** Nonrefundable prepaid credits are an obligation with method `REDEMPTION_PATTERN`; `DELIVERY_RECORDED` (redemption) or `USAGE_REPORTED.quantity` (drawdown) counts redeemed units R. Revenue follows §9.2.8, so drawdown relieves the contract liability through JET-02 and expected breakage follows POL-055 |
| S09-R-22 | **Credit rollover (PT-02, POL-241).** Expected total redemptions include credits expected to be used after a rollover; the breakage rate excludes them. A renewal that carries unconsumed credits is a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` of kind `RENEWAL` on the source contract that extends the redemption obligation's end date (`PROSPECTIVE` segment with the same exact allocation), so the unredeemed consideration stays with the obligation it was paid for. The renewal's own consideration is booked as its own obligation or contract with `renewal_of_contract_id` lineage (REQ-MOD-017). If no renewal occurs, the remaining balance is recognised at lapse except the unclaimed-property share (§9.2.8; CHK-111) |

#### 9.2.7 Returns within the units measures (ALG-06)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ReturnState:                 # erev_engine/stages/state.py; built from AllocatedState.return_paths (stage 04)
    N: Fraction                    # cumulative units transferred
    Y: Fraction                    # cumulative units returned
    E: Fraction                    # expected further returns
    E_b: Fraction                  # part of E relating to units billed or paid (ALG-06 step 3)
    k: Fraction                    # carrying cost per unit (estimate parameters)
    c_rec: Fraction                # recovery cost per unit
    p_ref: Fraction                # refund price per unit
    window_end: date | None
    returned_before_E: Fraction    # min(units returned by the latest return event, E before it) for JET-07d

def return_state(ob, d, ev) -> ReturnState:      # computed once by s04_transaction_price/returns.py and published (OQ-B-02)
    v = ev.pin(ob, kind="RETURN_RATE", at=d)                      # expected_quantity = E at v.effective_date
    N, Y = ev.units_transferred(ob, at=d), ev.units_returned(ob, at=d)
    if ctx.policies.value("returns.model", obligation=ob) == "ACTUAL_RETURNS_ONLY" or v is None:
        E = Fraction(0)
    else:
        E = v.expected_quantity - ev.units_returned(ob, after=v.effective_date, at=d)   # ALG-06 step 1
        E = max(Fraction(0), E)
        w = v.parameters.window_end_date
        if w and (d > w or (d == w and not ev.is_positioned)):                         # S09-R-26: the right expires at the end of w, after that day's events
            E = Fraction(0)                                                            # window expiry
    E_b = min(E, units_billed_or_paid(ob, d) - Y)                                       # S09-R-23a; never negative
    return ReturnState(N, Y, E, max(Fraction(0), E_b), ...)

def units_billed_or_paid(ob, d) -> Fraction:     # D-91; stage 09's own AllocatedState, no stage 10 helper (D-88 L7-5-Q-3)
    kept = billing_identity.kept_lines(events, contract_key=ob.contract_key)        # kernel view kept_lines(events, *, contract_key=None, before=None) -> Iterator[E]: every non-update occurrence in stream order, the first-seen line of an identity (contract, invoice_number, line_external_id) and every repeat flagged cancellable alike (not one line per identity; D-91 C606-01 (2)); status-update records are excluded, mismatched or not, so a translation cannot recount an update (S10-R-07 at contract scope, before attribution)
    # the consumer keeps the eligibility evidence the view drops: first_update, the first same-amount, no-obligation-change update per identity read from the full classified stream through iter_billing_lines, and the applied PAYMENT_RECEIVED receipts per invoice, for the ENGINE-mode S10-R-06 unconditional date below; mismatch validation stays with stage 10 (S10-R-07)
    counted = []
    for ln in kept:
        mode = billing_mode_at(policies, ob.contracting_entity, ln.effective_date)  # stages/state; POL-123 else POL-004 (L2-4-Q-11), resolved per kept line at its effective date
        u = unconditional_date(ln, mode)                                             # S10-R-06: ERP the effective date; ENGINE the effective date of a noncancellable line, else the later of the line date and the earlier of an applied receipt and its first_update (L2-4-Q-11); a memo-only line has none and counts nothing
        if u is not None and u <= d:
            counted.append(ln)
    amount = Σ ln.amount for counted lines naming ob + ob's S10-R-01 share of each counted document's unreferenced lines
    return amount / path.p_ref                                # credit memos never enter; Y removes returned units
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-23 | For an obligation with a return right the allocation stays on the pre-return basis, because stage 04 excludes expected returns from the allocation basis (S04-R-02, S04-R-08), and the posted revenue target is ALG-06 step 2 exactly: C = round(r × (N − Y − E)), with r = X ÷ Q of the segment in force (ALG-06 §2.7.1). The obligation version reports `allocated_amount` = a_posted − round(r × (Y + E)) at the version date, so DB-17 holds with `revenue_cum + scheduled_amount + awaiting_trigger_amount`, and V1 holds because stage 04 measures the `expected_returns` memo on the same Y + E (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S04-R-08; 04 DB-17 rev 1.2; OQ-B-02 resolved by D-76). [J] Returns are the one measure whose target is not formed by CV-63: POLICIES ALG-06 publishes its own composition, and the reduction moves with every return, which is a measure event, not a boundary (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.3-A) |
| S09-R-23a | **Units billed or paid for E_b (D-91).** `units_billed_or_paid(ob, d)` counts the `BILLING_RECORDED` lines of the obligation's contract from the kernel kept-lines view (`billing_identity.kept_lines`: every non-update occurrence in stream order, the first-seen line of an identity (contract, `invoice_number`, `line_external_id`) and every repeat flagged cancellable alike, not one line per identity; status-update records are excluded, mismatched or not) tracked over the whole ENG-06 stream before the per-obligation filter (S10-R-07 at contract scope: a repeat with `is_cancellable` not true and the same amount adds nothing and keeps the first line's amount and attribution; a repeat flagged cancellable is a new line; a different amount is refused, CV-15), the amounts of lines naming the obligation plus its S10-R-01 share of each document's unreferenced lines (D-88 L7-5-Q-3), each line from its S10-R-06 unconditional date under the billing mode of its own date (L2-4-Q-11): `ERP` the effective date; `ENGINE` the effective date of a noncancellable line, else the later of the line date and the earlier of a payment applied to its invoice and its first same-amount noncancellable status update; a memo-only line contributes nothing. Credit memos never enter the base. Stage 09 computes it from its own AllocatedState and emits `return_units_billed` (`returns.units_billed.v1`: one `SourceRef` per counted direct line with its value, and per counted document with unreferenced lines its unreferenced total with params `keys`, `weights`, `key` so the S10-R-01 share is recomputed) and `return_refundable_units` (`returns.refundable_units.v1`), which `rl.return.v2` cites (§10.2.4). [J] 606-10-55-23 and 32-10 measure the refund liability on consideration received or receivable; within the engine a cancellable invoice records that the right is not yet unconditional (POL-123; 606-10-45-4, 55-286). Consideration received without an invoice and unbilled unconditional consideration (ALG-03) are outside this proxy (recorded limitations, D-91) |
| S09-R-24 | POL-053 `REDUCE_CONTRACT_QUANTITY` (default): returned units leave the contract; the undelivered quantity Q − N is unchanged. `RESTORE_REMAINING_QUANTITY` (parity): the transferred count N decreases by the returned units, which become deliverable again; E = 0 (POL-051 `ACTUAL_RETURNS_ONLY`) |
| S09-R-25 | Units returned in excess of the previous E reverse revenue at the POL-052 rate. `AVERAGE_CARRYING_RATE`: posted cumulative revenue ÷ units kept before the return. `CURRENT_REMAINING_RATE`: remaining exact allocation ÷ remaining quantity, falling back to X × f ÷ N when the remaining quantity is 0 (DEV-060). The reversal posts through ALG-01 §2.1.3 as a `RETURN` cause (CHK-061) |
| S09-R-26 | Window expiry sets E = 0 on a measurement dated on or after `window_end_date` (D-87 L6-5-Q-25; rev 1.7 editorial) and is time-driven (RCP-07): the close run posts its effect in mode `CLOSE_RELEASE` (RCP-08). The right expires at the end of `window_end_date`, and the expiry sorts after that day's events (rev 1.64; D-87 L6-5-Q-25): a measurement whose evaluation position lies among the events of `window_end_date` (`ev.is_positioned` in §9.2.7: the measure taken at an event of that day, up to its ENG-06 position) precedes the expiry and keeps E, so a return recorded on the last day is measured against the unexpired estimate (JET-07d); the measurement dated `window_end_date` that sees every event of the day has E = 0. A positioned measurement on that day evaluates `returns.expected_units.v1` with param `expiry_pending` = `true`; the formula gives 0 from `window_end` unless that param is set, so the trace re-evaluates both measurements |

#### 9.2.8 Breakage and the unclaimed-property carve-out (POL-055, POL-241)

```python
def redemption_target(ob, seg, d, ev, mu) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """Returns (C_total, C_redeemed, C_breakage) for a REDEMPTION_PATTERN obligation or LOYALTY_POINTS option."""
    X, A, Q = seg.x_exact, seg.a_posted, seg.totals.quantity         # Q = units or points issued
    R = ev.units_redeemed(ob, at=d)
    v = ev.pin(ob, kind="BREAKAGE", at=d)
    if ob.expired_on(d):                                             # end date passed, MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRED or termination
        u = escheat_share(v, Q)                                       # 1 − R_exp ÷ Q − b, floored at 0; 0 when v is None
        E_total = X * (1 - u)
    elif v is not None and ctx.policies.value("breakage.method", obligation=ob) == "PROPORTIONAL_TO_EXERCISE":
        R_exp, b = v.expected_quantity, v.rate
        if R_exp <= 0: raise EngineError("BREAKAGE_EXPECTED_ZERO", subject_key=ob.subject_key)
        rho = R_exp / Q + b                                           # expected entitlement ratio
        if rho > 1: raise EngineError("ESTIMATE_CONSTRAINT_RANGE", subject_key=ob.subject_key)
        if R > R_exp: finding("BREAKAGE_OVER_REDEMPTION", ob)
        E_total = min(X, max(X * R / Q, X * rho * min(Fraction(1), R / R_exp)))
    else:                                                             # WHEN_REMOTE
        b_r = v.rate if (v is not None and remote_attested(ob, v, d)) else Fraction(0)
        E_total = min(X, X * (R / Q + b_r))
    C_total = money.cumulative_posted(X, A, E_total / X, mu)
    C_red = min(money.round_half_up(X * R / Q, mu), C_total)
    return C_total, C_red, C_total - C_red
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-27 | Breakage revenue to date is expected breakage × cumulative redemptions ÷ expected total redemptions, cumulatively rounded (POL-055). The breakage estimate states R_exp (`expected_quantity`, including expected rollover use, POL-241) and b (`rate`, the constrained share of issued units the entity expects never to be exercised and to be entitled to). The entitlement ratio ρ = R_exp ÷ Q + b is at most 1 |
| S09-R-28 | **Unclaimed property (606-10-55-49).** The share u = 1 − ρ is expected to be remitted to a government and is never revenue. The preparer excludes it from b and records `constraint_checklist.unclaimed_property_excluded = true` (05 §3.6.5). At expiry the obligation recognises X × (1 − u) and stage 10 moves the unclaimed-property amount A − C_total from the contract liability to a refund liability component `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY` (JET-04b), consumed when the remittance credit memo is ingested (§10.2.4) |
| S09-R-29 | `WHEN_REMOTE`: nothing beyond redemptions is recognised until a `BREAKAGE` version whose `rate` is the remaining share and a REVIEWED judgement record topic `CONSTRAINT` attest remoteness; the remaining breakage then posts at that version's effective date (05 §3.6.5) |
| S09-R-30 | The breakage line (E-28 `BREAKAGE`) of a period is ΔC_breakage; the redemption line (`NORMAL`) is ΔC_redeemed. A breakage estimate change appears inside the `BREAKAGE` line (EX-09-D P3) |
| S09-R-31 | Material-right options with `option_type = LOYALTY_POINTS` use the same function with Q = points issued (CHK-053). Other options follow ALG-05: exercise under `CONTINUATION` closes the option obligation (its remaining allocation joins the optioned goods obligation, stage 08) and expiry recognises the remaining allocation (JET-08, cause `NORMAL`) |

#### 9.2.9 Royalties (POL-056, POL-057; 606-10-55-65 to 55-65B)

```python
def royalty_target(ob, d, ev) -> int:
    if not licence_satisfied(ob, d):                                  # later of sale or usage and satisfaction
        return 0                                                      # realised royalties wait as awaiting trigger
    CR = 0
    for period in ev.royalty_usage_periods(ob, at=d):                 # sorted by (start, end)
        stmt = ev.latest_statement(ob, period, at=d)                  # USAGE_REPORTED is_royalty_statement, rated_amount
        if stmt is not None:
            CR += stmt.rated_amount
        elif ctx.policies.value("royalty.unreported_sales", contract=ob.contract_key) == "ACCRUE_ESTIMATE":
            acc = ev.pin_royalty_accrual(ob, period, at=d)            # ROYALTY_ACCRUAL, parameters usage period = period
            CR += acc.expected_total_amount if acc else 0
    G = guarantee_total(ob) if ctx.policies.value("royalty.minimum_guarantee", contract=ob.contract_key) == "FIXED_ON_LICENCE_PATTERN" else 0
    return max(0, CR - G)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-32 | A royalty usage period is identified by (`usage_period_start`, `usage_period_end`). The latest statement admitted for a period is its measurement: it replaces every accrual whose usage period lies within it and any earlier statement for the same period (a corrected statement). The difference from the prior measurement of the period, the accrued estimates it replaces or the statement it corrects, posts on the statement's effective date as cause `ROYALTY` (JET-04a); a statement that replaces nothing posts no true-up (rev 1.13 erratum, lane ENG-C3) |
| S09-R-33 | The minimum guarantee is fixed consideration in the `FIXED` component, recognised on the licence pattern (POL-024: point in time for functional licences, `TIME_ELAPSED` for symbolic licences). Royalty revenue is recognised only for cumulative royalties above the whole guarantee (POL-057 `FIXED_ON_LICENCE_PATTERN`; PRD WLD-X-21) |
| S09-R-34 | **Coverage gap (close-gate fact).** For each royalty obligation with a satisfied licence: let L be the length in months of the latest usage period covered by a statement or accrual, and u_next = [latest covered end + 1 day, add_months(latest covered end, L)]. When end(u_next) ≤ the period end and no statement or accrual covers u_next, stage 09 emits a coverage-gap fact; the close step `INVARIANTS` raises `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL_MISSING` (ERROR, source `CLOSE`) unless POL-056 is `AS_REPORTED` with its approved attestation. Before the first covered period no fact is emitted [J: the cadence is not known until the licensee's first report] |

#### 9.2.10 Sequential cause decomposition of a period amount

```python
CAUSE_OF_EVENT = {                                     # E-03 literal -> E-28 line type
    "DELIVERY_RECORDED": "NORMAL", "PROGRESS_RECORDED": "NORMAL", "MILESTONE_ACHIEVED": "NORMAL",
    "COST_INCURRED": "NORMAL", "USAGE_REPORTED": "NORMAL", "HOLD_RELEASED": "NORMAL", "HOLD_APPLIED": "NORMAL",
    "MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_APPLIED": "NORMAL", "MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRED": "NORMAL", "BILLING_RECORDED": "NORMAL",
    "CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED": "NORMAL", "CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET": "NORMAL",
    "CONTRACT_AMENDED": "MODIFICATION", "CONTRACT_TERMINATED": "MODIFICATION", "REGROUPED": "MODIFICATION",
    "COMBINATION_CHANGED": "MODIFICATION", "MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED": "MODIFICATION",
    "RETURN_RECORDED": "RETURN", "OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED": "OPENING_BALANCE",
}
CAUSE_OF_ESTIMATE_KIND = {                            # ESTIMATE_CHANGED by E-09 kind
    "VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION": "TP_CHANGE", "IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION": "TP_CHANGE",
    "EXPECTED_PURCHASES": "TP_CHANGE", "EXERCISE_LIKELIHOOD": "TP_CHANGE", "RETURN_RATE": "RETURN",
    "EAC": "CATCH_UP", "BREAKAGE": "BREAKAGE", "ROYALTY_ACCRUAL": "ROYALTY", "RENEWAL_EXPECTATION": "CATCH_UP",
}

def decompose(ob, period, target_fn) -> dict[str, int]:
    """target_fn(d, upto) = posted component-summed target at date d including events up to ENG-06 position `upto`."""
    out: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    prev = target_fn(period.start - timedelta(days=1), upto=END)          # posted target at the previous period end
    for evt in events_affecting(ob, period):                               # ENG-06 order, effective within the period
        before = target_fn(evt.effective_date, upto=position_before(evt))
        after = target_fn(evt.effective_date, upto=position_of(evt))
        out["NORMAL"] += before - prev                                     # time and progress between points
        out[component_cause(ob, evt)] += after - before
        prev = after
    out["NORMAL"] += target_fn(period.end, upto=END) - prev
    return {k: v for k, v in out.items() if v != 0}
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-35 | `component_cause` returns `BREAKAGE` for any delta of the breakage part of a redemption-pattern obligation and `ROYALTY` for any delta of the `ROYALTY` component, whatever the event; otherwise `CAUSE_OF_EVENT` or `CAUSE_OF_ESTIMATE_KIND`. A `CONTRACT_AMENDED` of a material-right exercise under `CONTINUATION` has cause `MODIFICATION` on the closed option and `NORMAL` on the optioned goods. A same-date `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` version that S09-R-04's D-92 exception assigns to a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` produces no `CATCH_UP` or `TP_CHANGE` cause of its own on d; its effect is carried by the amendment's `MODIFICATION` cause (S06-R-17; D-92 (1)) |
| S09-R-36 | The decomposition sums exactly to the period amount (S09-INV-04; PRD J-16-AC-1). For a boundary event the delta is the stage 06 or stage 08 node `catch_up@<event key>` (CV-50; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.9-A), cited as input and never recomputed. The decomposition is the source of the catch-up disclosure measures of REQ-MOD-015: `catch_up_modification_cum` = Σ `MODIFICATION`, `catch_up_tp_change_cum` = Σ `TP_CHANGE`, `catch_up_estimate_cum` = Σ `CATCH_UP`, `catch_up_cum` = their sum, and `catch_up_amount` = the catch-ups of the events first included in this version. The exact value of a catch-up node is E_after(d) − E_before(d); its `rounding_residue` is exact − posted (DG-PAR-05 `cumulative_catchup`) |
| S09-R-37 | The sequential catch-up differs from the 50-12A prior-period portion of §15.2.4, which evaluates both bases at the start of the period (ALG-10 §2.11.3). CHK-110's column "of which catch-up" asserts the prior-period portion (−5,000.00); `catch_up_tp_change` for the same key is −10,000.00 when the estimate version is effective on 30 June (EX-09-E; OQ-B-06) |

#### 9.2.11 Manual release, deferral and schedule overrides (REQ-REC-023, REQ-JE-019)

| Id | Kind (E-93) | Posted target for a period t ending on or after the adjustment period P | Rationale [J] |
|---|---|---|---|
| S09-R-38 | `MANUAL_RELEASE` {`amount` \| `ratio` \| `remaining`} | min(A, max(C(t), C(P) + m)) with m = `amount`, `ratio` × (A − C(P)), or A − C(P). Mirrored for A < 0 | The released amount stays recognised; later normal progress absorbs it and the obligation still completes at A |
| S09-R-39 | `MANUAL_DEFER` {`amount` \| `ratio` \| `remaining`} | For t = P: max(C(P − 1), C(P) − m). For t > P: C(t) | A deferral moves an amount out of the current period only; it never defers past the next period |
| S09-R-40 | `SCHEDULE_OVERRIDE` {`periods`: [{`period_id`, `amount`}]} | For a listed period t_j: T_j = T_(j−1) + amount_j. For an unlisted period t after the latest listed t_k: C = `cumulative_posted`(X, A, (T_k + (X − T_k) × g) ÷ X) with g = (f(t) − f(t_k)) ÷ (1 − f(t_k)), and g = 1 when f(t_k) = 1 | The override re-spreads the remaining allocation over the remaining progress, so the schedule still completes at A |
| S09-R-41 | All kinds | Order of application: segment targets (§9.2.2 to §9.2.9) → manual adjustments in ENG-06 order → holds (§9.2.12) → V4 status guard. `MANUAL_JOURNAL` and `ACCOUNT_RECLASS` do not change stage 09 targets; their approved lines are stage 14 role targets (S14-R-09a; rev 1.63). An adjustment applies in the book its payload names (`book_code`, the T-SL-05 column; rev 1.63) and leaves every other book as it was; a payload without the member applies in every book | |

#### 9.2.12 Recognition holds (REQ-REC-022, REQ-POL-010)

```python
def held_target(ob, t_end: date, C_t: int, A: int, holds: Sequence[HoldInterval]) -> int:
    """holds: merged intervals [applied, released) of recognition holds on the obligation or its contract."""
    for h in holds:
        if h.applied <= t_end and (h.released is None or t_end < h.released):
            L = target_before_hold(ob, h)          # C at applied − 1 day, with events preceding HOLD_APPLIED in ENG-06 order
            return min(C_t, L) if A >= 0 else max(C_t, L)
    return C_t
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-42 | Only `hold_type = recognition` affects stage 09; `journal_export` holds affect export (§14.3.6). A contract-level hold (no `obligation_key`) applies to every obligation of the contract. Overlapping intervals are merged before evaluation |
| S09-R-43 | A hold freezes movement towards completion and never defers a reversal: for A ≥ 0 the target is min(C(t), L); for A < 0, max(C(t), L) [J: a price reduction is recognised when it occurs, 606-10-32-42] |
| S09-R-44 | While a hold is open, schedule lines of the held obligation for periods ending on or after the hold date carry the frozen targets, and the held amount is awaiting trigger (its release). The `HOLD_RELEASED` event recomputes the group; the period containing the release date receives the catch-up as a `NORMAL` line released at close |

#### 9.2.13 Obligation measures, scheduled and awaiting trigger

```python
def obligation_measures(ob, v: date, horizon_end: date) -> ObligationMeasures:
    A = sum(component_allocation(ob, c, v) for c in components(ob))         # current segments; realised PERIOD_VC/ROYALTY
    rev = posted_target(ob, v)                                               # §9.2.2 to §9.2.12 at the version date
    det = [c for c in components(ob) if is_deterministic(ob, c)]             # the ONE predicate of S09-R-45 (rev 1.126)
    scheduled = sum(projected_final_target(ob, c) - posted_target_component(ob, c, v) for c in det if not held(ob, v))
    awaiting = A - rev - scheduled
    return ObligationMeasures(allocated_amount=A, revenue_cum=rev, scheduled_amount=scheduled,
                              awaiting_trigger_amount=awaiting, remaining_allocation=A - rev, ...)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-45 | Scheduled = amounts a deterministic pattern places in future periods without further events; awaiting trigger = allocated − recognised − scheduled (REQ-REC-021; GLOSSARY). `MILESTONE`, units, input measures, usage fees and royalties (the `PERIOD_VC` and `ROYALTY` components), redemptions and held amounts are awaiting trigger. **The deterministic component: ONE predicate (rev 1.126; item ENG-USAGE-FIXED-SCHEDULE-1; supervisor ruling R-116 (a); candidate AD-61 for the independent accountant).** An obligation is deterministic when its `FIXED` component is measured by time elapsed and carries an allocation: (i) a `TIME_ELAPSED` obligation, by its method alone, as before — a custodial obligation among them (S09-R-09); (ii) a `USAGE` obligation one of whose `FIXED` segments holds an exact or a posted allocation other than 0 — its stand-ready fee or minimum (S09-R-19; the PT-01 minimum of S09-R-20); (iii) a `ROYALTY` obligation whose licence is not point in time, on the same condition — the guarantee of a right to access (S09-R-33). They are the methods whose `FIXED` component progresses by time elapsed (§9.2.3). A `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` obligation never is: its `FIXED` component is measured by the right to invoice (S09-R-18). Nor is, under (ii) or (iii), an obligation measured by its redemption pattern (§9.2.8). The predicate is stated here once and read at three places: the split of this rule with the flag of S09-R-49, the projection of S09-R-50 and the current part of S10-R-24; the release of a loss provision asks another question and keeps the method (S11-R-17). The pattern of an obligation at d_v is `DETERMINISTIC` on the further conditions that held before: no guard stands before its recognition but a dated start, and a custodial term has begun (S09-R-09); a held target awaits its release (S09-R-44). Until rev 1.126 the predicate was the method `TIME_ELAPSED` alone, so the fixed fee of a usage obligation, which no event moves, was awaiting trigger, its schedule ended with the evaluation horizon, the RPO time bands placed what lay after the open periods whole at the obligation's end date (S15-R-10) and its current part read 0. Measured on a fee of 300,000.00 for three years with the first nine months of the term open: bands of 75,000.00 / 0.00 / 225,000.00 at signing where 100,000.00 falls into each, and a current part of 0.00 where 100,000.00 is earned within twelve months (key `REC-USAGE-STAND-READY-FEE-SCHEDULED-TO-TERM-END`, a candidate for the accountant). No posting moves: the fee was and is recognised by time through the horizon (S09-R-19), and usage that is not yet reported is in no figure |
| S09-R-46 | `satisfaction_status` (E-22): `CANCELLED` when terminated with remaining allocation removed; `SATISFIED` when f = 1 on the final segment; `UNSATISFIED` when revenue_cum = 0 and f = 0; otherwise `PARTIALLY_SATISFIED`. `satisfied_date` is the first date f reaches 1. **Zero components (rev 1.22, ENC6-R3).** A `FIXED` segment with X = 0 (a `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` line with stated price 0, D-87) completes on the obligation's own measure — time elapsed over its service term (§9.2.4) — never on the arithmetic convention f = 1 of an empty denominator; an obligation whose fees are realised on `PERIOD_VC` stays `PARTIALLY_SATISFIED` while its term runs and becomes `SATISFIED` at the term end |
| S09-R-47 | Legacy measures for the history export (T-CON-11, D-33): `ssp_delivered` and `ssp_delivered_cum` = quantity delivered × unit SSP of the active segment; every period measure is 0 on a non-delivery event and every `previous_*` measure rolls (DEV-051) |

#### 9.2.14 Schedules and versioning (REQ-REC-020)

```python
def build_revenue_schedule(ob, targets, causes, horizon_period, mu) -> list[ScheduleLineOut]:
    lines = []
    last = schedule_last_period(ob, horizon_period)       # term end period for deterministic components, else horizon
    cum_by_type: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    for p in periods_from(group_inception_period, last):
        for line_type, amount in sorted(causes[p.key].items()):
            if amount == 0:
                continue
            cum_by_type[line_type] += amount
            lines.append(ScheduleLineOut(
                schedule_kind="REVENUE", subject_type="obligation", subject_key=ob.subject_key,
                entity=ob.performing_entity, period_key=p.key, line_type=line_type, amount=amount,
                cumulative_amount=cum_by_type[line_type], cumulative_exact=exact_by_type(ob, p, line_type),
                quantity=units_in_period(ob, p) if line_type == "NORMAL" else None,
                is_released_at_close=(line_type == "NORMAL" and is_deterministic_period_amount(ob, p)),
                trace_node_id=f"revenue_by_cause:{ob.subject_key}/{line_type}:{p.key}"))
    return lines
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S09-R-48 | A schedule belongs to one contract version (T-ENG-01) and is superseded by the next version, never updated. Lines are identified by (subject key, schedule kind, line type, period key). The engine emits the complete set for the version; zero amounts are not emitted |
| S09-R-49 | `is_released_at_close = true` only for `NORMAL` lines of deterministic components (the predicate of S09-R-45, rev 1.126: a `TIME_ELAPSED` obligation, a custodial term among them; the fixed fee or minimum of a `USAGE` obligation, PT-01 included; the guarantee of a right-to-access `ROYALTY`); every other line is event-driven and posts at compute (RCP-07, RCP-08; §14.2.3). Rev 1.82 (supervisor rulings R-79 (a) and R-99 (a); lane decision L3-2-Q-15; candidate AD-50 for the independent accountant): the flag describes the schedule line and defers nothing. As built the revenue of every line, the flagged ones included, posts at compute as `EVENT` deltas in every postable period — stage 13 binds no obligation as released at close — so a month's scheduled revenue is in the ledger as soon as the contract that earns it is computed; the close run's `CLOSE_RELEASE` pass posts the `TIME` amounts of S14-R-05. As built, and stated at rev 1.126: the line of a period in which a realised `PERIOD_VC` amount of the obligation moved — the month a usage fee is reported — is not flagged; it holds the fee beside the month's fixed amount |
| S09-R-50 | Deterministic components are projected to the period containing their term end with no further events; event-driven components have lines only through the evaluation horizon (rev 1.126: the components of S09-R-45 — the fixed fee of a usage obligation has a line in every period of its term, and a period after the horizon holds the fee alone) |

### 9.3 Invariants

| Id | Invariant | Check |
|---|---|---|
| S09-INV-01 | For every component and date: 0 ≤ C ≤ A when A ≥ 0; A ≤ C ≤ 0 when A < 0 | PROP:P4 |
| S09-INV-02 | C = A when f = 1 on the final segment of a component | PROP:P4 |
| S09-INV-03 | `allocated_amount = revenue_cum + scheduled_amount + awaiting_trigger_amount`; for A ≥ 0 each term is ≥ 0 | DB-17; DG-ENG-05 |
| S09-INV-04 | Σ over line types of `revenue_by_cause(t)` = target(t) − target(t − 1); Σ over periods of schedule lines = the target at the last emitted period | unit test per cause |
| S09-INV-05 | No revenue target while `status_in_book = NOT_A_CONTRACT` at the period end (V4) and none for `scope_flag ≠ IN_SCOPE_606` (REQ-CON-016) | V4 |
| S09-INV-06 | Target 0 for dates before `recognition_start_date` (V5) | V5 |
| S09-INV-07 | One recognition method per obligation version (REQ-REC-018) | stage entry assertion |
| S09-INV-08 | While a recognition hold is open the posted target never exceeds the freeze level (A ≥ 0) | holds unit test |
| S09-INV-09 | A `PROSPECTIVE` boundary changes no posted target: C_after(d) = C_before(d) = R_k | EX-09-A |
| S09-INV-10 | 0 ≤ f ≤ 1; `TIME_ELAPSED` progress is non-decreasing within a segment | ALG-11 |
| S09-INV-11 | C_redeemed ≤ C_total and cumulative breakage ≥ 0 | breakage unit test |
| S09-INV-12 | Replaying with shuffled arrival of commuting events gives identical targets | PROP:P8, P10 |

### 9.4 Findings and codes

| Code (04 §15.4) | Severity | Condition in stage 09 |
|---|---|---|
| `PROGRESS_OUTSIDE_POB_TERM` | WARNING | Progress event dated outside [`start_date`, `end_date`] |
| `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` | ERROR | S09-R-14 denominator not positive with margin-bearing costs; S09-R-15 hours without expected hours; S09-R-18 usage without `rated_amount` |
| `RTI_EXPEDIENT_NOT_APPLICABLE` | ERROR; WARNING under POL-092 `ALLOW` | S09-R-18 POL-092 guard (rev 1.21): a tier, rebate, declining-rate or minimum-commitment element targets the `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` obligation, or `total_price` ≠ `quantity × unit_price` (both present, total ≠ 0) |
| `LOSS_EAC_MISSING` | WARNING | S09-R-16 for a 605-35-scoped contract (raised by stage 11, §11.4) |
| `BREAKAGE_EXPECTED_ZERO` | ERROR | `expected_quantity` ≤ 0 on a `PROPORTIONAL_TO_EXERCISE` version |
| `BREAKAGE_OVER_REDEMPTION` | WARNING | R > R_exp |
| `ESTIMATE_CONSTRAINT_RANGE` | ERROR | Breakage entitlement ratio ρ > 1 (S09-R-27) |
| `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL_MISSING` | ERROR (source `CLOSE`) | Raised by the close step from a coverage-gap fact (S09-R-34) |
| `BILL_AND_HOLD_CRITERIA_UNMET` | WARNING (04 table 15.4-D, rev 1.2) | S09-R-12 |
| `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` | ERROR | Any S09-INV violation (DG-ENG-05) |

Submission-time refusals that protect stage 09 inputs: problem `eac-below-costs-incurred` (S09-R-17); `ESTIMATE_METHOD_LOCKED` (POL-040). Raised earlier and never re-raised by stage 09: `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY`, `RETURN_EXCEEDS_DELIVERED` and `ESTIMATE_VERSION_NOT_APPROVED` (stage 01, S01-R-16 to S01-R-18); `MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED` (stage 06).

### 9.7 Worked examples

Every figure below is recomputed by `verify_examples.py`.

**EX-09-A. Time elapsed with a 25-13(a) boundary (the shape of PRD WLD-X-05 and WLD-X-06, K-02; the example's own SSP entries).** O1: allocation 240,000.00, `DAILY` over 1 Jan 2026 to 31 Dec 2027 (730 days). Modification effective 16 Sep 2026 adds seats O2 for 60,000.00 over the remaining 472 days; mod-date SSP weights 155,000 : 77,500. Note (rev 1.6, D-90b, editorial): O1 and O2 are series (increment day; PRD K-02, `TPL-SUB-DAILY`), and the mod-date SSP entries (SSP v2) price the remaining increments, so the weights follow the `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S06-R-11 series row and its time-elapsed remaining-service factor does not apply. No figure changes.

The example has the shape of the seeded PRD K-02 world, not its weights. The seeded world prices the added seats at the nearest bound of their SSP range, because their stated price lies below it (POL-072), and counts O1's remaining increments on the `DAILY` convention (the `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S06-R-11 series row; D-97 (3)).
