## 10. Stage 10: billing and balances

Stage 10 attributes billing to obligations, measures the D-12 net position per (combination group, contracting entity, book), splits a debit position into unbilled receivable and contract asset (ALG-02, ALG-03), measures refund liabilities, return assets and receivable contras, and classifies balances as current or noncurrent. It produces targets; stage 14 derives JET-03, JET-04b, JET-04c, JET-06, JET-07c and JET-07d lines. JET-06, the netting reclass of ALG-02 step 5, is a close-pass posting of the `NETTING_RECLASS` pass (D-94 (3)): a COMMAND compute alone — a January-only world without a close pass — posts no JET-06 line, while the T-CON-09 balances of the same position already present `unbilled_receivable_txn` and `contract_liability_txn` by the balance rules (D-97 (31); rev 1.11).

### 10.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Produced by | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `BILLING_RECORDED`, `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED`, `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` | stage 01 (`measure_events`, `ledger`) | Billing attribution, unconditional dates, AR (ENGINE mode) |
| `revenue_targets`, `revenue_by_cause`, `return_states`, `escheat_components` | stage 09 | Revenue relief, return measurements, unclaimed property |
| Concession amounts settled by credit (POL-104 via JET-05c; PT-04) and termination refunds (`CONTRACT_TERMINATED.refund_amount`; POL-242) | stage 06 | Refund liability components |
| VC pins with a refund-liability target; `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION` pins | stage 01 pin index | Refund liability components; receivable contra (ENGINE mode) |
| `specialist_targets`: deposit transfer at criteria met (EMOD-12), interest accretion (EMOD-18), noncash unconditional amounts (EMOD-23), consideration-payable memo, ordinary release and asset (EMOD-22), 25-7 deposit revenue (EMOD-12) | stages 01 to 08 | Other control-role flows (§10.2.3) |
| `revenue_targets` of the related obligations; `AllocatedState.concession_history` (the dated concession portions per producing event and receiving obligation, validated by `check_history` against the `CONCESSION` refund quotas) and the stage 10 `concession_created_cum` targets (cited as `aggregate_node` lineage only; rev 1.8); `SHARE_BASED_CONSIDERATION` pins; share-based promises | stages 09, 06 and 08, 10, 01 | Share-based reduction R(t) (S10-R-26) |
| Resolved POL-004, POL-045, POL-120 to POL-128, POL-242 | `BookContext.policies` | Modes and options |
| Contract header termination terms (T-CON-01) | bundle | ALG-03 derivation |

| Output (`BalanceState`) | Content | Consumers |
|---|---|---|
| `billing` | Per obligation and period: `billed_cum`, `billed_amount`, unconditional billed amount; per invoice line: attribution and unconditional date | stage 12, 14, 15; T-CON-11 |
| `positions` | Per (group, contracting entity) and period end: net position NP and its components | stages 12, 14, 15 |
| `presentation` | Per (group, contracting entity) and period end: presented CL, CA, UR; per obligation: `netting_reclass_amount`, `netting_reclass_role`; per member contract: T-CON-09 labelled balances | stage 14 (JET-06), 15, persistence |
| `refund_liabilities` | Open components per (group, contracting entity, component, obligation or contract) and period end | stage 14 (JET-04b), 15 |
| `return_assets` | RA balance and cumulative JET-07d derecognition per returnable obligation and period end | stage 14 (JET-07c, 07d) |
| `receivable_contra`, `accounts_receivable` | ENGINE mode only, per member contract and period end | stages 12, 14; V8 fact |
| `current_split` | Current parts of CL, CA, UR per (group, contracting entity) and period end (POL-124) | persistence, reports |
| `obligation_measures` | `position_obligation`, `position_contract_entity`, `remaining_billing` (T-CON-11) | persistence, parity runner |
| `close_gate_facts` | Per-contract AR tie-out values (REQ-BIL-011, V8) in ENGINE mode | platform reconciliation |
| `customer_consideration` | Per `<contract>@<entity>` and period end: the stage 04 `consideration_payable`, `incentive_release_ordinary_cum`, `customer_incentive_asset` rows and the stage 10 `share_based_reduction_element_cum` (per element), `share_based_reduction_cum` and `incentive_release_cum` = ordinary + share-based (posted sums; S10-R-26; D-91) | stages 12 (Rel = ordinary), 13/14 (JET-14 parts), engine root (`contract_version.revenue_cum`) |

### 10.2 Pseudocode

#### 10.2.1 Billing ingestion and attribution

```python
def attribute_document(doc: BillingDocument, contract: ContractView, obs: Sequence[ObligationState], at: date,
                       invoice_attr: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    """One invoice or one credit memo (all its lines in the event); payload amounts exclude tax_amount (§16.3).
    Returns magnitudes as recorded: S10-R-03 adds invoice attributions and subtracts credit-memo attributions."""
    out: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    for line in doc.lines:
        if line.obligation_key is not None:                       # referenced line: attributed directly
            out[subject_of(contract, line.obligation_key)] += line.amount
    T = sum(line.amount for line in doc.lines if line.obligation_key is None)   # unreferenced total of the document
    if T == 0:
        return dict(out)
    if doc.kind == "CREDIT_MEMO" and doc.credited_invoice_number in invoice_attr:
        base = invoice_attr[doc.credited_invoice_number]         # attribution of the credited invoice
        keys = sorted(k for k in base if base[k] != 0)
        weights = [abs(base[k]) for k in keys]
    else:
        cands = [o for o in obs if o.contract_key == contract.key
                 and o.contracting_entity == contract.contracting_entity and o.obligation_kind != "VC_LINE"]
        keys = sorted(o.subject_key for o in cands)
        weights = [allocation_posted(k, at) for k in keys]       # A_p at the document effective date
        if sum(weights) == 0:
            weights = [resolved_ssp(k) for k in keys]            # positive by POL-077
    sign = 1 if T > 0 else -1
    for k, share in zip(keys, money.largest_remainder(abs(T), weights, keys)):   # apportion |T| once per document
        out[k] += sign * share                                   # sign applied after apportioning (ALG-02 step 3)
    return dict(out)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-01 | Billing is attributed to obligations per document (`attribute_document`). A line with `obligation_key` is attributed to that obligation. The unreferenced lines of one invoice or credit memo are summed to T, and \|T\| is apportioned once with `largest_remainder`, with the sign of T applied after apportioning (CHK-003c). The weights are the attribution of the credited invoice for a credit memo that names `credited_invoice_number`; otherwise the posted allocations of the contract's non-VC obligations with the header's contracting entity at the document date, and their resolved SSPs when those sum to 0 (POLICIES ALG-02 step 3 as amended in rev 1.2; ALG-01 §2.1.2; `_coverage/answer-keys-fx-entity-books:OQ-AK-03`, resolved by D-76). The entity of an attributed amount is the obligation's contracting entity |
| S10-R-02 | Taxes (`tax_amount`) never enter billing attribution or the position (POL-045 `EXCLUDE_ALL_IN_SCOPE`); in ENGINE mode stage 14 posts them to `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE` (JET-03). Under POL-045 `ASSESS_EACH_TAX` a tax line assessed as principal is part of the line amount supplied by the adapter |
| S10-R-03 | `billed_cum` (T-CON-11) at date d = attributed invoice amounts with unconditional date (S10-R-06) ≤ d − attributed credit-memo amounts with ENG-06 position ≤ d; in `ERP` mode and for noncancellable lines the unconditional date is the effective date; billing while `NOT_A_CONTRACT` still counts (S10-R-04) (D-89 L7-6-Q-9). **Opening baseline (rev 1.12; ENB-9; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S07-R-05).** For an obligation whose stage 07 `OpeningBaseline` has cutover date D, `billed_cum` at d ≥ D counts the baseline `billed_cum` (the payload value; under S07-R-08 IFRS15 the obligation's fair-value share) in place of the ledger's invoice and credit-memo lines dated on or before D, cited through the baseline node; lines after D add as above, and `remaining_billing` measures the billing since the boundary net of the baseline (the ERP already holds the cutover balances, T-MIG-01). Under POL-210 `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` an `ENGINE`-mode line dated on or before D is recomputed, not replaced (the engine posts JET-03 for it, S07-R-07: the difference between those lines and the imported `billed_cum` posts once at D), while `ERP`-mode lines are replaced in every mode (rev 1.16). When the ledger holds such `ENGINE`-mode lines for the contract, the baseline `billed_cum` is not counted at all — the recomputed lines stand for it in `billed_cum`, in B_u (S10-R-08) and in the S12-R-03 opening flow; without them the imported billing is the ERP's, the baseline is counted as in every mode and JET-03 has no difference (rev 1.16). `REFUND_EXCEEDS_BILLED` is raised by stage 01 over the whole stream (S01-R-16), never by stage 10. Stage 10 emits the version-state node `billed_cum` for every obligation; for an obligation that received a share of unreferenced billing, that share is also emitted as node `billed_attributed_cum`, an input of `billed_cum` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S01-R-19; OQ-B-17 resolved by D-76) |
| S10-R-04 | While the contract is `NOT_A_CONTRACT` in the book, billing attributed to it counts for `billed_cum` only; it enters no position (stage 02 raises `INVOICE_ON_NOT_A_CONTRACT`, WARNING, S02-R-11; JET-01b) |
| S10-R-05 | `remaining_billing` = the billing plan of the segment in force (`AllocationSegment.remaining_billing_plan`, stated-price basis) − `billed_cum`, with `billed_cum` as amended in S10-R-03 (in `ENGINE` mode counted from the S10-R-06 unconditional date; D-89 L7-6-Q-9); its exact value is the exact billing plan − `billed_cum` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` Table 0.9-A; DG-PAR-05 `tp_billing_basis`) |

#### 10.2.2 Unconditional billing (POL-004, POL-123)

```python
def unconditional_date(line, ev, mode: str) -> date | None:
    if mode == "ERP":                                            # ERP_POSTED_INVOICES: mirror what the ERP posted
        return line.effective_date
    if not line.is_cancellable:                                  # UNCONDITIONAL_INVOICES_ONLY
        return line.effective_date
    dates = [p.effective_date for p in ev.payments(applied_to=line.invoice_number)]
    if line.is_first_seen:                                       # D-92 (4): a status update dates the identity's FIRST-SEEN line only
        dates += [u.effective_date for u in ev.billing_status_updates(line.invoice_number, line.line_external_id,
                                                                       is_cancellable=False, amount=line.amount)]   # same-amount updates only (S10-R-07)
                                                                 # a cancellable repeat of the identity stays memo-only until a receipt applied to its invoice
    return max(line.effective_date, min(dates)) if dates else None   # memo-only while None; never before the line (L2-4-Q-11; D-91)
```

D-92 (4) (policy edge A; rev 1.9): `is_first_seen` is the kernel's `BillingLine.line` (the first-seen event of the S10-R-07 identity). A same-amount cancellable repeat of the identity is NOT dated by the first line's status update (the pre-1.9 pseudocode, per identity and same amount, would have dated both lines of a same-amount repeat: 600 + 600 → 1,200 where the rule gives 600); it becomes billing only through a receipt applied to its invoice or its own update. Stage 10 (bf79c60) and stage 09 (61309a6) implement it (L9-ENG-B4).

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-06 | In `ERP` mode every ingested invoice counts on its effective date, so the subledger contract liability ties to the ERP's contract liability (research 07 RC-02). In `ENGINE` mode a cancellable invoice is memo-only until the earlier of a payment applied to it and a status update making it noncancellable (606-10-55-286; CHK-024). **The mode of a period (rev 1.162; item PINP-PERIOD-VALUE-1).** The mode of a line is read at the period of its effective date in the contracting entity's calendar: the POL-123 basis when the bundle holds a POL-123 value for THAT period, else the mode derived from POL-004 at that period (`state.billing_mode_at`; `PolicyResolver.has(code, entity, period)`). POL-123 has no framework default and the orchestrator resolves each period at its own instant (05 RCP-15), so a period before the first POL-123 version has no POL-123 value and reads POL-004, whatever later periods hold. Before this revision POL-123 was read for every period once the bundle held any POL-123 value, and a bundle that held it for some periods only was refused (CV-17) |
| S10-R-07 | A status update is a `BILLING_RECORDED` with the same `invoice_number` and `line_external_id` as an earlier line of the same contract (identity at contract scope; the payload `obligation_key` is not part of it), `is_cancellable = false` (or absent, the 04 default; L2-4-Q-8) and the same `amount`. It adds no billing anywhere in the engine; its effective date is the noncancellable date; it never changes the first line's attribution. A status update whose `tax_amount` differs from the first line's (absent reads 0.00) raises `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_TAX_MISMATCH` (`WARNING`, stage 10; D-97 (27), rev 1.14): it keeps its status-update role and dates its line when `amount` and `obligation_key` match, and its tax is dropped — a status update adds no tax anywhere, so there is no value effect. A different amount, or a non-null `obligation_key` different from the first line's non-null `obligation_key`, raises `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` (`ERROR`; an amount mismatch raises it with the finding detail unchanged, `invoice_number`, `line_external_id` and `rule`; only an `obligation_key` mismatch, both keys non-null and different, adds the detail member `obligation_key`; 04 table 15.4-B, rev 1.2 and 1.11; OQ-B-08 resolved by D-76; D-91; L9-ENG-B1 Q-2); a null `obligation_key` on either side means no change. A repeated identity whose `is_cancellable` is `true` is a new line, not a status update; the API refuses the duplicate with 422 `validation-failed` (`rule_id` `INVOICE_IDENTITY_REPEATED`) and the CSV v2 import raises `INVOICE_IDENTITY_REPEATED` at validation, so a re-issued cancellable invoice carries a new identity and no stored stream of a supported channel holds such a repeat (D-97 (30); lane F-CTR, 14f7adc0; 04 table 15.4-B). The same identity governs every consumer of billing lines: `billed_cum` (S10-R-03), ALG-06 units billed (S09-R-23a; D-88 L7-5-Q-3), the S04-R-10a payment points, S04-R-17 incentive invoices and S04-R-20 taxes (aligned by lane ENG-B4, 790a93e — C606-05h), all through the kernel helper `erev_engine/billing_identity.py` (D-91; D-97 (32)). API event commands refuse a mismatch with 422 `validation-failed` and this `rule_id`, and the CSV v2 import raises the finding at validation (D-91; landed by lane F-CTR, 14f7adc0); a stream that still holds such an event is quarantined (CV-15) |
| S10-R-08 | B_u(c, e, t) = Σ attributed amounts of lines with `unconditional_date ≤ period end t` − credit memos with position ≤ t. In `ERP` mode B_u = Σ `billed_cum` of the (c, e) obligations. **Opening baseline (rev 1.12; S10-R-03; S07-R-05).** When every obligation of (c, e) carries a stage 07 opening baseline with one cutover date D, B_u at t ≥ D counts Σ baseline `billed_cum` in place of the `ERP`-mode lines dated on or before D (under `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` with `ENGINE`-mode lines dated on or before D those lines stay and the baseline is not counted, rev 1.16, S10-R-03), so the opening net position is the ERP's cutover position (CHK-121: 240,000.00 − 120,000.00 = 120,000.00 in ASC606; 90,000.00 in IFRS15) |

#### 10.2.3 Net position (D-12; ALG-02)

```python
def net_position(c, e, t) -> PositionComponents:
    obs_e = [o for o in group_obligations(c) if o.contracting_entity == e and in_book_active(o, t)]
    B_u   = billed_unconditional(c, e, t)                        # S10-R-08
    R_ctl = sum(revenue_relief(o, t) for o in obs_e)             # S10-R-09
    DT    = specialist.deposit_transferred(c, e, t)             # JET-01b criteria met
    NC    = specialist.noncash_unconditional(c, e, t)           # JET-17
    IE    = specialist.interest_expense_accreted(c, e, t)       # JET-11b
    II    = specialist.interest_income_accreted(c, e, t)        # JET-11a
    RC    = receivable_contra(c, e, t)                          # JET-04c, ENGINE mode only
    RL    = refund_liability_open(c, e, t)                      # all components, §10.2.4
    NP = B_u + DT + NC + IE - II - R_ctl - RC - RL
    return PositionComponents(NP=NP, B_u=B_u, R_ctl=R_ctl, DT=DT, NC=NC, IE=IE, II=II, RC=RC, RL=RL)

def revenue_relief(o, t) -> int:
    """Cumulative control-role debits for revenue of obligation o (JET-02, 04a, 05a, 05b, 08, 13 contracting side)."""
    return (revenue_targets[o, t]                                # stage 09 posted target: gross of the agent split and of consideration
                                                                 # payable (S04-R-02; JET-14 releases separately); 0 while NOT_A_CONTRACT
            + concession_total(o, t))                            # JET-05c reduced revenue without touching CL
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-09 | NP is the balance of the `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` control role for (c, e, b) excluding JET-06 lines (ALG-02 definition), computed from targets so that stage 14's lines equal it by construction (S14-INV-04). With no refund, contra, deposit, financing or noncash flows, NP = cumulative billed or unconditionally due − cumulative revenue (D-12) |
| S10-R-10 | NP includes financing accretion: interest accreted under JET-11 posts to the control role, so it is part of the carrying amount and therefore of the net position (D-76 ruling on financing interest; POLICIES ALG-02 definition; CHK-137; REQ-BIL-007 and 05 EMOD-18 as amended; OQ-B-09 resolved by D-76). The financed balance stays a separate measure (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S04-R-13). For the presentation split the net accretion is attributed to obligations (S10-R-20) |
| S10-R-11 | The unit is (combination group, contracting entity, book) (POL-120 `CONTRACT_ENTITY_BOOK`, FORCED). Performing entities hold no contract balances (ALG-07 step 3). Contracts in different groups never net (CHK-012) |
| S10-R-12 | `position_obligation` (T-CON-11) keeps its legacy definition `billed_cum − revenue_cum`, with exact value `billed_cum − (revenue_cum + rounding_residue)` for the parity runner; `position_contract_entity` sums it over the contract's obligations with the same contracting entity (REQ-ENT-002) |

#### 10.2.4 Refund liabilities (POL-127, POL-242; ALG-06 step 3; JET-04b, JET-05c)

| Component | Target balance at period end t | Created by | Released by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RETURN` | round(p_ref × E_b) per returnable obligation (§9.2.7; `rl.return.v2`, input 0 the stage 09 node `return_refundable_units:<ob>:<period>`, params `p_ref`, `as_of`, `minor_unit`; `rl.return.v1` remains registered for stored traces; D-91) | JET-04b | Remeasurement only |
| `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` | `refund_liability_target` of the latest APPROVED pin of a VC element whose `vc_element_type` ∈ {`REBATE`, `PRICE_PROTECTION`, `REFUND`, `SLA_CREDIT`, `DISCOUNT`} (ALG-10 §2.11.1 values; 04 T-CON-13 `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` parameter, rev 1.2; OQ-B-07 resolved by D-76); 0 without it, except the derived form of D-87 L5-3-Q-7 for a `DECREASE` element of `REBATE`, `PRICE_PROTECTION`, `REFUND` or `VOLUME_TIER` (direction rule below; D-97 (23), rev 1.11) | JET-04b | Remeasurement only (the preparer's version is net of credit memos issued by its effective date) |
| `TERMINATION` | `refund_amount` of `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` − consumption | JET-04b (POL-242) | Consumption by credit memos |
| Direction rule (D-97 (23); rev 1.11) | An explicit `refund_liability_target` on one of the five types of the `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` row is measured for either `direction` of the element (`INCREASE` or `DECREASE`; B3-D16 magnitudes × direction, ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-04). The derived form of D-87 L5-3-Q-7 = L6-5-Q-11 — RL(t) = max(0, min(K_t, B_t − R_t − O_t)) for `REBATE`, `PRICE_PROTECTION`, `REFUND` and `VOLUME_TIER` without a target — applies to `DECREASE` elements only: an `INCREASE` element of those types carries no derived refund liability, because an increase creates no refund (D-97's reading of D-87 L5-3-Q-7; D-87's text is unchanged). A target on any other type fails closed (finding `REFUND_TARGET_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED`, D-97 (24); the engine already refuses such streams). Absent `direction` ⇒ the B3-DG-17 default | JET-04b | as the component |
| `CONCESSION` | Attributed POL-104 amounts settled by credit memo or refund − consumption | JET-05c | Consumption by credit memos (JET-04b) |
| `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY` | A − C_total at expiry of a redemption-pattern obligation − consumption (S09-R-28) | JET-04b | Consumption by the remittance credit memo |

```python
def consume_credit_memos(components: list[RlComponent], memos: list[CreditMemo]) -> None:
    """Deterministic FIFO consumption; components sorted (kind order, created (effective_date, event key))."""
    KIND_ORDER = {"TERMINATION": 0, "CONCESSION": 1, "UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY": 2}
    open_ = sorted((c for c in components if c.kind in KIND_ORDER),
                   key=lambda c: (KIND_ORDER[c.kind], c.created_on, c.event_key))
    for memo in memos:                                           # ENG-06 order
        left = memo.amount
        for comp in open_:
            if left == 0: break
            if comp.created_on > memo.effective_date or comp.open == 0: continue
            take = min(comp.open, left)
            comp.consume(take, at=memo.effective_date)
            left -= take
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-13 | Refund liabilities are measured per component, presented separately and never netted into the position of §10.2.3 except through the control-role flows JET-04b creates (POL-127; D-15). The open balance RL(c, e, t) = Σ open component balances. Components are measured in transaction currency. When that currency differs from the contracting entity's functional currency they are monetary items: stage 12 creates their layers at spot and remeasures them at every period end and at settlement (D-25b; S12-R-11, S12-R-19, S12-R-20; JET-10d) |
| S10-R-14 | A credit memo reduces billing (S10-R-03) and, in the same ENG-06 position, consumes open `TERMINATION`, then `CONCESSION`, then `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY` components, oldest first [J: event-created refund amounts are extinguished by the credit memo that pays them; estimate-driven components are remeasured by their estimates]. `RETURN` and `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` components are never consumed |
| S10-R-15 | A component with a contract-level subject (for example a termination refund) is attributed to obligations by posted allocation at its creation date (ALG-01 §2.1.2) for the account strings of JET-04b and for ALG-02 step 5 fallbacks |

#### 10.2.5 Return assets (JET-07c, JET-07d; ALG-06 step 4)

```python
def return_asset_targets(o, t) -> tuple[int, int]:
    rs = return_states[o, t]
    unit = max(Fraction(0), rs.k - rs.c_rec)
    RA = money.round_half_up(unit * rs.E, mu)                                  # balance target
    D = sum(money.round_half_up(unit * min(ret.quantity, ret.E_before), mu)   # JET-07d cumulative
            for ret in ev.return_events(o, at=t))
    return RA + D, D           # (cumulative JET-07c debits − credits, cumulative JET-07d amount); balance = first − second
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-16 | The return-asset balance target is round(max(0, k − c_rec) × E); each return event derecognises round(max(0, k − c_rec) × min(units returned, E before the return)) into inventory (JET-07d, `BILLING_CLEARING` purpose `INVENTORY`); JET-07c carries the remainder against `COST_OF_REVENUE` (CHK-029, CHK-060) |
| S10-R-17 | Return assets are excluded from the position and from netting (POL-127) |

#### 10.2.6 Receivable contra and accounts receivable (`ENGINE` mode)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-18 | RC(c, e, t) = round(B_u excluding taxes × κ), κ = the `rate` of the APPROVED `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION` version at t when present, else (stated consideration − `expected_total_amount`) ÷ stated consideration (S04-R-09; JET-04c; 606-10-32-7; CHK-138). In `ERP` mode RC = 0 and the ERP's contra adjustment arrives as `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` (POLICIES JET-04 [J]) |
| S10-R-19 | AR(c, e, t) = unconditional invoices including tax − credit memos − payments applied. A payment applies to the invoices named in `applied_invoice_numbers`, else to open invoices by ascending (issue date, invoice number). AR feeds JET-10a′ (§12) and the V8 tie-out fact: AR + UR + CA − CL = revenue to date + taxes − cash applied, per contract (REQ-BIL-011) |

#### 10.2.7 Contract asset, unbilled receivable and reclass attribution (ALG-02 steps 2 to 5; ALG-03)

```python
def right_class(o: ObligationState, contract: ContractView) -> str:
    rp = ctx.policies.resolved("balance.right_to_consideration", obligation=o)             # POL-122 (CV-17)
    v, level = rp.value, rp.level
    if level in ("O", "P", "C"):
        return v
    if contract.termination_party in ("CUSTOMER", "BOTH") and contract.termination_has_penalty is False:
        return "CONDITIONAL"                                     # table 2.4-A row 5
    if o.recognition_method in ("RIGHT_TO_INVOICE", "USAGE", "ROYALTY"):
        return "UNCONDITIONAL"                                   # table 2.4-A row 1
    if o.recognition_method == "POINT_IN_TIME" and sole_nonzero_obligation(o, contract):
        return "UNCONDITIONAL"                                   # row 3: payment cannot depend on other POBs
    return "CONDITIONAL"                                         # rows 2 and 4 need P or O values

def accretion_attributed(c, e, t) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Net JET-11 accretion through t by obligation: JET-11a debits less JET-11b credits (ALG-02 step 3; D-76)."""
    net = specialist.interest_income_accreted(c, e, t) - specialist.interest_expense_accreted(c, e, t)
    carriers = sorted(financing_obligations(c, e))               # obligations for which S04-R-10 requires an adjustment
    if net == 0 or not carriers:
        return {}
    weights = [allocation_posted(k, t) for k in carriers]
    if sum(weights) == 0:
        weights = [resolved_ssp(k) for k in carriers]            # positive by POL-077
    return dict(zip(carriers, money.largest_remainder(net, weights, carriers)))   # symmetric for net < 0 (CV-34)

def split_and_attribute(c, e, t) -> Presentation:
    NP = positions[c, e, t].NP
    if NP >= 0:
        return Presentation(CL=NP, CA=0, UR=0, attributions={})
    D = -NP
    obs_e = group_obligations_for_entity(c, e)
    acc = accretion_attributed(c, e, t)
    R = {o.subject_key: revenue_relief(o, t) + acc.get(o.subject_key, 0) for o in obs_e}   # R_p (S10-R-20)
    B = {o.subject_key: billed_attributed(o, t) for o in obs_e}
    uncond = {o.subject_key for o in obs_e if right_class(o, contract_of(o)) == "UNCONDITIONAL"}
    U = sum(max(0, R[k] - B[k]) for k in uncond)
    UR = min(D, U); CA = D - UR
    if ctx.policies.value("position.reclass_attribution_key") == "POB_DEBIT_POSITIONS":
        ur_attr = apportion(UR, {k: max(0, R[k] - B[k]) for k in sorted(uncond)})
        cond = sorted(set(R) - uncond)
        w_ca = {k: max(0, R[k] - B[k]) for k in cond}
        if sum(w_ca.values()) == 0:
            w_ca = {k: allocation_posted(k, t) for k in cond} if cond else {}
        if sum(w_ca.values()) == 0:
            w_ca = {k: allocation_posted(k, t) for k in sorted(R)}
        if sum(w_ca.values()) == 0:
            w_ca = {k: resolved_ssp(k) for k in sorted(R)}
        ca_attr = apportion(CA, w_ca)
    else:                                                         # CUMULATIVE_SSP_DELIVERED (parity)
        non_vc = sorted(k for k in R if kind(k) != "VC_LINE")
        for w in (ssp_delivered_cum, revenue_relief_at, allocation_posted, resolved_ssp):   # DEV-058 chain
            weights = {k: w(k, t) for k in non_vc}
            if sum(weights.values()) != 0:
                break
        shares = apportion(D, weights)
        ur_attr = {k: v for k, v in shares.items() if k in uncond}
        ca_attr = {k: v for k, v in shares.items() if k not in uncond}
        UR, CA = sum(ur_attr.values()), sum(ca_attr.values())
    return Presentation(CL=0, CA=CA, UR=UR, attributions={**{k: ("UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE", v) for k, v in ur_attr.items()},
                                                          **{k: ("CONTRACT_ASSET", v) for k, v in ca_attr.items()}})
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-20 | R_p is cumulative revenue relief attributed to p (S10-R-09 `revenue_relief`) plus the net JET-11 accretion attributed to p (`accretion_attributed`: JET-11a debits less JET-11b credits, apportioned by `largest_remainder` over the obligations for which `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S04-R-10 requires a financing adjustment, weighted by posted allocation, then by resolved SSP; POLICIES ALG-02 step 3 as amended in rev 1.2; D-76; `_coverage/answer-keys-industries:OQ-AKI-12`). B_p is cumulative invoiced consideration attributed to p (S10-R-01). U counts only unconditional obligations (ALG-02 step 3). `apportion` is `largest_remainder` with keys = subject keys. EX-10-C gives the CHK-137 figures |
| S10-R-21 | Default classification follows table 2.4-A with the data the model holds: right-to-invoice, usage billed in arrears and royalties are unconditional; a point-in-time obligation is unconditional only when it is the sole obligation with a non-zero posted allocation for its contracting entity in the contract; a customer termination right without penalty makes every obligation conditional; everything else is conditional unless POL-122 is set at product, contract or obligation level (OVR). This derivation reproduces CHK-010 to CHK-014, CHK-071, CHK-081, CHK-110 and CHK-137, and it stays the 1.0 derivation (OQ-B-10 resolved by D-76; billing plans, REQ-BIL-009, may refine it later) |
| S10-R-22 | Every reclass debit line has a paired `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` credit on the same obligation string (ALG-02 step 5); the reversal is dated the first day of t + 1 with `reason_code = RECLASS_REVERSAL` (JET-06). `netting_reclass_amount` and `netting_reclass_role` (T-CON-11) record the attribution; their exact node values are D × w_p ÷ Σw (DG-PAR-05 `uar_reclass_field`). **No measured period (rev 1.100; C-05).** For a contracting entity none of whose periods from the group's inception period lies inside its horizon, stage 10 presents no period target, reclass entry or member balance; each of its obligations carries the version-date `netting_reclass_amount` of 0 and `netting_reclass_role` NULL, with a node of formula `pos.reclass_attribution.no_measured_period.v1` (no input; exact value 0; params `as_of`, the version date, and `key`), so that every stored figure links the node that produced it (ENGINE_SPEC CV-56) and the exact-column exports bind it (DG-PAR-05) |
| S10-R-23 | Member-contract balances (T-CON-09) for a group with several member contracts: UR and CA are the sums of the member's obligation attributions; CL is apportioned over members by weights max(0, Σ_member (B_p − R_p)) with largest remainder |

#### 10.2.8 Current and noncurrent (POL-124)

```python
def current_split(c, e, t, pres: Presentation) -> CurrentSplit:
    if ctx.policies.value("balance.current_noncurrent", entity=e) == "NONE":
        return CurrentSplit(cl=0, ca=0, ur=0)                     # reporting view suppressed (T-CON-09 defaults)
    h12 = dates.add_months(period_end(t), 12)
    relief_12 = 0
    for o in group_obligations_for_entity(c, e):
        if is_deterministic(o):
            relief_12 += projected_target(o, h12) - revenue_targets[o, t]
        elif o.end_date is None or o.end_date <= h12:
            relief_12 += awaiting_trigger(o, t)
    cl_current = min(pres.CL, max(0, relief_12))
    ca_current = sum(v for k, (role, v) in pres.attributions.items()
                     if role == "CONTRACT_ASSET" and (end_date(k) is None or end_date(k) <= h12))
    return CurrentSplit(cl=cl_current, ca=ca_current, ur=pres.UR)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S10-R-24 | The current part of a contract liability is the revenue relief expected within 12 months after the period end: deterministic projections plus the awaiting-trigger amounts of obligations ending within 12 months or without an end date, capped at the presented liability. Rev 1.126 (item ENG-USAGE-FIXED-SCHEDULE-1; supervisor ruling R-116 (a)): "deterministic" is the predicate of S09-R-45, so the fixed fee of a usage obligation and the guarantee of a right-to-access royalty are projected as a time-elapsed obligation is; until then a usage obligation that ended after the twelve months relieved nothing (0.00 current where 100,000.00 of a liability of 125,000.00 is earned within twelve months; the candidate key of S09-R-45). **Left as it is, and stated (the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-03 on the lane's question).** The relief of a period is the projected target at its twelve-month point less the period's target, both of the computing version. For the last measured period that is exact. For an EARLIER period, as a later version restates it, the difference also holds the usage fees realised since, which were no part of the fee's projection at that period's end: on the candidate key the version of 30 September 2026 states the current part of 31 December 2025 as 163,750.00, where the version of that date states 100,000.00 — the 63,750.00 of fees reported in 2026. The amount is capped at the liability, no platform read serves an earlier period's current part from a later version, and an exact figure would need the projection of the `FIXED` component alone, which stage 09 does not publish. The case is new with rev 1.126: no deterministic obligation held realised usage before |
| S10-R-25 | A contract asset attributed to an obligation ending within 12 months (or without an end date) is current; an unbilled receivable is current [J: only invoicing and the passage of time remain, 606-10-45-4]. The split is a reporting view and posts nothing |
| S10-R-26 | **Share-based reduction (D-91; ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-17; rev 1.8 erratum: count-once numerator, D-91 C606-03 (1)).** Runs last in stage 10, after billing, refund liabilities, concessions, positions and presentation. R(t), the related revenue at the contracting cutoff t, is Σ over the related obligations of the posted `revenue_cum` of the selected node (the latest performing-entity period end c ≤ t, as S10-R-09 maps relief) minus the posted amounts of the concession portions of those obligations dated after c and on or before t (a signed sum; no floor). Each subtracted portion is cited as a formula input with sign − through its own node `concession_portion@<event>:<obligation>:<period>` (`tp.cpc_release.v1` over the producing event's `contract_event` source valued at the portion's posted amount; params `effective_date`, `exact`, `producer`, `revenue_basis`, `signs` and `aggregate_node`, the id of the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node at t or `-` when none exists at t; emitted once per (event, obligation, period) and shared by every element that cites it; the aggregate node's id is never an input, so it is never duplicated). A portion dated on or before c is embedded in the selected node (both 1.0-rc producers apply the negative share to the obligation's recognition segment, and stage 09 excludes only boundaries after the node's own cutoff) and is neither subtracted nor cited; the element param `concessions_embedded` counts them and `revenue_cutoff` lists the selected nodes' cutoffs (`-` without one). A portion marked SEPARATE (no 1.0-rc producer; a producer that posts a JET-05c reduction the recognition segment does not carry must mark its portions so) is always subtracted; without a revenue node at t every portion dated on or before t is subtracted (signed, no floor). Each concession therefore counts exactly once at every t and drops out of the subtraction at the first performing close that embeds it. The portions are `AllocatedState.concession_history`, a dated source subledger with one entry per (receiving obligation, producing event) carrying the producer's signed exact share and its separately allocated posted minor units as produced, never re-rounded: stage 06 S06-R-09 under CREDIT_OR_REFUND (−receiver.exact / −receiver.posted; event `CONTRACT_AMENDED`; producer `06`) and stage 08 D-88 L7-5-Q-4 (iii) targeted `DISCOUNT` / `SLA_CREDIT` on a complete obligation without an explicit `refund_liability_target` (−share.exact / −share.delta; event `ESTIMATE_CHANGED`; producer `08`); eligibility is unchanged and there is no reversal behaviour. Conservation (`check_history`, once per measure, before any element): per obligation Σ portion.exact = quota.x_exact and Σ portion.posted = quota.a_posted against the FINAL `CONCESSION` refund quota, never re-rounded (termination keys `<contract>#TERMINATION@<event>` are outside the check); every portion names a producing event of the state, is dated by it and names producer `06` or `08`; a quota without history, a history without a quota, a history that does not conserve the quota in exact or posted terms, or a non-empty quota (exact ≠ 0 or posted ≠ 0) with absent history raises `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` (CV-46; detail `rule` S10-R-26); the empty-quota shortcut applies only when exact and posted are both 0 and no history exists. The portions are never compared with the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node at an intermediate t, neither by equality nor by a bound: the aggregate is dated by `refund_liability._concession_event` (a settled modification is preferred over a later TP_CHANGE), so the two populations carry unlike dates across producers. The aggregate refund component, its node, JET-05c / JET-04b netting and credit-memo consumption are unchanged. Per element Red_e(t) = 0 while t < `grant_date` or `vesting_probable` is false under the version pinned at t; when E ≤ 0, R(t) = 0 gives Red_e(t) = 0 before any division (no 0 ÷ 0) and R(t) ≠ 0 raises `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` (CV-32); else Red_e(t) = round_half_up(min(F, F × R(t) ÷ E)); `CPC_RELATED_SCOPE_INVALID` (`MISMATCH`, `UNKNOWN_OBLIGATION`) when element keys disagree with the promise scope or name no obligation. Nothing in stages 04 to 10 reads the release, the reduction is outside `allocation_basis` (S04-R-02) and outside the net position (REQ-BIL-007), so one ordered pass is exact and no fixed point exists; `RecognitionState.allocated` stays unchanged (§0.4, §9.1). Nodes: `share_based_reduction_element_cum:<contract>/<element>:<period>` (`tp.cpc_share_based.v1`), `concession_portion@<event>:<obligation>:<period>` (`tp.cpc_release.v1`; rev 1.8), `share_based_reduction_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>` and `incentive_release_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>` (`tp.cpc_release.v1` signed sums of posted nodes; no further rounding) |

### 10.3 Invariants

| Id | Invariant | Check |
|---|---|---|
| S10-INV-01 | presented CL − CA − UR = NP for every (c, e, b, t) | ALG-02 step 6; DG-ENG-05 |
| S10-INV-02 | CL, CA, UR, RL components, RA, RC and AR are ≥ 0 | T-CON-09 CHECKs |
| S10-INV-03 | Σ UR attributions = UR; Σ CA attributions = CA; every attribution is on an obligation of (c, e) | unit test |
| S10-INV-04 | Σ over obligations of a billing document's attribution = the document amount excluding tax (S10-R-01) | unit test |
| S10-INV-05 | NP = B_u + DT + NC + IE − II − R_ctl − RC − RL (S10-R-09) | PROP:P6 |
| S10-INV-06 | In `ERP` mode no `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE`, `RECEIVABLE_CONTRA` or `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE` targets exist | unit test |
| S10-INV-07 | Consumption of a credit memo never exceeds its amount; components never go below 0 | unit test |
| S10-INV-08 | No balance exists for an entity that is only a performing entity (ALG-07 step 3) | PROP:P6 |
| S10-INV-09 | Current parts are ≤ their balances | unit test |

| Code (04 §15.4) | Severity | Condition |
|---|---|---|
| `INVOICE_ON_NOT_A_CONTRACT` | WARNING | Raised by stage 02 (S02-R-11); stage 10 excludes the billing from the position (S10-R-04). `REFUND_EXCEEDS_BILLED` is raised by stage 01; `VC_CREDIT_EXCEEDS_ESTIMATE` by the import pipeline (CV-44) |
| `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` | ERROR (04 table 15.4-B, rev 1.2) | S10-R-07 |
| `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_TAX_MISMATCH` | WARNING (04 table 15.4-B, rev 1.24) | S10-R-07: a status update's `tax_amount` differs from the first line's; the update still dates its line, its tax is dropped, no value effect (D-97 (27)) |
| `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` | ERROR | S10-INV-01, 03, 04, 05, 07 |
| `CPC_RELATED_SCOPE_INVALID` | ERROR (04 table 15.4-C, rev 1.11; D-91) | S10-R-26: element keys disagree with the share-based promise scope, or a scope key names no obligation of the contract |
| `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` | ERROR | S10-R-26: `expected_total_amount` ≤ 0 with related revenue ≠ 0 (related revenue 0 gives a zero reduction) |

### 10.5 Trace nodes and formula ids

| Measure | Subject | Period | Formula id |
|---|---|---|---|
| `billed_cum` | obligation | period, `-` | `bil.attribution.v1` (every obligation; inputs: the billing and credit-memo events and, where present, `billed_attributed_cum`; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S01-R-19) |
| `billed_attributed_cum` | obligation | period, `-` | `bil.attribution.v1` (only for obligations that received a share of unreferenced billing, S10-R-01) |
| `accretion_attributed_cum` | obligation | period | `pos.accretion_attribution.v1` (net JET-11 accretion attributed for the presentation split, S10-R-20) |
| `billed_unconditional_cum` | contract@entity | period | `bil.unconditional_date.v1` |
| `remaining_billing` | obligation | `-` | `bil.remaining_billing.v1` |
| `position_obligation`, `position_contract_entity` | obligation; contract@entity | `-` | `pos.obligation.v1` (with `rounding_residue` from the exact revenue) |
| `net_position` | group@entity | period | `pos.net_position.v1` |
| `contract_liability`, `contract_asset`, `unbilled_receivable` | group@entity | period | `pos.split_ca_ur.v1` |
| `netting_reclass_amount` | obligation | period, `-` | `pos.reclass_attribution.pob_debit_positions.v1`, `pos.reclass_attribution.cumulative_ssp_delivered.v1`; at `-` only, for an obligation whose contracting entity has no measured period: `pos.reclass_attribution.no_measured_period.v1` (no input; 0; S10-R-22 rev 1.100) |
| `refund_liability` | `<group@entity>/<component>` or obligation | period | `rl.return.v2` (`rl.return.v1` stored traces only; rev 1.7), `rl.vc_target.v1`, `rl.termination.v1`, `rl.concession.v1`, `rl.unclaimed_property.v1`, `rl.consumption.v1` |
| `return_asset`, `return_asset_derecognised_cum` | obligation | period | `returns.return_asset.v1` |
| `receivable_contra`, `accounts_receivable` | contract@entity | period | `pos.receivable_contra.v1`, `bil.ar_balance.v1` |
| `share_based_reduction_element_cum` | `<contract>/<element>` | period | `tp.cpc_share_based.v1` (inputs: the award `estimate_version` source with value = grant-date fair value, then per related obligation the selected posted `revenue_cum` node (latest performing-entity period end c ≤ t) and the `concession_portion@<event>` nodes of the portions dated after c and on or before t, or every portion when no revenue node exists; never the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node; params `signs`, `expected`, `vesting_probable`, `grant_date`, `period_end`, `revenue_cutoff`, `concessions_embedded`, `timing`, `minor_unit`; S10-R-26 rev 1.8) |
| `concession_portion@<event>` | obligation | period | `tp.cpc_release.v1` (one `contract_event` `SourceRef` of the producing `CONTRACT_AMENDED` or `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event, member `refund_component`, valued at the portion's posted amount; params `aggregate_node` (the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node id at t, or `-`), `effective_date`, `exact`, `producer` (`06` or `08`), `revenue_basis`, `signs` `+`; emitted once per (event, obligation, period) and shared by every element that cites it; S10-R-26 rev 1.8) |
| `share_based_reduction_cum` | contract@entity | period | `tp.cpc_release.v1` (signed sum of the element nodes) |
| `incentive_release_cum` | contract@entity | period | `tp.cpc_release.v1` (inputs [`incentive_release_ordinary_cum`, `share_based_reduction_cum`], signs +,+; posted sum, no further rounding) |
| `contract_liability_current`, `contract_asset_current`, `unbilled_receivable_current` | group@entity | period | `pos.current_split.v1` |

## 11. Stage 11: contract costs and loss contracts

Stage 11 measures ASC 340-40 cost assets (capitalisation, amortisation, impairment, the IFRS reversal, clawbacks and termination acceleration) and ASC 605-35 or IAS 37 loss provisions. It produces cumulative targets and the `COST_AMORTIZATION` and `LOSS_PROVISION_RELEASE` schedules; stage 14 derives JET-09 and JET-12 lines.

### 11.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Produced by | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `COST_INCURRED` with `purpose` ∈ {`COST_TO_OBTAIN`, `COST_TO_FULFILL`} (payload `amount`, `obligation_key`, `payee`, `plan_code`, `is_incremental`, `has_clawback`) | stage 01 | Capitalisation and clawback (S11-R-01 to S11-R-06) |
| `COST_INCURRED` with `purpose = PROGRESS_INPUT` | stage 01 | Costs to date for impairment and loss tests |
| `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` for `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` and `EAC` | stage 01 pin index | Amortisation period; remaining direct costs; loss test costs |
| `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` | stage 01; stage 06 segments | Acceleration (POL-145) |
| `revenue_targets`, obligation allocations | stage 09 | Proportional amortisation; revenue to date; remaining consideration |
| `tp_unconstrained` | stage 04 (S04-R-21) | Unconstrained, credit-adjusted consideration (§11.2.5) |
| REVIEWED judgement records (fulfilment-cost 25-5 attestation, POL-146) | bundle | Fulfilment-cost gate |
| Resolved POL-090 (straight-line convention), POL-140 to POL-146, POL-150 to POL-153 | `BookContext.policies` | Options per book |
| Contract flag `scope_605_35` in force at the period end: T-CON-01 column, set by the booking payload and changed only by a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` payload that carries the member (04 rev 1.2; OQ-B-12 resolved by D-76; the interim `custom_attributes.scope_605_35` is never read) | bundle | ASC606 loss scope (S11-R-15) |

| Output (`CostLossState`) | Content | Consumers |
|---|---|---|
| `cost_assets` | Per asset, book and period end: `capitalized_cum`, `amortized_cum`, `impaired_cum`, `impairment_reversed_cum`, `clawback_cum`, `accelerated_cum`, `carrying_amount`, `remaining_months` (T-CON-16) | stage 14 (JET-09), stage 15 (cost rollforward), persistence |
| `cost_asset_specs` | Identity and terms for T-CON-15: `cost_kind`, `capitalization_date`, `amount_capitalized`, `related_obligation_ids` (as keys), `amortization_pattern`, `amortization_start_date`, `amortization_months`, `has_clawback` | persistence |
| `expensed_costs` | Costs not capitalised with the reason (`NOT_INCREMENTAL`, `EXPEDIENT_ONE_YEAR`, `FULFILMENT_NOT_ATTESTED`, `CONTRACT_NOT_ACTIVE`) | stage 15 (`EXPEDIENTS_USED`), trace |
| `loss_provisions` | Per loss unit, book and period end: T-CON-17 values | stage 14 (JET-12), register (REQ-LOS-003) |
| `schedule_lines` | `COST_AMORTIZATION` per asset; `LOSS_PROVISION_RELEASE` per unit | persistence, waterfall |
| `findings` | §11.4 | orchestrator |

### 11.2 Pseudocode

#### 11.2.1 Capitalisation gate (340-40-25-1 to 25-8; POL-140, POL-146)

```python
def capitalisation(evt: CostEvent, ctx: BookContext) -> CostAssetSpec | ExpensedCost:
    contract = contract_of(evt)
    related = related_obligations(evt)                        # obligation_key, else every obligation of the contract
    if any(o.group_code != group_code for o in related):
        raise EngineError("COST_RELATED_POB_MISSING", subject_key=evt.key)
    active_on = first_active_date(contract, ctx.book_code)    # status_in_book becomes ACTIVE
    if active_on is None:
        return ExpensedCost(evt, reason="CONTRACT_NOT_ACTIVE")
    cap_date = max(evt.effective_date, active_on)
    start = min(o.start_date or contract.inception_date for o in related)
    months = amortisation_months(evt, related, cap_date)      # §11.2.2
    if evt.purpose == "COST_TO_OBTAIN":
        if not evt.is_incremental:
            return ExpensedCost(evt, reason="NOT_INCREMENTAL")                   # 340-40-25-3
        if months <= 12 and ctx.policies.value("costs.obtain_expedient", contract=contract) == "APPLY":
            return ExpensedCost(evt, reason="EXPEDIENT_ONE_YEAR")                # 340-40-25-4; 50-5 disclosure
        kind = "OBTAIN"
    else:
        if not fulfilment_attested(evt):                                         # POL-146 WHEN_25_5_ATTESTED
            return ExpensedCost(evt, reason="FULFILMENT_NOT_ATTESTED")
        kind = "FULFILL"
    pattern = ctx.policies.value("costs.amortisation_pattern", obligation=related[0])
    if pattern == "PROPORTIONAL_TO_RELATED_REVENUE" and months != contract_term_months(related):
        raise EngineError("COST_PATTERN_INVALID", subject_key=evt.key)            # POL-143
    return CostAssetSpec(kind=kind, capitalization_date=cap_date, amount=evt.amount, related=related,
                         pattern=pattern, start=start, months=max(1, months), has_clawback=bool(evt.has_clawback))
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-01 | An incremental cost of obtaining a contract is capitalised to `COST_TO_OBTAIN_ASSET` unless POL-140 `APPLY` and the derived amortisation period is 12 months or less (PRD J-12: `SF-ORD-20417` commission 5,760.00 expensed). A cost incurred while the contract is not active in the book is capitalised on the date the contract becomes active |
| S11-R-02 | A cost to fulfil is capitalised to `COST_TO_FULFILL_ASSET` only with a REVIEWED judgement record attesting the three 340-40-25-5 criteria and no other-Topic scope (25-6), referenced by the event's `plan_code` or by the obligation's product (POL-146). Wasted costs and costs of satisfied performance are never capitalised (25-8) |
| S11-R-03 | `amortization_start_date` is the earliest start date of the related obligations (the contract inception date when none has a start date); the asset is amortised over `amortization_months` from that date, end date = add_months(start, months) − 1 day |

#### 11.2.2 Amortisation period (POL-141, POL-142; 340-40-35-1, 35-2)

```python
def amortisation_months(evt, related, at: date) -> int:
    option = ctx.policies.value("costs.amortisation_period", obligation=related[0])
    term = contract_term_months(related)                     # whole months of [min start, max end], rounded up
    if option == "CONTRACT_TERM":
        return term
    pin = ev.pin_contract(contract_of(evt), kind="RENEWAL_EXPECTATION", at=at)
    if option == "EXPECTED_CUSTOMER_LIFE":
        return pin.amortization_months if pin else term
    return pin.amortization_months if pin else term          # TERM_PLUS_EXPECTED_RENEWALS_UNLESS_COMMENSURATE
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-04 | Under the default option the approved `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version states the amortisation months including expected renewals when renewal commissions are not commensurate (POL-142 ratio test documented in the version's rationale and approved under EST). Without a version the initial contract term applies. The engine does not compute the POL-142 test in 1.0: it stays documented in the approved `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version, and no payload fields are added (OQ-B-11 resolved by D-76) |
| S11-R-05 | A later `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version is a significant change in expected timing (340-40-35-2): a new segment starts on the day after its effective date and amortises the carrying amount after amortisation through the effective date over the new remaining months (D-89 L7-6-Q-7). Prior amortisation is not restated |

#### 11.2.3 Amortisation segments (POL-143; JET-09b)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CostSegment:
    start: date                 # first day amortised in this segment
    end: date                   # last day of the amortisation period
    base: int                   # carrying amount at the start of the segment (minor units)
    cause: str                  # CAPITALISATION | IMPAIRMENT | REVERSAL | CLAWBACK | ACCELERATION | PERIOD_CHANGE

def amortised_cum(asset, d: date) -> int:
    total = 0
    for k, seg in enumerate(asset.segments):
        stop = asset.segments[k + 1].start - timedelta(days=1) if k + 1 < len(asset.segments) else d
        g = segment_progress(asset, seg, min(d, stop))
        total += money.cumulative_posted(Fraction(seg.base, 10**mu), seg.base, g, mu)
        if d < (asset.segments[k + 1].start if k + 1 < len(asset.segments) else date.max):
            break
    return total

def segment_progress(asset, seg, d) -> Fraction:
    if asset.pattern == "STRAIGHT_LINE":
        conv = ctx.policies.value("recognition.time_convention", obligation=asset.related[0])   # POL-090 (CV-17)
        return progress.time_fraction(conv, seg.start, seg.end, d, calendar=entity_calendar(asset.entity))
    related_A = sum(allocation_posted(o, d) for o in asset.related)                        # PROPORTIONAL
    C_d = sum(revenue_targets[o, d] for o in asset.related)
    C_0 = sum(revenue_targets[o, seg.start - timedelta(days=1)] for o in asset.related)
    return Fraction(1) if related_A == C_0 else min(Fraction(1), Fraction(C_d - C_0, related_A - C_0))
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-06 | Straight-line amortisation is ALG-11 over the segment with the time convention resolved under POL-090 for the plan's product or tenant (`DAILY` by default; CHK-130 uses `MONTHLY_EVEN` on whole years). The posted amount of a segment is cumulatively rounded over its own base (ALG-01 §2.1.3), so each segment completes exactly at its base |
| S11-R-07 | A segment ends the day before the next segment starts; the next segment's base is the carrying amount after the event that starts it. Events that start a segment: impairment and reversal (period end + 1 day), a `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` change (effective date + 1 day, with base the carrying amount after amortisation through the effective date; D-89 L7-6-Q-7), clawback and acceleration (their effective date). The segment end date stays the asset's end date except for a period change (S11-R-05) or acceleration (S11-R-13) |

#### 11.2.4 Impairment and the IFRS reversal (340-40-35-3 to 35-6; IFRS 15.104; POL-144; JET-09c, JET-09d)

```python
def period_end_test(assets_of_contract, t: date, book: BookCode) -> None:
    carrying = {a.key: carrying_amount(a, t) for a in assets_of_contract}          # after amortisation through t
    related = union_related(assets_of_contract)
    remaining_consideration = (credit_adjusted_unconstrained_allocated(related, t)   # §11.2.5
                               - sum(revenue_targets[o, t] for o in related)
                               + renewal_expected_consideration(related, t))        # anticipated renewals and extensions:
                                                                                     # RENEWAL_EXPECTATION expected_total_amount (D-76)
    eac = ev.pin_for(related, kind="EAC", at=t)                                       # includes the costs of those renewals (S11-R-09)
    if eac is None:
        remaining_costs = 0
        finding_once("COST_NO_EAC", asset_keys(assets_of_contract))                  # INFO
    else:
        if has_progress_input_cost(related, at=t):                                   # a PROGRESS_INPUT cost effective on or before t
            costs = progress_costs_to_date(related, t)
        else:                                                                         # run-off with revenue progress (D-89 L7-6-Q-7)
            costs = money.cumulative_posted(Fraction(eac.expected_total_amount, 10**mu), eac.expected_total_amount, revenue_progress_since(related, eac.effective_date, t), mu)  # run-off with revenue progress since the latest EAC version effective at t (D-89 L7-6-Q-7)
        remaining_costs = max(0, eac.expected_total_amount - costs)
    recoverable = remaining_consideration - remaining_costs
    total = sum(carrying.values())
    impairment = min(total, max(0, total - recoverable))                              # never above the carrying amount (rev 1.3; S11-R-08)
    if impairment:
        split = money.largest_remainder(impairment, [carrying[k] for k in sorted(carrying)], sorted(carrying))
        for k, amount in zip(sorted(carrying), split):
            start_segment(k, start=t + timedelta(days=1), base=carrying[k] - amount, cause="IMPAIRMENT", amount=amount)
    elif book == "IFRS15":                                                            # POL-144 REQUIRED_CAPPED
        for a in assets_of_contract:
            unimpaired = unimpaired_carrying(a, t)                                    # first-segment schedule, net of clawbacks
            reversal = max(0, min(recoverable_share(a, recoverable, carrying), unimpaired) - carrying[a.key])
            reversal = min(reversal, impaired_open(a))                                # never above impairments not yet reversed
            if reversal:
                start_segment(a.key, start=t + timedelta(days=1), base=carrying[a.key] + reversal, cause="REVERSAL", amount=reversal)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-08 | The test runs at every period end within the horizon, after amortisation through the period end. Assets whose related obligations are the same are tested together; the impairment is apportioned by carrying amount (ALG-01 §2.1.2) [J: the remaining consideration of 340-40-35-3(a) belongs to the contract, so testing assets separately would count it twice]. The impairment never exceeds the total carrying amount, so each asset's carrying amount floors at 0.00: 340-40-35-3 recognises a loss to the extent that the carrying amount exceeds the net amount (rev 1.3; ADJUDICATION.md R-COST-03). A shortfall beyond the carrying amount is never posted by JET-09c; it is a matter for the Subtopic 605-35 loss test when the contract is in that scope (S11-R-15) |
| S11-R-09 | The impairment test includes consideration from anticipated renewals and extensions (340-40-35-3, 35-4 as amended by ASU 2016-20; D-76 ruling on contract-cost impairment; POLICIES JET-09c rev 1.2; 05 EMOD-16 as amended). Remaining consideration = the unconstrained, credit-adjusted transaction price allocated to the related obligations, less revenue recognised through the period end, plus the consideration of the anticipated renewals and extensions used in the amortisation period: the `expected_total_amount` of the approved `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version effective at the period end, 0 without one. Remaining direct costs = max(0, the `expected_total_amount` of the latest `EAC` version effective at the period end − costs). Costs are the progress costs incurred when a `PROGRESS_INPUT` cost of the related obligations is effective on or before the period end; otherwise costs = cumulative_posted(eac, g), with eac the `expected_total_amount` of the latest `EAC` version effective at the period end and g the related obligations' revenue progress since that version's effective date, so the remaining costs run off with revenue progress (D-89 L7-6-Q-7; JE-CHK-131 ASC606 P03 recoverable 12,500.00). That version includes the costs of the anticipated renewals and extensions whenever the `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` version in force includes renewals (the preparer's estimate; no data member is added). With no `EAC` version the costs are 0 and `COST_NO_EAC` (INFO) is raised once per asset (05 §3.6.1). COST-CAP-COMMISSION-EXPECTED-RENEWALS-IMPAIRMENT tests the fall of the renewal expectation to the initial term |
| S11-R-10 | ASC606: impairment is never reversed. IFRS15: a reversal is recognised when the recoverable amount rises, capped at the carrying amount that would have existed without impairment and at impairments not yet reversed (CHK-131). A recoverable amount is shared across a contract's assets by carrying amount |

#### 11.2.5 Unconstrained, credit-adjusted consideration

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-11a | The unconstrained, credit-adjusted consideration is `tp_unconstrained[contract].at(d)` of stage 04 (S04-R-21, which governs): fixed consideration + unconstrained VC + realised usage and royalties + implicit price concession + financing adjustment + noncash consideration − consideration payable + the expected-returns memo, replaced by the latest `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED.expected_collectible_amount` of the book when lower (605-35-25-46A; 340-40-35-4) |
| S11-R-11b | For a unit smaller than the contract (POB loss unit or a cost asset related to some obligations), the unconstrained consideration is allocated by the obligations' current exact allocation weights: X_u,p = TP_u × x_exact_p ÷ Σ x_exact [J: the 32-28 to 32-41 allocation already fixed the relative weights, and 32-43 forbids re-weighting for price changes] |

#### 11.2.6 Clawbacks and termination acceleration (POL-145; JET-09e)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-12 | **Clawback.** A `COST_INCURRED` with `purpose = COST_TO_OBTAIN` and payload member `cost_adjustment = CLAWBACK` (04 E-03 payload, rev 1.2, where `payee` and `plan_code` are then required; 05 §3.6.1; OQ-B-11 resolved by D-76) names the recovered commission by `payee` and `plan_code`. It reduces the matching asset (oldest capitalisation first) by min(amount, carrying) and starts a segment. JET-09f posts Dr `CONTRACT_COST_CLEARING` / Cr asset role with `reason_code = CLAWBACK` (POLICIES rev 1.2); any excess over the carrying amount stays in the ERP |
| S11-R-13 | **Acceleration.** On `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` effective d_T with POL-145 `ACCELERATE_TO_REMAINING_BENEFIT`: ρ = remaining allocation of the related obligations after the termination ÷ remaining allocation before (0 for a full termination of every related obligation; 1 when none is affected). Acceleration = round(carrying(d_T) × (1 − ρ)), posted by JET-09e with `reason_code = TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`; a new segment amortises the rest to the latest end date of the remaining related obligations (CHK-112). Under `CONTINUE` nothing accelerates |

#### 11.2.7 Loss provisions (605-35-25-45 to 25-47; IAS 37.66, 68A; POL-150 to POL-153; JET-12)

```python
def loss_units(book: BookCode, t: date) -> list[LossUnit]:                  # evaluated at every period end t
    unit = ctx.policies.value("loss.unit")                                   # CONTRACT | POB (IFRS15 forced CONTRACT)
    scope = ctx.policies.value("loss.scope")                                 # SCOPED_605_35_ONLY | ALL_CONTRACTS_WITH_EAC
    units = []
    for contract in contracts:
        in_scope = scope_605_35_at(contract, t) if scope == "SCOPED_605_35_ONLY" \
                   else ev.has_pin(contract, kind="EAC")                     # T-CON-01 flag in force at t (§11.1)
        if not in_scope:
            continue
        units += [LossUnit(contract, None)] if unit == "CONTRACT" else [LossUnit(contract, o) for o in obligations_of(contract)]
    return units

def required_provision(u: LossUnit, t: date, book: BookCode) -> LossMeasure:
    eac = ev.pin_for(u, kind="EAC", element=eac_element_for_book(u, book), at=t)   # §11.2.7 S11-R-15
    costs = progress_costs_to_date(u, t)                                             # all PROGRESS_INPUT costs, any flag
    revenue = sum(revenue_targets[o, t] for o in unit_obligations(u))
    tp_u = credit_adjusted_unconstrained(u, t)                                       # §11.2.5
    if eac is None:
        if costs > 0 and uses_cost_measure(u):
            finding("LOSS_EAC_MISSING", u)                                           # WARNING; blocks lock via EXCEPTIONS_CLEARED
        return LossMeasure(required=0, expected_consideration=tp_u, expected_total_costs=0, costs=costs, revenue=revenue)
    total_loss = max(0, eac.expected_total_amount - tp_u)
    margin_loss = max(0, costs - revenue)                                           # loss already recognised through margin
    required = max(0, total_loss - margin_loss)
    return LossMeasure(required=required, expected_consideration=tp_u, expected_total_costs=eac.expected_total_amount,
                       costs=costs, revenue=revenue, expected_margin=tp_u - eac.expected_total_amount)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S11-R-14 | The provision balance at period end t is required(t) = max(0, total expected loss − loss already recognised through margin to date), where total expected loss = max(0, EAC costs − unconstrained credit-adjusted consideration) and loss through margin to date = max(0, costs incurred − revenue recognised) for the unit (POL-153; CHK-132). JET-12 posts required(t) − required(t − 1): an increase to `LOSS_EXPENSE`, a release against it |
| S11-R-15 | Books: ASC606 tests contracts flagged in 605-35 scope (POL-151), at the POL-150 unit, with 605-35 costs from the `EAC` element. IFRS15 tests every contract with an `EAC` version at contract level (POL-150, POL-151 FORCED), with IAS 37.68A costs from an element whose `element_code` is `EAC_IAS37` when present, otherwise the `EAC` element (04 T-CON-12 reserved element code and T-CON-01 `scope_605_35`, rev 1.2; OQ-B-12 resolved by D-76) |
| S11-R-16 | The consideration in the test is unconstrained (605-35-25-46A), so a constrained bonus lowers revenue to date without lowering the expected consideration; the margin loss then absorbs part of the expected loss (EX-11-B) |
| S11-R-17 | The `LOSS_PROVISION_RELEASE` schedule is informational (`is_released_at_close = false`): it projects the release of the current provision in proportion to the remaining scheduled revenue of the unit when every unit obligation is `TIME_ELAPSED`, else as one line in the period of the latest unit end date. Postings come only from S11-R-14. Rev 1.126 (item ENG-USAGE-FIXED-SCHEDULE-1; supervisor ruling R-116 (a) and the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-03 on lane ENG-FX's reading): the release asks the METHOD of every unit obligation, not the predicate of S09-R-45. Its question is whether the unit's `REVENUE` schedule lines are the whole of the unit's remaining revenue, and the lines of a usage obligation with a fixed fee are the fee alone — its usage fees stand on no schedule. Such an obligation is deterministic for S09-R-45, and the provision of its unit is still released as one line in the period of the unit's latest end date. Until rev 1.126 the two questions had one answer and this row said "deterministic" for both; no released amount changes |

### 11.3 Invariants

| Id | Invariant | Check |
|---|---|---|
| S11-INV-01 | `carrying_amount = capitalized_cum − amortized_cum − impaired_cum + impairment_reversed_cum − clawback_cum − accelerated_cum` ≥ 0 | T-CON-16; unit test |
| S11-INV-02 | Each segment amortises exactly its base when its progress reaches 1; with no impairment, reversal, clawback or acceleration Σ amortisation = amount capitalised | PROP:P4 analogue |
| S11-INV-03 | ASC606: `impairment_reversed_cum = 0` | POL-144 |
| S11-INV-04 | IFRS15: carrying after a reversal ≤ unimpaired carrying amount; Σ reversals ≤ Σ impairments | CHK-131 |
| S11-INV-05 | 0 ≤ required provision ≤ total expected loss; at completion (costs = EAC and revenue = TP_u) required = 0 | CHK-132 |
| S11-INV-06 | Cost-rollforward identity per asset: opening + additions − amortisation − impairment + reversal − clawback − acceleration = closing | PROP:P6; REQ-CST-006 |
