## 12. Stage 12: foreign currency and multi-entity

Stage 12 gives every target of stages 02 to 11 its functional-currency amount per ASC 830 and IAS 21 with IFRIC 22 (D-25, D-25a; ALG-08), measures the remeasurement of monetary positions, including refund liabilities, deposit liabilities and consideration payable denominated in a foreign currency (D-25b; POL-164; JET-10d), and derives intercompany pair amounts when the performing entity differs from the contracting entity (D-23; ALG-07).

### 12.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Produced by | Use |
|---|---|---|
| Control-role flows per (group, contracting entity) with dates: unconditional billing and receipts, credit memos, revenue relief per obligation, refund-liability and contra movements, deposit transfers, noncash, interest | stages 09, 10; `specialist_targets` | Layer algorithm |
| `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` | stage 01 | POL-160 layer date |
| Accounts receivable per invoice (`ENGINE` mode) | stage 10 | JET-10a′ |
| Rate sets pinned for the computation (T-REF-10 to T-REF-12: `spot`, `closing`, `average`) | bundle | Conversion |
| Entity functional currencies and calendars | `BookContext.entities` | Conversion and periods |
| Resolved POL-160 to POL-164, POL-170, POL-171, POL-181 | `BookContext.policies` | Options; the POL-163 contract override; POL-164 `ASSET_POSITIONS_ENGINE_AR_AND_MONETARY_LIABILITIES` (D-25b) |
| Monetary-liability flows per (group, contracting entity) with dates: refund-liability component creation, remeasurement by its estimate, consumption and window expiry (§10.2.4; JET-04b, JET-05c); deposit receipts, refunds, transfers at criteria met and 25-7 derecognitions (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S02-R-07, S02-R-08; JET-01b); consideration-payable promises (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S04-R-14; JET-14) | stages 02, 04, 10 | Monetary-liability layers (S12-R-19 to S12-R-21) |

| Output (`FxState`) | Content | Consumers |
|---|---|---|
| `layer_movements` | T-CON-18 rows: `layer_key`, `movement_kind` (E-86), `balance_role` (`CONTRACT_LIABILITY`, `CONTRACT_ASSET`, `UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE`, `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE`, `REFUND_LIABILITY`, `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY`, `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`), `effective_date`, amounts in transaction and functional currency, rate | persistence, rollforward FX lines |
| `functional_targets` | The functional amount of every target that stage 14 posts (revenue relief, reclass, refund-liability, deposit-liability and consideration-payable movements, cost and loss targets of foreign-currency assets) | stage 14 |
| `remeasurement_targets` | Cumulative JET-10a (asset positions, including settlement), JET-10a′ (AR), JET-10b (contract-liability override), JET-10c (credit-memo settlement difference, S12-R-08) and JET-10d (monetary liabilities at recognition, period end and settlement, S12-R-11, S12-R-19, S12-R-20) per (group, contracting entity) and period | stage 14 |
| `ic_pairs` | Per obligation with performing entity ≠ contracting entity and period: transaction amount and each entity's functional amount | stage 14 (JET-13), report `intercompany_pairs` |
| `findings` | §12.4 | orchestrator |

### 12.2 Pseudocode

#### 12.2.1 Rates

```python
def rate(base: str, quote: str, kind: str, *, on: date | None = None, period: PeriodView | None = None) -> RateView:
    """Explicit rates only; no inversion or triangulation at use (research 06 §9.5)."""
    if base == quote:
        return RateView(value=Fraction(1), rate_id=None)
    rows = fx_rates(base=base, quote=quote, rate_type=kind)                     # pinned APPROVED versions
    if kind == "spot":
        cands = [r for r in rows if r.effective_date <= on]
        r = max(cands, key=lambda r: r.effective_date) if cands else None       # latest on or before the date
    else:                                                                         # "average" | "closing"
        cands = [r for r in rows if r.period_key == period.key] or \
                [r for r in rows if kind == "closing" and r.effective_date == period.end]
        r = cands[0] if len(cands) == 1 else None
    if r is None:
        raise EngineError("FX_RATE_MISSING", detail={"base": base, "quote": quote, "type": kind})
    return RateView(value=to_fraction(r.rate), rate_id=r.key)

def to_functional(amount_minor: int, txn: str, fn: str, rv: RateView) -> int:
    return money.round_half_up(Fraction(amount_minor, 10**mu(txn)) * rv.value, mu(fn))
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-01 | A rate converts one unit of the transaction currency into the functional currency (base = transaction, quote = functional). Spot is the latest rate on or before the date; average and closing are the rates of the period (T-REF-12 `period_key`, or for closing the rate dated the period end). A missing rate raises `FX_RATE_MISSING` (ERROR; the computation is `QUARANTINED`; API 422 `missing-fx-rate`) |
| S12-R-02 | Every functional amount is round(transaction exact × rate) at the functional minor unit, except layer reliefs, which are cumulatively rounded shares of the layer's functional amount (S12-R-05). The rate id of every conversion is carried to the subledger line (REQ-FX-006) |

#### 12.2.2 Layers per (group, contracting entity, book) (POL-160 to POL-163; ALG-08 §2.9.1)

```python
@dataclass
class Layer:                                   # engine-internal mutable state inside one compute call only
    key: str                                   # "<balance role>:<global event key>" (T-CON-18; CV-22)
    role: str                                  # CONTRACT_LIABILITY | CONTRACT_ASSET (asset position; presentation split in §10) |
                                               # REFUND_LIABILITY | DEPOSIT_LIABILITY | CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE (monetary, D-25b)
    txn_original: int
    txn_open: int
    fn_original: int                           # historical functional amount (liability) or carrying at creation (asset)
    fn_carrying: int
    rate_id: str | None

def process_flows(flows: Sequence[Flow], fn: str, txn: str) -> list[LayerMovement]:
    moves = []
    for fl in flows:                           # ENG-06 order; period-end flows ordered by subject key
        if fl.side == "CREDIT":                # unconditional billing or receipt, deposit transfer, noncash, interest expense
            left = fl.amount
            for a in fifo(open_layers("CONTRACT_ASSET")):
                take = min(a.txn_open, left)
                portion = carrying_portion(a, take)                                    # cumulatively rounded share of carrying
                at_spot = to_functional(take, txn, fn, rate(txn, fn, "spot", on=fl.date))
                moves.append(remeasure(a, at_spot - portion, kind="ASSET_LAYER_REMEASURED", reason="SETTLEMENT"))
                moves.append(settle(a, take, at_spot))                                 # ASSET_LAYER_SETTLED
                left -= take
                if left == 0: break
            if left:
                rv = rate(txn, fn, "spot", on=layer_date(fl))                          # POL-160
                moves.append(create("CONTRACT_LIABILITY", left, to_functional(left, txn, fn, rv), rv, fl))
        else:                                  # DEBIT: revenue relief, refund liability or contra increase, interest income, credit memo
            left = fl.amount
            for L in consumption_order(open_layers("CONTRACT_LIABILITY")):             # POL-161 FIFO | PRO_RATA
                take = allocation_for(L, left)                                         # FIFO: min(L.txn_open, left)
                relief = relieve(L, take)                                              # S12-R-05
                moves.append(relief)
                left -= take
                if left == 0: break
            if left:
                rv = debit_remainder_rate(fl)                                          # POL-162, S12-R-06
                moves.append(create("CONTRACT_ASSET", left, to_functional(left, txn, fn, rv), rv, fl))
    return moves

MONETARY_ROLES = ("REFUND_LIABILITY", "DEPOSIT_LIABILITY", "CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE")      # D-25b; POL-164

def process_monetary_liability_flows(flows: Sequence[MonetaryFlow], fn: str, txn: str) -> list[LayerMovement]:
    """Monetary-liability layers per (group, contracting entity, book), in every book (S12-R-19 to S12-R-21)."""
    moves = []
    for fl in flows:                           # time-driven releases first, then ENG-06 order (ALG-08 §2.9.1)
        spot = rate(txn, fn, "spot", on=fl.date)
        at_spot = to_functional(fl.amount, txn, fn, spot)
        if fl.kind == "INCREASE":              # JET-04b or JET-05c component increase, JET-01b receipt, JET-14 promise
            moves.append(create(fl.role, fl.amount, at_spot, spot, fl))                       # LIABILITY_LAYER_CREATED
            if fl.cl_relief_functional is not None:                                          # JET-04b: control role debited
                diff = at_spot - fl.cl_relief_functional                                     # historical relief (+ asset layer)
                if diff:
                    moves.append(jet_10d(fl, diff, reason="RECOGNITION"))                   # S12-R-19
        else:                                  # "DECREASE": settlement or estimate-driven decrease (S12-R-20)
            left = fl.amount
            for L in fifo(open_layers(fl.role, component=fl.component_key)):
                take = min(L.txn_open, left)
                portion = carrying_portion(L, take)                                          # cumulative_posted, f = take ÷ open
                settled_at_spot = to_functional(take, txn, fn, spot)
                if settled_at_spot != portion:
                    moves.append(remeasure(L, settled_at_spot - portion, kind="LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED", reason="SETTLEMENT"))
                moves.append(consume(L, take, settled_at_spot))                              # LIABILITY_LAYER_CONSUMED at spot
                left -= take
                if left == 0: break
    return moves
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-03 | **Flows.** Credits to the control role are unconditional billing (or receipt, whichever is earlier), deposit transfers, noncash consideration becoming unconditional, advance-payment interest (JET-11b), refund-liability releases and negative revenue. Debits are revenue relief (JET-02, 04a, 05a, 08, 13 contracting side), refund-liability and receivable-contra increases, deferred-payment interest (JET-11a) and credit memos. Both mirror S10-INV-05, so Σ open liability layers − Σ open asset layers = NP in transaction currency (S12-INV-03). **Opening baseline (rev 1.12; S07-R-05).** The stage 07 opening baselines of a contract enter the control role at their cutover date D as one flow per `<contract>@<entity>`: the ERP's cumulative billing Σ baseline `billed_cum` less the ledger's in-position lines dated on or before D (already flowed line by line), a `BILLING` credit when positive and a `CREDIT_MEMO` debit when negative, with `record_seq` None (before the events of D) and source `<contract>@<entity>@<D>/opening_billed_cum`; it creates the opening liability layer at the cutover-date spot and posts nothing (S14-R-26); there is no flow under `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` when the ledger holds `ENGINE`-mode lines of the contract dated on or before D (on the S10-R-03 date), the baseline then not being counted in B_u (S10-R-08 rev 1.16) |
| S12-R-04 | **Layer date (POL-160).** `EARLIER_OF_RECEIPT_AND_UNCONDITIONAL_DUE`: the layer of an invoice is dated the earlier of its unconditional date (S10-R-06) and the first receipt applied to it; the later of the two creates nothing. `INVOICE_ISSUE_DATE` (ASC606 book only): the invoice issue date; for an `ENGINE`-mode cancellable line the election's issue date is the S10-R-06 date on which the line becomes billing (a receipt applied or a same-amount noncancellable update), because a layer cannot predate the contract liability it represents (S12-INV-03); ERP-mode lines and noncancellable ENGINE lines keep the invoice issue date (D-92 (5); flagged AD-29 for G12). In the IFRS15 book the first option is forced and each advance is a separate layer (IFRIC 22.8, 22.9). **Unconditional due and the period of entry (rev 1.83; D-87 L6-5-Q-14; supervisor ruling R-81 of 2026-09-30, to be transcribed as D-100; candidate AD-51, pending the independent accountant).** Under the first option the "unconditional due" date of an `ERP`-mode line is its payload `due_date` when present, else its S10-R-06 date (606-10-55-284, noncancellable); an `ENGINE`-mode line keeps its S10-R-06 date; the first receipt is the earliest `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` that names the invoice (D-87 L6-5-Q-14, which this row had not carried). The billing credit of a line is processed on its layer date (POLICIES ALG-08 §2.9.1: the event date of a billing is its POL-160 date), while S10-R-06 alone decides when the line enters the position. The layer date therefore may not leave the accounting period of the contracting entity in which the line enters the position: a date in a later period — an invoice neither due nor paid by the end of its issue period — gives the end of that period; a date in an earlier period — a receipt that names an invoice issued in a later period, or a later line of an invoice already paid — gives the line's S10-R-06 date; a date inside the period is unchanged. At every period end the layers then hold exactly the lines the position counts — no layer stands at a period end before the contract liability it represents (D-92 (5)), and none is missing behind a liability the period end presents — so S12-INV-03 holds whatever the payment terms; before rev 1.83 a period end between the two dates failed S12-INV-03 and the computation of the group was refused then and at every later computation. An invoice due beyond the end of its issue period is measured exactly as one due on that period end; a same-currency figure does not depend on the layer date. The two alternatives the accountant may prefer — the S10-R-06 date whenever the POL-160 date lies outside the period, or a pending element converted into the layer on the due or receipt date — are recorded with the ruling; this rule closes neither |
| S12-R-05 | **Relief.** A liability layer relieved by `take` records functional relief = `cumulative_posted`(fn_original ÷ 10^μ_fn, fn_original, consumed_cum ÷ txn_original) − previous cumulative relief of the layer. A fully consumed layer ends at functional 0 (PROP:P12). Functional revenue of a flow = the sum of its reliefs plus any asset-layer creation |
| S12-R-06 | **Remainder rate (POL-162).** Revenue recognised beyond the available liability creates an asset layer at the average rate of the flow's period (`PERIOD_AVERAGE`) or at spot on the flow date (`TRANSACTION_DATE_SPOT`). A credit memo, or a refund-liability increase (D-25b; POLICIES ALG-08 §2.9.1 rev 1.2), beyond the available liability creates an asset layer at spot on the event date |
| S12-R-07 | **POL-161 `PRO_RATA`.** The debit is apportioned over open liability layers by open transaction balance (ALG-01 §2.1.2, keys = layer keys), then each share is relieved as in S12-R-05 |
| S12-R-08 | **Credit-memo settlement difference (JET-10c; POLICIES rev 1.2; OQ-B-14 resolved by D-76).** A credit memo relieves contract-liability layers at historical carrying, while the billing system (`ERP` mode) or JET-03 (`ENGINE` mode) debits the liability at spot. Stage 12 publishes the difference round(m × spot) − historical relief as a JET-10c target (Dr `FX_GAIN_LOSS` / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` when positive, swapped when negative) so the GL contract liability ties to the layers. Under `ENGINE` mode the JET-03 credit memo debits `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` at the historical relief and credits `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE` at spot, and the same difference posts as an `FX_GAIN_LOSS` line of that entry |
| S12-R-19 | **Monetary-liability layers at recognition (D-25b; POL-164; ALG-08 §2.9.1).** In every book, when the transaction currency differs from the contracting entity's functional currency, each increase of a refund-liability component (JET-04b, JET-05c, including an estimate-driven increase), each deposit receipt (JET-01b) and each consideration-payable promise (JET-14) creates a layer with `balance_role` = its role, key `<balance role>:<global event key>`, at spot on the recognition date (`LIABILITY_LAYER_CREATED`). When the recognition debits the control role (JET-04b), the contract-liability relief is at historical carrying (S12-R-05) or creates an asset layer (S12-R-06), and the difference round(amount × spot) − that functional debit posts by JET-10d on the recognition date. Customer incentive assets, return assets and noncash consideration assets are nonmonetary and have no layer (POL-164) |
| S12-R-20 | **Settlement and decrease (D-25b; ALG-08 §2.9.1).** The portion of a monetary layer that leaves the liability is remeasured to spot on that date before it is relieved: the settled carrying share = `cumulative_posted`(fn carrying ÷ 10^μ_fn, fn carrying, settled ÷ open before the settlement), and round(settled × spot) − that share posts by JET-10d (`LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED`, reason `SETTLEMENT`); the portion is then consumed at spot (`LIABILITY_LAYER_CONSUMED`). Settlements are a credit memo or refund consuming a component, return-window expiry, `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET`, a deposit refund on termination and 606-10-25-7 derecognition. An estimate-driven decrease of a `RETURN` or `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` component is relieved in the same way. A release to the control role (JET-04b credit, JET-01b criteria met) enters S12-R-03 as a credit flow at the release carrying, Σ per-layer round(take × spot) of the layers it consumes; it settles open contract-asset layers first, and the contract-liability layer it then creates takes that carrying less the asset settlements, so GL and layers tie with no residue (D-87 L6-5-Q-26; D-88 L7-6-Q-2; D-89b), so a deposit transferred at criteria met creates a contract-liability layer dated the transfer date at spot (IFRIC 22.8), whatever S12-R-04 gives for invoices. Flows of one date are processed with time-driven releases first, then events in ENG-06 order |
| S12-R-21 | **Consideration payable (D-25b; POLICIES rev 1.2 ALG-08 §2.9.1).** 04 rev 1.2 records no settlement of a consideration-payable promise, because accounts payable in the ERP settles it (POLICIES JET-14). A consideration-payable layer therefore stays open and is remeasured at every period end (S12-R-11). The release of the customer incentive asset against revenue (JET-14) is not a settlement of the payable |

#### 12.2.3 Period-end remeasurement (POL-163, POL-164; JET-10a, JET-10a′, JET-10b, JET-10d; CLOSE_RELEASE pass `FX_REMEASUREMENT`)

```python
def remeasure_period_end(state, period, fn, txn) -> list[LayerMovement]:
    closing = rate(txn, fn, "closing", period=period)
    moves = []
    for a in open_layers("CONTRACT_ASSET"):                                           # POL-164, FORCED
        target = to_functional(a.txn_open, txn, fn, closing)
        if target != a.fn_carrying:
            moves.append(remeasure(a, target - a.fn_carrying, kind="ASSET_LAYER_REMEASURED", reason="PERIOD_END"))
    for role in MONETARY_ROLES:                                                       # D-25b, every book, JET-10d
        for L in open_layers(role):
            target = to_functional(L.txn_open, txn, fn, closing)
            if target != L.fn_carrying:
                moves.append(remeasure(L, target - L.fn_carrying, kind="LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED", reason="PERIOD_END"))
    if book == "ASC606" and ctx.policies.value("fx.cl_historical_layering", contract=contract) == "DISABLED_REMEASURE_AS_MONETARY":
        for L in open_layers("CONTRACT_LIABILITY"):                                   # D-25a override, JET-10b
            target = to_functional(L.txn_open, txn, fn, closing)
            if target != L.fn_carrying:
                moves.append(remeasure(L, target - L.fn_carrying, kind="LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED", reason="PERIOD_END"))
    if billing_posting == "ENGINE":
        for inv in open_ar(state):                                                    # JET-10a′
            target = to_functional(inv.open_txn, txn, fn, closing)
            post_ar_remeasurement(inv, target - inv.fn_carrying)
    return moves
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-09 | Asset positions are remeasured to the closing rate at every period end, non-reversing, against `FX_GAIN_LOSS` (JET-10a). The JET-06 reclass of the period uses the remeasured functional carrying of the asset layers, apportioned over the obligation attributions of §10.2.7 by largest remainder |
| S12-R-10 | Liability layers are never remeasured in the IFRS15 book, and in the ASC606 book only under the approved contract-level POL-163 override for refundable advance consideration (D-25a; CHK-082). Under the override a relief uses the remeasured carrying (S12-R-05 over `fn_carrying`) |
| S12-R-11 | **Monetary liabilities (rev 1.2; D-25b; OQ-B-15 resolved by D-76).** Refund liabilities, deposit liabilities and consideration payable to a customer denominated in a currency other than the contracting entity's functional currency are monetary items. In every book they are remeasured to the closing rate at every period end and to spot at settlement, non-reversing, against `FX_GAIN_LOSS` (JET-10d; POL-164 `ASSET_POSITIONS_ENGINE_AR_AND_MONETARY_LIABILITIES`; ASC 830-20-35-1; IAS 21.16, 21.23(a); CHK-084). Layers are created by S12-R-19, settled by S12-R-20, and consideration payable follows S12-R-21. Contract liabilities stay nonmonetary except under the S12-R-10 override (D-25, D-25a). Intercompany balances are not remeasured by the engine (ALG-07 step 5) |
| S12-R-12 | **Movement kinds (amended in rev 1.2).** The E-86 literals of T-CON-18 cover creation, consumption, settlement and remeasurement. Every liability remeasurement, under S12-R-10 (`CONTRACT_LIABILITY`) or under S12-R-11 and S12-R-20 (`REFUND_LIABILITY`, `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY`, `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`), is stored with `movement_kind = LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED` and the layer's `balance_role` (04 E-86 and `ck_fx_layer_movement__liability_kinds`, rev 1.2; OQ-B-14 resolved by D-76). The rev 1.0 interim storage as `ASSET_LAYER_REMEASURED` is withdrawn |

#### 12.2.4 Late events (POL-181; ALG-09 step 5)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-13 | The replay converts every flow at the rates of its own effective date and period. Period-end remeasurements are evaluated for every period through the horizon; stage 14 posts the cumulative difference into the first open period with `origin_period` (RCP-04, RCP-06). A locked period's remeasurement and reclass are never re-reversed; the reclass of the posting period is recomputed. **A unit after the horizon (rev 1.100; C-05).** A unit whose replay would begin after the horizon — the group's inception lies after it and no flow is dated inside it — has no period to close: stage 12 processes nothing for it, no layer, remeasurement or functional amount |

#### 12.2.5 Performing and contracting entities (D-23; ALG-07; POL-170, POL-171; JET-13)

```python
def ic_pair(o: ObligationState, flow: RevenueFlow, reliefs_fn_ec: int) -> IcPair | None:
    if o.performing_entity == o.contracting_entity:
        return None
    if ctx.policies.value("ic.revenue_entity", entity=o.contracting_entity) == "CONTRACTING_ENTITY":
        return None                                                     # JET-02 in the contracting entity
    fn_p = functional_currency(o.performing_entity)
    period_p = period_of(flow.date, entity=o.performing_entity)          # performing entity's calendar (C-04)
    rv = debit_remainder_rate_for(fn_p, flow, period_p)                  # POL-162 rate for the performing entity
    return IcPair(obligation=o.subject_key, txn_amount=flow.amount,
                  ec_functional=reliefs_fn_ec,                           # layer relief in the contracting entity (ALG-07 step 5)
                  ep_functional=to_functional(flow.amount, txn, fn_p, rv),
                  ec_period=period_of(flow.date, entity=o.contracting_entity), ep_period=period_p)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-14 | Allocation spans the group across entities in the transaction currency (ALG-07 step 1). Balances, netting and layers belong to the contracting entity; the performing entity holds no contract balances (POL-120; ALG-07 step 3) |
| S12-R-15 | Under POL-170 `PERFORMING_ENTITY`, revenue of an obligation performed by another entity posts in the performing entity through JET-13: the contracting entity relieves its liability against `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_TO` (counterparty = performing entity) at the layer relief; the performing entity books `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_FROM` (counterparty = contracting entity) against `REVENUE` at its own POL-162 rate. The pair carries the revenue amount in transaction currency (POL-171) |
| S12-R-16 | Each entity's entry balances on its own in both currencies (D-16). Due-to and due-from agree in transaction currency per entity pair and period, so the unmatched balance of report `intercompany_pairs` is 0 (REQ-ENT-005; PRD WLD-X-14). Eliminations belong to consolidation (ASC 810-10-45-1) |
| S12-R-17 | Revenue schedule lines carry the performing entity (T-ENG-02), in that entity's calendar; contract-balance targets carry the contracting entity in its calendar |

#### 12.2.6 Reporting currency

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S12-R-18 | Reporting-currency views are platform projections: balances at the closing rate, flows at the average rate, and a translation line in rollforwards (research 06 §9.6; REQ-FX-005). The engine posts no translation adjustments. When a summarised batch is converted to another currency, the residue posts to `ROUNDING` in that batch (§14.3.5; D-16) |

### 12.3 Invariants

| Id | Invariant | Check |
|---|---|---|
| S12-INV-01 | A liability layer's `fn_original` never changes after creation (outside the S12-R-10 override); a fully consumed layer has functional 0 | PROP:P12 |
| S12-INV-02 | No remeasurement targets `REVENUE` | PROP:P12 |
| S12-INV-03 | Σ open `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` layers (txn) − Σ open asset layers (txn) = NP (txn) per (c, e, b, t); monetary-liability layers are outside NP | DG-ENG-05 |
| S12-INV-04 | After period-end remeasurement, each open asset layer's carrying = round(txn open × closing rate) | unit test |
| S12-INV-05 | Every entry derived from stage 12 amounts balances per entity in transaction and functional currency | DB-06; PROP:P5 |
| S12-INV-06 | IFRS15: no remeasurement movement of a `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` layer exists (amended in rev 1.2: monetary-liability layers are remeasured in every book, D-25b) | POL-163 FORCED |
| S12-INV-07 | For every (contracting entity, performing entity, period): Σ due-to txn = Σ due-from txn | REQ-ENT-005 |
| S12-INV-08 | For each monetary-liability role and (c, e, b, t): Σ open layers (txn) = the open balance of that role (refund-liability components, deposit liability, consideration payable); after period-end remeasurement each open layer's carrying = round(txn open × closing rate); a fully consumed layer ends at functional 0 (rev 1.2; D-25b) | unit test; CHK-084 |

### 12.4 Findings and codes

| Code (04 §15.4) | Severity | Condition |
|---|---|---|
| `FX_RATE_MISSING` | ERROR | S12-R-01 |
| `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` | ERROR | S12-INV-01, 03, 05, 07 |

`DQ_NEGATIVE_LIABILITY_LAYER` is a platform monitor over T-CON-18; S12-INV-03 and the flow model make a negative open layer impossible in engine output.

### 12.7 Worked examples

**EX-12-A. Layers, an asset layer, remeasurement, settlement and intercompany pairs (own).** Contract in EUR. Contracting entity US (functional USD); services obligation S (EUR 3,000.00, Jan to Mar, `MONTHLY_EVEN`) performed by UK (functional GBP); licence obligation L (EUR 1,500.00, point in time on 15 Mar) performed by US. Both obligations are conditional (§10.2.7). `ERP` billing mode; POL-160 default; POL-161 `FIFO`; POL-162 `PERIOD_AVERAGE`.

| Date | Flow | US (USD) | UK (GBP) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Jan | Invoice EUR 3,000.00, noncancellable, spot 1.123456 | Liability layer L1: EUR 3,000.00 / USD 3,370.37 | |
| 31 Jan | S revenue EUR 1,000.00 | L1 relief round(3,370.37 × 1/3) = 1,123.46; JET-13 Dr CL 1,123.46 / Cr IC_DUE_TO (UK) 1,123.46 | EUR/GBP average 0.8612: Dr IC_DUE_FROM (US) 861.20 / Cr REVENUE 861.20 |
| 28 Feb | S revenue EUR 1,000.00 | cumulative relief round(3,370.37 × 2/3) = 2,246.91, period 1,123.45 | average 0.8655: 865.50 |
| 15 Mar | L revenue EUR 1,500.00 | L1 remainder EUR 1,000.00 relieved to completion: 1,123.46; asset layer A1 EUR 500.00 at March average 1.1350 = 567.50; licence revenue USD 1,690.96 | |
| 31 Mar | S revenue EUR 1,000.00 | No liability layer left: asset layer A2 EUR 1,000.00 at 1.1350 = 1,135.00; JET-13 Dr CL 1,135.00 / Cr IC_DUE_TO (UK) 1,135.00 | average 0.8700: 870.00 |
| 31 Mar close | Closing 1.1400 | A1 + A2 EUR 1,500.00: carrying 1,702.50 → 1,710.00; JET-10a Dr CL 7.50 / Cr FX_GAIN_LOSS 7.50; JET-06 Dr CONTRACT_ASSET 1,710.00 / Cr CL 1,710.00 (reversed 1 Apr) | |
| 20 Apr | Invoice EUR 1,500.00 at spot 1.1450 | Settles A1 and A2: at spot 1,717.50 − carrying 1,710.00 = JET-10a settlement Dr CL 7.50 / Cr FX_GAIN_LOSS 7.50; no liability layer; position 0.00 | |

Totals: US revenue USD 1,690.96; US FX gain USD 15.00; intercompany pairs EUR 3,000.00 (US USD 1,123.46 / 1,123.45 / 1,135.00); UK revenue GBP 2,596.70. Every entry balances in each entity's functional currency.

**EX-12-B. Monetary liabilities (CHK-084; D-25b; functional USD, contract EUR, `ERP` mode; the same figures in every book).** Refund liability of a returnable sale (CHK-084 (a)):

| Date | Flow | Stage 12 movements | JET-10d |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Mar 2026 | 100 units transfer for EUR 10,000.00 received the same day at spot 1.1000; E = 3 at p_ref 100.00 (k = c_rec, so no return asset) | CL layer 11,000.00; revenue EUR 9,700.00 relieves 10,670.00; the refund-liability increase EUR 300.00 relieves the remaining 330.00 of the CL layer and creates a `REFUND_LIABILITY` layer of 330.00 at spot (S12-R-19) | none (spot equals the historical relief) |
| 31 Mar | Closing 1.1200 | layer remeasured to 336.00 (`LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED`) | loss 6.00 |
| 15 Apr | Two units returned and credited, EUR 200.00 at spot 1.1500 | settled carrying share `cumulative_posted`(336.00, 33,600, 2/3) = 224.00, remeasured to 230.00 and consumed at 230.00 (S12-R-20); the ERP credit memo debits CL 230.00 and the JET-04b release credits CL 230.00; carrying 112.00 for EUR 100.00 | loss 6.00 |
| 30 Apr | Closing 1.0800 | layer remeasured to 108.00 | gain 4.00 |
| 31 May | Window expiry; spot, average and closing 1.1000 | settled share 108.00 remeasured to 110.00 and consumed; JET-04b release 110.00; JET-04a revenue 110.00 | loss 2.00 |

Totals: revenue USD 10,780.00; net FX loss 10.00; refund liability 0.00; contract liability 0.00. Deposit liability (CHK-084 (b)): EUR 1,000.00 received on 10 January 2026 at 1.1000 creates a `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` layer of 1,100.00; closing 1.1200 on 31 January gives a loss of 20.00; `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET` on 15 February at spot 1.1300 gives a loss of 10.00, the layer is consumed at 1,130.00, and a contract-liability layer of EUR 1,000.00 at 1,130.00 dated 15 February is created (S12-R-20). Consideration payable (CHK-084 (c)): EUR 50,000.00 promised on 1 June 2026 at 1.1000 creates a `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE` layer of 55,000.00; unsettled at 30 June, closing 1.0900 gives a gain of 500.00; the customer incentive asset stays at 55,000.00 (S12-R-19, S12-R-21).

Published checks: CHK-080 (two advances, layers), CHK-081 (arrears billing, remeasurement and settlement), CHK-082 (POL-163 override), CHK-083 (ENGINE-mode AR), CHK-084 (monetary liabilities, D-25b), CHK-070 and CHK-071 (intercompany pairs, per-entity balances).

## 13. Stage 13: books

Stage 13 runs the pipeline once per enabled book inside one `compute` call, reuses the output of a stage when two books have equal stage keys (research 06 D11; RCP-11 to RCP-14), and folds the `LEGACY` book for delta posting (D-24, D-34). It is the orchestration around stages 02 to 12, 14 and 15, called by `compute` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.3).

### 13.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Source | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `CanonicalBundle` (`cb.books`, `cb.entities`, `cb.events`) | stage 01 | Books per entity, resolved policies per book, period states per book, events |
| `STAGES` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.10) | engine constant | Ordered stage specs with entry functions, declared policy keys and formula ids |
| `STAGE_POLICY_KEYS`, `STAGE_CONTEXT_MEMBERS` | `s13_books/memo.py` | Stage-key contents (§13.2.2) |
| Resolved POL-005, POL-007, POL-008 | `BookInput.policies` | Book set, delta mode, LEGACY source |

| Output | Content | Consumer |
|---|---|---|
| `BookResult` per book in RCP-11 order | The final state of stages 02 to 12, 14, 15 for `ASC606` and `IFRS15`; the LEGACY fold for `LEGACY` | `compute` assembles `OutputBundle.books` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.5) |
| Per-book `Trace` | Nodes of the book, with alias inputs for memoised stages (CV-55) | persistence (T-ENG-03) |
| `pre_standard_revenue` targets | Per obligation and period: cumulative pre-standard revenue from the LEGACY fold | primary book `obligation_measures` (T-CON-11 `pre_standard_revenue_*`), stage 14 LEGACY intents |

### 13.2 Pseudocode

#### 13.2.1 Book loop

```python
BOOK_ORDER = ("ASC606", "IFRS15", "LEGACY")                      # RCP-11

def run_books(cb: CanonicalBundle, stages: Sequence[StageSpec]) -> tuple[BookResult, ...]:
    memo: dict[str, MemoEntry] = {}                               # lives only inside this call (RCP-13; DG-ENG-08)
    results = []
    legacy = None
    for book in sorted(cb.books.values(), key=lambda b: BOOK_ORDER.index(b.book_code)):
        ctx = book_context(cb, book)                              # BookContext (§0.5)
        tb = TraceBuilder(engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION)
        if book.book_code == "LEGACY":
            legacy = legacy_book.run(ctx, cb, tb)                  # §13.2.4
            results.append(BookResult(book="LEGACY", state=legacy, trace=tb.build(root_measures=legacy.roots)))
            continue
        state, prev_key = None, "root"
        for spec in stages:                                        # stages 02 to 12, 14, 15 in STAGES order
            key = stage_key(spec, prev_key, ctx)
            if key in memo:
                state = memo[key].output
                tb.alias(memo[key].node_ids)                       # inputs = [{"alias_of": <node id>}] (RCP-12; CV-55)
            else:
                before = tb.node_ids()
                state = spec.run(ctx, state if state is not None else cb, tb)
                memo[key] = MemoEntry(output=state, node_ids=tb.node_ids() - before)
            prev_key = key
        results.append(BookResult(book=book.book_code, state=state, trace=tb.build(root_measures=roots(state))))
    if legacy is not None:
        attach_pre_standard(results, legacy)                       # T-CON-11 pre_standard_revenue_* on the primary book (RCP-14)
    return tuple(results)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S13-R-01 | Books run in the order `ASC606`, `IFRS15`, `LEGACY`, for the entities that keep each book (`BookInput.entity_codes`; POL-007). A book whose entity set is empty produces no result |
| S13-R-02 | Memoisation lives only inside one call and is keyed by stage keys; nothing is cached across computations (RCP-13). A stage output is an immutable frozen value, so reuse cannot leak state between books |
| S13-R-03 | Findings of a memoised stage are reported once per book that uses the output, with the book code in `Diagnostic.book_code` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.5) |

#### 13.2.2 Stage keys (RCP-12)

```python
def stage_key(spec: StageSpec, prev_key: str, ctx: BookContext) -> str:
    policies = sorted(
        (p.code, p.scope, p.subject_key, canonical.canonical_bytes(p.value).decode(), p.level)
        for p in ctx.policies.all() if p.code in STAGE_POLICY_KEYS[spec.stage])
    context = {m: STAGE_CONTEXT_MEMBERS_VIEW[m](ctx) for m in sorted(STAGE_CONTEXT_MEMBERS[spec.stage])}
    return canonical.sha256_hex({"stage": spec.stage, "formulas": sorted(spec.formula_ids),
                                 "previous": prev_key, "policies": policies, "context": context})
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S13-R-04 | The stage key covers the stage id, its registered formula ids, the previous stage's key, the resolved values of the declared policy keys, and the book-dependent context members the stage reads. [J] The previous stage's key identifies its output because every stage is a pure function of the previous output, the policies and the context; hashing the key chain is equivalent to hashing canonical outputs (RCP-12) and costs nothing |
| S13-R-05 | `STAGE_CONTEXT_MEMBERS` names the non-policy members of `BookContext` a stage reads: `book_code` for stages that filter events by book (stage 02 collectibility and criteria events); `horizon` and entity period states for stages 09 to 12 and 15; `mapping` for stage 14. `ENGINE_SPEC.md` declares the entries of stages 02 to 08; this file declares 09 to 15 (Table 13-A) |

Table 13-A: `STAGE_POLICY_KEYS` and `STAGE_CONTEXT_MEMBERS` for stages 09 to 15

| Stage | Policy keys (POLICIES §1, §5.0) | Context members |
|---|---|---|

|---|---|
| S13-R-06 | A switch is a resolved policy value in the book's `BookInput.policies`; no stage branches on `book_code` except to read a FORCED value the orchestrator already supplies, and the three `book_code` context members of Table 13-A (DG-ENG-09) |

#### 13.2.4 LEGACY book and delta posting (POL-005, POL-007, POL-008; JET-15; RCP-14)

```python
def legacy_book_run(ctx: BookContext, cb: CanonicalBundle, tb: TraceBuilder) -> LegacyState:
    source = ctx.policies.value("legacy_book.source")                 # PRE_STANDARD_EVENTS | ERP_REVENUE_FEED
    targets: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}
    for ob_key in sorted(all_obligation_keys(cb)):
        running = 0
        for period in periods_through_horizon(owner_entity(ob_key)):
            running = sum(e.payload["amount"] for e in cb.events
                          if e.event_type == "PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED"
                          and ob_key in e.obligation_subject_keys and e.effective_date <= period.end)
            targets[(ob_key, period.key)] = running
            tb.node(measure="pre_standard_revenue_cum", subject_key=ob_key, period_key=period.key, value=running,
                    currency=ctx.txn_currency, minor_unit=mu, formula_id="books.legacy_fold.v1",
                    inputs=event_refs(cb, ob_key, period), narrative_key="books.legacy_fold")
    return LegacyState(pre_standard_revenue=targets, intents=legacy_intents(ctx, targets))
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S13-R-07 | The LEGACY book folds only `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED` events and never runs stages 02 to 12 (RCP-14). Under `ERP_REVENUE_FEED` the adapter ingests the ERP's pre-standard revenue lines as the same event type with `origin = ADAPTER`; under `PRE_STANDARD_EVENTS` they come from progress uploads (legacy template column F). Modification, price-change and estimate events carry no pre-standard amount (DEV-050; GT-23) |
| S13-R-08 | LEGACY subledger intents reverse the pre-standard revenue the ERP booked: entry kind `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE`, Dr `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE` (revenue string of the obligation) / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` (contract string), amount = the period change of L_p, in the obligation's contracting entity (D-89 L7-6-Q-8). They post to the `LEGACY` book's subledger (T-SL-01 `book_code = LEGACY`). **Posting period (rev 1.103; supervisor rulings R-112 (e) and R-114 (d)).** A LEGACY intent is assigned by S08-R-08 from the LEGACY states of `EntityInput.periods`. Bundle assembly hands those states (05 RCP-15 rev 1.94): the LEGACY book has no close of its own and follows the primary book's, so for a period whose primary-book state is not postable while the LEGACY row's is, the state handed is the primary's. The period change of L_p dated in a period the primary book has locked therefore posts in the first later period postable in both books, with the dated period as origin. The engine reads the states it is handed: no engine change and no figure change |
| S13-R-09 | A journal run with `mode = DELTA` (POL-005; T-SL-06) reads the primary book's seal range and the LEGACY range and outputs primary lines plus LEGACY lines per grouping key, with no negation (JET-15: "delta lines = primary-book lines + LEGACY-book lines per role"; §14.3.4; D-89 L7-6-Q-8). With `GROSS` the LEGACY range is not read, but the adjustment-format legacy report is still produced (POL-005 parity note) |
| S13-R-10 | `pre_standard_revenue_cum` and `pre_standard_revenue_amount` of the primary book's obligation versions are the LEGACY targets at the version date; the cumulative rolls on every version (REQ-BK-005; DEV-051) |
| S13-R-11 | The LEGACY `BookResult` carries no contract version, obligation versions, balances or schedules; only posting intents and trace. `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.5 makes `BookOutput.contract_version` optional (`None` for `LEGACY`) (OQ-B-16 resolved by D-76) |

### 13.7 Worked examples

**EX-13-A. Delta posting (own; JET-15).** In one period an obligation recognises 1,000.00 in the ASC606 book, while the ERP booked pre-standard revenue of 1,200.00 (LEGACY line Dr `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE` 1,200.00 / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 1,200.00, the reversal of what the ERP booked; D-89 L7-6-Q-8). Primary lines: Dr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 1,000.00 / Cr `REVENUE` 1,000.00. DELTA journal = primary + LEGACY: Dr `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE` 1,200.00; Cr `REVENUE` 1,000.00; Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 200.00. Debits equal credits.

**EX-13-B. Stage keys when only the cost-impairment reversal differs (CHK-131).** An entity keeps `ASC606` and `IFRS15` with identical resolved values for every key of stages 02 to 10; POL-144 is `PROHIBITED` in ASC606 and `REQUIRED_CAPPED` in IFRS15 (FORCED). Stage keys of stages 02 to 10 are equal, so the IFRS15 book aliases their nodes. Stage 11's keys differ (policy `costs.impairment_reversal` and context `book_code`), so stages 11, 12, 14 and 15 run again for IFRS15. The Year-2 reversal of 10,000.00 exists only in the IFRS15 book (EX-11-A).

Published check: CHK-020 (golden GT-07 adjustment journal: debits 240.22 = credits 240.22).

---

## 14. Stage 14: journal derivation

Stage 14 turns the cumulative targets of stages 02 to 13 into balanced posting intents: JET templates to subledger lines with account roles, clearing purposes and counterparties; posting-period assignment with origin tagging; separation of event-driven and time-driven amounts; account resolution; deterministic keys and ordering. The platform part summarises sealed subledger lines into GL journals at the POL-006 grain.

### 14.1 Inputs and outputs

| Input | Produced by | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `RecognitionState`, `BalanceState`, `CostLossState`, `FxState`, LEGACY targets | stages 09 to 13 | Cumulative targets per template part (Table 14-A) |
| `specialist_targets` (deposits, 25-7 revenue, financing accretion, warranty accruals, noncash) and `BalanceState.customer_consideration` (consideration payable; S10-R-26) | stages 02, 04 and 10 | JET-01b, JET-11, JET-14, JET-16, JET-17 targets |
| Approved manual adjustments of kinds `MANUAL_JOURNAL`, `ACCOUNT_RECLASS` (T-SL-05 payload lines) | bundle events `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_APPLIED`, whose engine payload carries the approved row (04 §16.3 rev 1.120) | Role targets of entry kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`: lines posted as approved (S14-R-09a) |
| `posted` (`PostedAmountInput`) | bundle (RCP-05) | Posted cumulative amounts to compare against targets |
| Period states per (entity, book); `s08_estimates_late_events.assign_posting_period` | bundle; stage 08 | Posting-period assignment (RCP-04; ALG-09) |
| Account mapping (`BookInput.account_mapping`), obligation `account_overrides`, template `account_role_overrides`, dimensions | bundle; stage 03 | Account resolution (T-REF-15 steps 1 to 4) |
| Resolved POL-004, POL-005, POL-006, POL-022, POL-030, POL-180, POL-181 | `BookContext.policies` | Modes |
| `ctx.trigger` (E-87 or `DRY_RUN`) and, for close passes, the pass name (E-31 `CLOSE_RELEASE`, `FX_REMEASUREMENT`, `NETTING_RECLASS`) | bundle | Which amount classes post (§14.2.3) |

| Output (`PostingState`) | Content | Consumers |
|---|---|---|
| `posting_intents` | `PostingIntent` per entry: book, entity, posting period, origin period, effective date, entry kind (E-29), posting class, reason code, ordered lines | orchestrator persistence (T-SL-01, T-SL-04); `OutputBundle.books[].posting_intents` |
| `line_rates` (rev 1.50) | Line key → the rate references behind the functional amount of a line whose transaction currency is not its functional currency (S14-R-28) | orchestrator persistence (T-SL-04 `fx_rate_id`, `fx_rate_set_version_id`, `fx_rate`; D-88 L7-6-Q-1); `OutputBundle.books[].line_rates` |
| `findings` | `ACCOUNT_MAPPING_MISSING` and invariant findings | orchestrator |
| Platform: `journal_run`, `journal_batch`, `journal_entry`, `journal_line` | §14.3 | GL export, reports |

### 14.2 Pseudocode (engine)

#### 14.2.1 Template parts (`JET_PARTS`, `COUNTER_ROLES`, `AMOUNT_CLASSES`)

Table 14-A: template parts, their targets and classes. "Class" is the `AMOUNT_CLASSES` value (RCP-07): `EVENT` amounts post at compute; `TIME` amounts post in the named close pass.

| Part | Cumulative target (producer) | Entry kind (E-29) | Debit role | Credit role(s) | Owner entity; subject | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| JET-01b receipt | deposits received (02) | `DEPOSIT` | `BILLING_CLEARING` (`UNAPPLIED_CASH`) | `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| JET-01b criteria met | deposit transferred (02) | `DEPOSIT` | `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| JET-01b 25-7 revenue | deposit revenue (02): `deposit_to_revenue_cum` attributed to the contract's `IN_SCOPE_606` obligations (S14-R-25) | `REVENUE_RECOGNITION` | `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` | `REVENUE` | contracting; obligation | `EVENT` for a recognition at an event; `TIME` (`CLOSE_RELEASE`) for `deposit_to_revenue_time_cum`, the recognitions at dated points ((a) at the term end, (c) at `event_c_met_on`) (D-91; rev pending END-4b: 1.0-rc binds no part and fails closed) |
| JET-01b refund | deposit refunded (02) | `DEPOSIT` | `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` | `BILLING_CLEARING` (`UNAPPLIED_CASH`) | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| JET-02 principal, JET-04a, JET-05a, JET-05b, JET-08 expiry | revenue relief by cause (10, from 09): relief = `revenue_cum` + concession components; stage 09 targets are gross of consideration payable (S04-R-02) and exclude 25-7 deposit revenue, which JET-01b posts (V4). Revenue equal to the cost of uninstalled materials (S09-R-14) is part of the relief; the engine posts no `COST_OF_REVENUE` line for it (D-76; POLICIES JET-02 row "Uninstalled materials") | `REVENUE_RECOGNITION` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `REVENUE` | contracting = performing; obligation | `TIME` for `NORMAL` amounts of deterministic components (pass `CLOSE_RELEASE`); `EVENT` otherwise; return-window expiry `TIME`. Rev 1.82 (supervisor rulings R-79 (a), R-99 (a); lane decision L3-2-Q-15; candidate AD-50): that is the class the release design gave the part; as built the part target carries no `TIME` share — no obligation is bound as released at close — so every amount of the part, the `NORMAL` amounts of deterministic components and the return-window expiry included, posts at compute as an `EVENT` delta in every postable period |
| JET-02 agent (POL-030) | as above, gross | `REVENUE_RECOGNITION` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `REVENUE` (retained) and `BILLING_CLEARING` (`AP_SUPPLIER`), split by the stage 03 gross-to-net basis with `largest_remainder` of the cumulative gross | contracting; obligation | as principal |
| JET-13 contracting side | relief of obligations performed by another entity (12) | `INTERCOMPANY` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_TO` (counterparty e_p) | contracting; obligation | as JET-02 |
| JET-13 performing side | pair (12) | `INTERCOMPANY` | `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_FROM` (counterparty e_c) | `REVENUE` | performing; obligation | as JET-02 |
| JET-03 invoice (`ENGINE`) | unconditional billing and tax (10) | `BILLING` | `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY`; `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE` | contracting; obligation | EVENT |
| JET-03 credit memo (`ENGINE`) | credit memos and tax (10) | `CREDIT_MEMO` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY`; `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE` | `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE` | contracting; obligation | EVENT |
| JET-04b | open refund-liability components (10) | `REFUND_LIABILITY` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `REFUND_LIABILITY` | contracting; obligation or contract@entity | EVENT; window expiry `TIME` |
| JET-04c (`ENGINE`) | receivable contra (10) | `RECEIVABLE_CONTRA` (E-29, 04 rev 1.2; OQ-B-18 resolved by D-76) | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `RECEIVABLE_CONTRA` | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| JET-05c | concession components created (06, 10) | `REFUND_LIABILITY` | `REVENUE` | `REFUND_LIABILITY` | contracting; obligation | EVENT |

| JET-06 reversal | posted reclass of the previous period | `NETTING_RECLASS_REVERSAL` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | `UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE`; `CONTRACT_ASSET` | contracting; obligation | TIME (pass `NETTING_RECLASS`) |

| JET-07d | cumulative derecognition (10) | `RETURN_ASSET` | `BILLING_CLEARING` (`INVENTORY`) | `RETURN_ASSET` | contracting; obligation | EVENT |

| JET-09b | amortisation (11) | `CONTRACT_COST_AMORTIZATION` | `CONTRACT_COST_AMORTIZATION` | asset role | asset entity; cost asset | TIME for `STRAIGHT_LINE`; EVENT for proportional over event-driven revenue |
