| JET-10d (D-25b; POLICIES rev 1.2) | monetary-liability remeasurement targets (12; S12-R-11, S12-R-19, S12-R-20) | `FX_REMEASUREMENT` | loss: `FX_GAIN_LOSS`; gain: `REFUND_LIABILITY`, `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` or `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE` | the other side | contracting; group@entity | TIME at period end (pass `FX_REMEASUREMENT`) and at return-window expiry (pass `CLOSE_RELEASE`); EVENT at recognition and at the other settlements (`CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED`, `RETURN_RECORDED`, `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET`, `CONTRACT_TERMINATED`, 25-7 derecognition) |
| JET-11a, JET-11b | `financing_interest_cum` (S04-R-12, S04-R-13) | `FINANCING_INTEREST` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY`; `INTEREST_EXPENSE` | `INTEREST_INCOME`; `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | contracting; contract@entity | TIME |
| JET-12 | required provision (11) | `LOSS_PROVISION` | `LOSS_EXPENSE` | `LOSS_PROVISION` | contracting; loss unit | TIME (05 RCP-07 and RCP-08(a) rev 1.2; OQ-B-13 resolved by D-76) |
| JET-14 promised, after revenue, release, share-based | `consideration_payable`, `incentive_release_ordinary_cum` (release part, posted on its own) and `share_based_reduction_cum` (share-based part) targets (S04-R-14 to S04-R-17; S10-R-26); the parts sum to `incentive_release_cum` with no residue (D-91) | `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE` | `CUSTOMER_INCENTIVE_ASSET`; `REVENUE` | `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`; `CUSTOMER_INCENTIVE_ASSET`; `BILLING_CLEARING` (`EQUITY`) | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| JET-15 (D-89 L7-6-Q-8) | LEGACY targets (13) | `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE` | `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE` | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` | contracting; obligation; book `LEGACY` | EVENT |
| JET-16 accrual; claim release | `warranty_accrual_cum` (S04-R-19); claim release on `COST_INCURRED` with payload `purpose = WARRANTY_CLAIM` (04 E-03 payload rev 1.2; POLICIES OQ-13 resolved by D-76) | `WARRANTY_ACCRUAL` | `WARRANTY_EXPENSE`; `WARRANTY_PROVISION` | `WARRANTY_PROVISION`; `COST_OF_REVENUE` | performing; obligation | EVENT |
| JET-17 unconditional; receipt | `noncash_asset_recognised_cum` (S04-R-18); receipt on `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` with payload `form = NONCASH` (04 E-03 payload rev 1.2; POLICIES OQ-13 resolved by D-76) | `NONCASH_CONSIDERATION` | `NONCASH_CONSIDERATION_ASSET`; `BILLING_CLEARING` (`INVESTMENTS`) | `CONTRACT_LIABILITY`; `NONCASH_CONSIDERATION_ASSET` | contracting; contract@entity | EVENT |
| Manual adjustment | approved lines (S14-R-09a) | `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT` | as approved | as approved | adjustment entity; contract or obligation | EVENT (posting kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`) |
| `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S07-R-07; rev 1.16) | no part of its own: the stage 14 difference at the cutover posts through the family's part above (JET-02 principal / JET-04a, JET-03 invoice, JET-01b receipt / criteria met / refund, JET-04b, JET-07c) | the family's | the family's | the family's | the family's | EVENT, dated the cutover, `reason_code = ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE`, RCP-04 assignment when the cutover period is closed |

`COUNTER_ROLES` is the mapping (template part, side) → (account role, clearing purpose) for the counter-entry roles of the parts above whose other side is owned by another subledger: JET-01b (`BILLING_CLEARING`, `UNAPPLIED_CASH`), JET-02 agent (`BILLING_CLEARING`, `AP_SUPPLIER`), JET-07c (`COST_OF_REVENUE`), JET-07d (`BILLING_CLEARING`, `INVENTORY`), JET-09a and 09a′ (`CONTRACT_COST_CLEARING`), JET-14 share-based (`BILLING_CLEARING`, `EQUITY`), JET-16 claim release (`COST_OF_REVENUE`), JET-17 receipt (`BILLING_CLEARING`, `INVESTMENTS`) (05 §3.6.1; POLICIES table 0.8-A).

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-01 | Every part target is a signed cumulative amount per (book, owner entity, subject, part) and period end, in transaction currency with its functional amount from stage 12. For each part the role targets balance by construction: the debit role target equals the sum of the credit role targets, and a split credit (agent, tax) is `largest_remainder` of the cumulative gross (D-16; CV-36) |
| S14-R-02 | JET-05b is not a separate target: a price change on satisfied performance settled through future pricing lowers the revenue relief target, and the derivation posts Dr `REVENUE` / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` through R4. JET-05c posts separately because its credit is `REFUND_LIABILITY` |
| S14-R-03 | JET-08 expiry and the prepaid-credit lapse post through the JET-02 target (cause `NORMAL`); the unclaimed-property part posts through JET-04b (S09-R-28) |

#### 14.2.2 Role targets and deltas (RCP-06)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RoleKey:                     # the posted grain of PostedAmountInput plus the two members of OQ-B-19
    book_code: str; entity: str; subject_key: str; entry_kind: str
    account_role: str; clearing_purpose: str | None; counterparty_entity: str | None

def role_targets(parts: Sequence[PartTarget]) -> dict[tuple[RoleKey, str, str], Target]:
    """(role key, period key, posting class) -> cumulative signed target; debit positive."""
    out: dict = defaultdict(int)
    for part in sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p.sort_key):
        for period, amounts in part.by_period():                  # cumulative through the period end
            for line in part.role_lines(amounts):                 # debit role +gross; credit roles −split shares
                out[(line.role_key, period.key, part.amount_class(period))] += line.signed_txn
    return out

def derive_intents(ctx: BookContext, targets, posted: PostedIndex) -> list[PostingIntent]:
    intents: list[PostingIntent] = []
    for rk in sorted(role_keys(targets, posted)):                # CV-23 order
        periods = entity_periods(rk.entity)                      # ascending (C-03)
        for p in periods:
            state = ctx.entities[rk.entity].state(p, ctx.book_code)
            if state == "future" or p.start > horizon_end(rk.entity):
                break
            if state in ("closed", "permanently_locked"):
                if rk.entry_kind in ("NETTING_RECLASS", "NETTING_RECLASS_REVERSAL"):
                    continue                                     # ALG-09 step 5: locked reclasses never re-reversed
                delta = period_target(targets, rk, p, classes=ALL) - posted.by_origin(rk, p, classes=ALL)
                if delta:
                    asg = s08_estimates_late_events.assign_posting_period(ctx, rk.entity, p.end)   # first open period
                    intents.append(intent(rk, delta, posting=asg.posting_period_key, origin=p.key,
                                          cls="EVENT", reason="LATE_EVENT"))
            else:                                                # open, closing, reopened
                for cls in classes_posted_by(ctx.trigger, ctx.pass_name):
                    delta = period_target(targets, rk, p, classes={cls}) - posted.by_origin(rk, p, classes={cls})
                    if delta:
                        intents.append(intent(rk, delta, posting=p.key, origin=None, cls=cls, reason=None))
    return group_into_entries(intents)                           # §14.2.4
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-04 | `period_target(rk, p, classes)` = cumulative target of the role key through the end of p for the classes, minus the same through the end of p − 1. `posted.by_origin(rk, p, classes)` = Σ posted amounts whose origin period (the `origin_period_id` of the line, else its posting period) is p and whose posting class is in the set. The posting class of a posted line is `EVENT` when its posting kind is `ENGINE_COMPUTE`, `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT` or `VOID_REVERSAL`, and `TIME` when it is `CLOSE_RELEASE`, `FX_REMEASUREMENT` or `NETTING_RECLASS` (E-31). `PostedAmountInput` carries `origin_period_key` and `posting_class`, and RCP-05 groups by them (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.4; 05 RCP-05 rev 1.2; OQ-B-19 resolved by D-76). **Posted subject (rev 1.165; item ENG-COST-READBACK-1; supervisor rulings R-11 as amended and R-44 (b); replaces "Posted remeasurements" of rev 1.50).** A posted amount answers the role key of the subject stored on its ledger lines (04 T-SL-04 `subject_key`, rev 1.282; 05 RCP-05 rev 1.202): the subject the engine posted under — for a JET-10 part the one subject `<group>@<entity>` of its contracting entity, for a refund-liability component, a cost asset or a contract-level loss unit its own (Table 14-A). A compute over its own postings then emits no intent in any subject family (S14-INV-02). Until rev 1.165 the read-back spelled every line without an obligation `<contract>@<entity>` and stage 14 matched a posted `FX_REMEASUREMENT` amount to the group's key by its entry kind alone; the other families were reversed and posted again by every compute whose input had moved, and the reason variants of S14-R-27 no longer summed to a role key that had lost its subject (a terminated contract with a capitalised commission could not be computed a second time). **Regrouping.** One rule, in place of the entry-kind match: a posted amount stored under a group-level subject of ANOTHER combination group — `<group>@<entity>` alone (FX remeasurement) or followed by further components (a refund-liability component `<group>@<entity>/<kind>/<source>`; any later family) — answers the same subject under the CURRENT group, every other component unchanged; `posted` holds the sealed lines of the current group's members (RCP-05), so such an amount was stored while a member belonged to that group. The amounts of the members then sum under one key and a combination posts only the difference to its target. A subject whose first component is a member contract, and one without a leading `<component>@`, is kept as stored. The rule assumes that no combination group's code is a member contract's external id; the engine refuses nothing in that case and reads such a key as the contract's own |
| S14-R-05 | `classes_posted_by`: triggers `COMMAND`, `REPLAY_VERIFY`, `RESTATE`, `UPGRADE_VALIDATE`, `FX_REPUBLISH`, `POLICY_RERUN`, `MIGRATION` and `DRY_RUN` post `EVENT` amounts for open periods and every amount of closed periods (RCP-08). Trigger `CLOSE_RELEASE` posts the `TIME` amounts of the close period for the parts of its pass: `CLOSE_RELEASE` (JET-01b re-evaluation, JET-02 and JET-13 `NORMAL` deterministic amounts where the set-based release did not already post them, JET-04a and JET-04b expiry with the JET-10d settlement remeasurement of the expired refund liability, JET-07c period-end remeasurement, JET-09b to 09d, JET-11, JET-12), `FX_REMEASUREMENT` (JET-10a, 10a′, 10b and 10d period end; D-25b), `NETTING_RECLASS` (JET-06). Rev 1.82 (supervisor rulings R-79 (a) and R-99 (a); lane decisions L3-2-Q-15, L4-3-Q-30, L5-5-Q-15; candidate AD-50): as built a pass posts the `TIME` share each part target carries, and these carry one — JET-12 (the whole amount) and the dated 606-10-25-7 releases of JET-01b in pass `CLOSE_RELEASE`, the period-end remeasurements of JET-10 in pass `FX_REMEASUREMENT`, JET-06 in pass `NETTING_RECLASS`. The amounts of JET-02 and JET-13 (the `NORMAL` deterministic amounts included; no set-based release exists), of JET-04, JET-07, JET-09 and JET-11 post at compute as `EVENT` deltas in every postable period and are no part of a close pass. A pass emits the `TIME` deltas of every postable period through the horizon (S14-R-04), not of one close period: which of them a close run seals is the platform's rule (05 RCP-08 rev 1.102; supervisor ruling R-112): the engine's pass emits every postable period and the close run takes its own — the amounts of the run's period and the reversal of the run's reclass in the next period; an amount of an earlier period refuses the run's step by name until that period's own close run has posted it. Rev 1.119 (supervisor ruling R-114 (a); 04 T-CLS-01 rev 1.171): the platform counts such an amount net per contract group, account role and currency — a set of entries of one period that nets to zero in every role, as S14-INV-02's delta produces while a posted amount is not read back under the key it was posted with (the loss unit, supervisor ruling R-11), is no such amount |
| S14-R-06 | Posting-period assignment is stage 08's rule (RCP-04; ALG-09; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §8): an amount of a closed period posts in the first later open period with `origin_period` and `reason_code = LATE_EVENT`; an amount of a future period posts nothing. Stage 08 raises `LATE_EVENT` (WARNING); stage 14 never re-raises it |
| S14-R-07 | **Netting reclass.** The JET-06 target for period t is the reclass attribution at the end of t (§10.2.7), dated the last day of t. The reversal target of t is the negation of the reclass actually posted for t − 1, dated the first day of t, even when t has no other activity. Locked periods are skipped (S14-R-06; ALG-09 step 5); the reclass of the posting period is recomputed |
| S14-R-08 | **Voids.** A voided event or contract changes targets (S01-R-12, S01-R-13); the difference posts as a delta in the first open period with `reason_code = VOID`, posting kind `VOID_REVERSAL` for `CONTRACT_VOIDED`. Posted lines are never updated (REQ-JE-006) |
| S14-R-09 | Account mapping changes never repost history: a delta resolves its account with the mapping version pinned for the computation and the obligation overrides in force at the line's effective date (T-REF-15; S06-R-26); reclassifying existing balances between accounts is an `ACCOUNT_RECLASS` manual adjustment |
| S14-R-09a | **Manual adjustment lines (rev 1.63; supervisor ruling R-51 (a); BUILD_SPEC CLO-12; §14.1; Table 14-A "Manual adjustment").** An included `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_APPLIED` event whose payload `kind` is `MANUAL_JOURNAL` or `ACCOUNT_RECLASS`, in the book its `book_code` names (S09-R-41), gives one share per approved line to the role key (book, the payload's `entity_code`, subject, entry kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`, the line's `account_role`, no clearing purpose, no counterparty). The subject is the obligation of `obligation_key`, or without one the contract's `<contract>@<entity>`. The cumulative target of a role key through a period end is the signed sum of `amount_txn` (a debit positive) of the lines whose adjustment period `period_key` is on or before it, from the first adjustment period through the horizon; the amounts are class `EVENT` and have no `TIME` share. **Limit of 1.0 (supervisor ruling R-51).** The contract is in its entity's functional currency: a T-SL-05 line states one amount and no rate reference, so a foreign-currency line could not name its rate (S14-R-28, S14-INV-08); each line therefore carries the same amount in both currencies, and the two-line form of S14-R-10 for a delta whose transaction and functional amounts differ in sign cannot arise from a manual line. The delta rule then treats these targets as every other (S14-R-04 to S14-R-08). The platform posts the approved lines when the adjustment is approved — posting kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`, the accounts as approved (04 T-SL-05) — so `posted` equals the target and a computation emits nothing for them, at the approval and at every later computation; a ledger that does not hold them (a replay into an empty ledger, a sandbox load) receives them from stage 14, the account resolved by the mapping of the role (S14-R-14); a target in a period closed since posts late with that period as origin (S14-R-06); an adjustment whose event is voided has no target, so its posted lines are reversed (S14-R-08). Lines of one role net within their role key: a reclassification between two accounts of one role has a target of 0, the platform's posting is its only record, and stage 14 never derives it. The adjustment is refused by name — `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, detail rule `S14-R-09a` and the event key — when it carries fewer than two lines; a malformed line or amount; an amount below the minor unit; a line of amount 0; lines that do not sum to 0 in either currency; a role among `RETAINED_EARNINGS`, `FINANCING_OBLIGATION` (D-14a), `ROUNDING` (S14-R-11), `BILLING_CLEARING`, `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_TO` and `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_FROM` (the line shape carries no clearing purpose and no counterparty); an entity or a period the bundle lacks; or a contract whose transaction currency is not the entity's functional currency (the limit above; the platform refuses the adjustment first, 422 with this rule id). No Table 14-A part carries the entry kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`. Trace: node `posting_target`, formula `post.role_target.v1`, inputs the event with member `lines[i].amount_txn` and its value, parameters `amount_functional` (equal to the target, by the limit), `signs` and `time = 0` |

#### 14.2.3 Functional amounts and rounding residue

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-10 | Every role target carries a functional amount from stage 12. Within an entry the debit functional amount equals the sum of the credit functional amounts: pair lines take one functional amount (layer relief, rate conversion or remeasurement difference), and split credits apportion the functional total with `largest_remainder` using the transaction split as weights. DB-06 then holds per (entity, book, period, entry) in both currencies. **Opposed signs (rev 1.65; ENG-S14R10-FX-SIGN-1; supervisor ruling R-44 (a)).** The transaction delta and the functional delta of a role key are derived independently (S14-R-04), so with every rate positive they can still differ in sign — for example when an invoice becomes known after revenue of its period was posted at the period rate. Such a delta posts as two lines of the same role in its entry: a *transaction line* carrying the transaction amount with a functional amount of 0, and a *functional line* carrying the functional amount with a transaction amount of 0; each takes its side from its own amount. A delta whose amounts agree in sign, or one of whose amounts is 0, posts one line. The two signed totals of the role per posting period, origin period and currency are those of the delta; the entry balances in each currency (S14-INV-01); the split uses no rate and creates no `FX_GAIN_LOSS` line. Decision L2-5-Q-34, which refused such a delta, is withdrawn as to the refusal; its side rule — the side of a line is that of its transaction amount, else of its functional amount — stands for each line (S14-INV-09) |
| S14-R-11 | The subledger has no rounding residue: amounts are integer minor units derived once (CV-35). `ROUNDING` is never emitted by the engine; it arises only in the platform conversion of a summarised batch into another currency (§14.3.5; D-16) |
| S14-R-28 | **Rate references of a line (rev 1.50; ENG-FXREM-RATEPIN-1; REQ-FX-006; S12-R-02; L2-5-Q-35).** A line whose transaction currency differs from its functional currency names the rates behind its functional amount in `PostingState.line_rates` (line key → rate references, each the natural keys `rate_key` and `version_key` of a rate row); a same-currency line names none. **(a)** The references are those of the line's role target through the delta's period: the distinct pinned rates of the stage 12 targets of the parts that share the role key (S14-R-01, S14-R-10). **(b)** A delta whose role target supplies none only reverses posted amounts — the target is absent or nil in the computation: the void of a posted delivery, a part that no longer arises once an earlier-dated event re-layers the contract, the remeasurement of a locked period taken back in the first open period — and names the references of the posted amounts it is measured against: `PostedAmountInput.rate_refs` of its origin period, of the locked periods a `TIME` delta covers, and of its posting classes (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §0.4 rev 1.43; 05 RCP-05 rev 1.41). Such a reference is the rate stamped on the posted lines; its rate-set version may have been superseded since, so it need not be pinned for the computation. **(c)** The JET-06 reversal of period t, whose target negates the reclass posted for t − 1 (S14-R-07), names the references of what it negates: those of the posted reclass lines of t − 1 and of the reclass delta of this computation; where the posted lines carry none, those of the reclass role target of t − 1. That amount is the asset carrying at the closing rate of t − 1 (S12-R-09), so the stamp of the reversal is that closing rate. T-SL-04 holds one rate per line: the platform stamps the reference with the latest effective date, ties to the greatest rate key, and the full list stays in the calculation trace (D-88 L7-6-Q-1; under (b) the posted source reference of the `posting_delta` node lists the rate keys as `rate_keys`). A foreign-currency line for which neither the computation nor the posted amounts hold a reference is refused (S14-INV-08). Both lines of an opposed delta (S14-R-10; rev 1.65) name the delta's references, so both take the same stamp |

#### 14.2.4 Entries, ordering, accounts and keys

```python
def group_into_entries(intents: list[RoleIntent]) -> list[PostingIntent]:
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for it in intents:
        groups[(it.book_code, it.entity, it.posting_period_key, it.origin_period_key or "", it.entry_kind,
                it.posting_class, it.subject_key, it.reason_code or "")].append(it)
    entries = []
    for key in sorted(groups):
        deltas = groups[key]
        assert sum(d.signed_txn for d in deltas) == 0 and sum(d.signed_functional for d in deltas) == 0   # S14-INV-01
        lines = sorted((l for d in deltas for l in legs(d)),                     # S14-R-10: one line, or two
                       key=lambda l: (0 if side(l) == "D" else 1, l.account_role,
                                      l.clearing_purpose or "", l.counterparty_entity or ""))
        entries.append(PostingIntent(entry_key=canonical.sha256_hex(entry_key_object(key)),
                                     lines=tuple(resolve_account(l) for l in lines), ...))
    return entries

def legs(delta: RoleIntent) -> list[RoleIntent]:
    # S14-R-10 (rev 1.65): a delta whose two amounts differ in sign is a transaction line and a functional line
    if delta.signed_txn and delta.signed_functional and (delta.signed_txn > 0) != (delta.signed_functional > 0):
        return [replace(delta, signed_functional=0),                           # the transaction line: the delta's line key
                replace(delta, signed_txn=0, currency_leg="FUNCTIONAL")]       # the functional line (S14-R-15)
    return [delta]

def side(line) -> str:
    # S14-INV-09: the side of the line's own non-zero amount — its transaction amount, else its functional amount
    return "D" if (line.signed_txn or line.signed_functional) > 0 else "C"

def resolve_account(line) -> IntentLine:
    override_key = line.account_role if line.account_role != "BILLING_CLEARING" \
                   else f"BILLING_CLEARING:{line.clearing_purpose}"
    code = (obligation_override(line.subject_key, override_key)             # T-REF-15 step 1
            or template_override(line.subject_key, override_key)            # step 2
            or mapping_rule(ctx.mapping, line))                             # step 3: specificity, then priority
    if code is None:
        finding("ACCOUNT_MAPPING_MISSING", line.subject_key,
                detail={"role": line.account_role, "clearing_purpose": line.clearing_purpose or ""})
    return IntentLine(side=side(line), amount_txn=abs(line.signed_txn),
                      amount_functional=abs(line.signed_functional), account_code=code, ...)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-12 | An entry groups the role deltas of one (book, entity, posting period, origin period, entry kind, posting class, subject, reason code). Because each part balances per entry kind and posted amounts balanced when sealed, the deltas of an entry balance (S14-INV-01). `entry_no` is the 1-based position of the entry in ascending entry-key order within the posting (T-SL-04; D-16). An entry holds one line per role delta, or the two lines of S14-R-10 for a delta whose amounts differ in sign (rev 1.65). Lines are ordered debit first, then by role, clearing purpose and counterparty (§14.2.4); the two lines of one role differ in side, so the order stays total |
| S14-R-13 | Every line carries the dimensions of JET rule R3: contract (combination group), obligation where the subject is one, product or revenue category, counterparty entity (intercompany roles only), `reason_code`, source event key, calculation run id (the orchestrator fills the id), and the trace node id of its target. `clearing_purpose` is present exactly on `BILLING_CLEARING` lines (R8; E-109); the reserved roles `RETAINED_EARNINGS` and `FINANCING_OBLIGATION` are never emitted (D-14a) |
| S14-R-13a | **Source event lineage of cumulative deltas (rev 1.29; D-98 candidate 95; Codex C6-RPT-R3).** A role delta is a difference of cumulative targets and carries no single source event (`source_event_key = None`; L2-5-Q-36). Every intent line carries instead `source_event_keys`: the keys of the events of the delta's subject's member contracts (`subject_contracts`) first included in the computation — stream version past the previous stream head (Table 0.9-A `is_new`) — in ENG-06 order, read from the bundle stream before the S01-R-12 removal so an `EVENT_VOIDED` and a `CONTRACT_VOIDED` name themselves. When the subject's member contracts have no first-included event, the set is the **group's** first-included events of the computation (every member, ENG-06 order): a combination group is one allocation unit, so member B's event legitimately moves member A's targets and A's lines have a real event cause (D-98 candidate 102b; Q-C6-RPT-6); the register labels the scope (`lineage_scope` `SUBJECT` \| `GROUP`), and a trigger is considered only when the group's set is also empty. Stage 13 binds the set (`new_events`) as it binds `posted` and `voided`; a stage 14 call without the binding yields empty sets. The engine computes no per-event split of the amount: the set is the delta's lineage, not an apportionment, and it is empty for a delta no first-included event produced (a trigger-only recomputation). The platform persists the set as T-SL-12 `subledger_line_event` rows in set order; `subledger_line.contract_event_id` stays the single-event key of non-cumulative lines |
| S14-R-14 | A missing account raises `ACCOUNT_MAPPING_MISSING` (ERROR): the computation is `QUARANTINED` and nothing posts (REQ-JE-022; DEV-042). The finding names contract, obligation, role and, for clearing lines, the purpose |
| S14-R-15 | **Keys.** `entry_key` = `canonical.sha256_hex` of {`book_code`, `entity`, `posting_period_key`, `origin_period_key`, `entry_kind`, `posting_class`, `subject_key`, `reason_code`}. `line_key` = `canonical.sha256_hex` of {`account_role`, `book_code`, `clearing_purpose`, `counterparty_entity`, `entity`, `entry_kind`, `origin_period_key`, `posting_class`, `posting_period_key`, `subject_key`} (DG §5.17), plus `reason_code` for a line whose `reason_code` is a Table 14-A template reason (`TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`, `CLAWBACK`; S14-R-27, rev 1.15): the untagged line of the same members may sit in another entry of the posting, and every other line keeps the ten-member object, so the EX-14-A vector stands. The functional line of an opposed delta (S14-R-10; rev 1.65) adds the member `currency_leg` = `FUNCTIONAL` — beside `reason_code` where the delta is a reason variant; its transaction line keeps the delta's own object. The orchestrator persists postings with the deterministic idempotency keys of T-SL-01 (`compute:<computation id>`, `release:<close run id>:<group id>`, `fx:<close run id>:<entity id>`, `reclass:<close run id>:<entity id>`), so a retried persist cannot double post (RCP-21). EX-14-A gives a test vector |

### 14.3 Platform: journal summarisation and export (`erev_api/domain/journals/`)

#### 14.3.1 Selection

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-16 | A journal run for (entity, book, period, mode) selects the sealed `subledger_line` rows of the entity, book and period whose posting seal `chain_seq` lies in (`from_chain_seq`, `to_chain_seq`] (T-SL-06; DB-16). With `mode = DELTA` it also selects the LEGACY book's range and outputs primary lines plus LEGACY lines per grouping key, with no negation (S13-R-09; JET-15; D-89 L7-6-Q-8). Both ranges start where the non-cancelled runs of the same entity, book and period end, whatever their mode, so a seal is journalised by one run only (rev 1.54; 04 DB-16 rev 1.106; supervisor ruling R-32 on security finding SC-7). Rev 1.164: beside its range a run summarizes the lines it takes over under S14-R-17 — a set named by id, in the range of no later run |
| S14-R-17 | Lines of contracts under an open `journal_export` hold are excluded from the batches and listed in the run's detail file as held; they stay close blockers until released or waived (REQ-JE-017). Rev 1.164 (item JRN-HELD-AFTER-EXPORT-1; the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-02 on the lane's measurement): **a released line is journalised by the next run of its entity, book and period.** That run takes over every line that a run of the key which is not cancelled left out as held, that no such run has taken over since, and whose contract is under no open `journal_export` hold when it is calculated. It summarizes them with the lines of its range, names them by id in its `CALCULATE` audit event (`taken_over_subledger_line_ids`; an empty list is the explicit state of none) and lists them, each with the run that left it out, in a detail file of its own whose id and SHA-256 the event carries. The set is computed under the key's coverage lock, which a cancel holds too, so a line is taken over once; the cancel of the taking run gives it back, because the record of a cancelled run counts for nothing; a line left out by a run that was cancelled lies in the next run's range (S14-R-16) and is not taken over too; a line whose contract is still under a hold stays with the record of the run that left it out; a LEGACY line is taken over by a `DELTA` run alone (S14-R-23). The completeness assertion (REQ-JE-005) counts a line as journalised by the run whose range holds it and that did not leave it out, or by the run that took it over. Until this revision a line left out by a run that was then exported was reached by no run: the run could not be cancelled, every later run started where it ended, and the period could not be locked |

#### 14.3.2 Grain (POL-006)

| Grain (E-33) | Grouping key of a journal line | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| `ENTITY_BOOK_CURRENCY_PERIOD_ACCOUNT_DIMENSIONS` (default) | (txn currency, GL account, dimension set SHA-256, counterparty entity, origin period) | REQ-JE-010 |
| `CONTRACT_ACCOUNT_DIMENSIONS` | as above plus contract (combination group) | REQ-JE-010; 04:OQ-05 |
| `LEGACY_CONTRACT_POB` | balance-sheet roles by (txn currency, GL account, contract); revenue roles by (txn currency, GL account, `legacy_key` of the obligation) | PJR-5; legacy 06 P-JE-05 |

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-18 | Each group nets its lines: debit = max(net, 0), credit = max(−net, 0), in transaction and functional currency independently; a group whose nets are both 0 is dropped (PJR-5). `source_grouping_sha256` is the SHA-256 of the canonical grouping key (T-SL-09), and `source_line_count` counts the contributing detail lines. **Manual adjustments (rev 1.63; BUILD_SPEC CLO-12).** At every grain the grouping key of a detail line of entry kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT` ends with that kind and the `manual_adjustment_id` of the line's posting (T-SL-01; empty for a line stage 14 posted): it nets with the lines of the same adjustment only, never with an automated line on the same account and dimensions. The keys of every other kind are those of table 14.3.2, member for member |
| S14-R-19 | Journal entries (T-SL-08) are one per (batch, entry kind); `je_type` is `manual` for `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`, `reversal` for lines of a `VOID_REVERSAL` posting, `automated` otherwise; `is_post_close = true` when any contributing line has `origin_period_id` or `is_post_reopen` (REQ-JE-018). Every entry balances because every detail entry of its kind balances (DB-16). **Manual adjustments (rev 1.63; BUILD_SPEC CLO-12).** The entry kind `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT` has one entry per adjustment: the summarised lines of one `manual_adjustment_id` (S14-R-18) form one `manual` entry that names the adjustment in T-SL-08 `manual_adjustment_id` and balances because the adjustment's lines do (S14-R-09a); lines of the kind that name no adjustment form one `manual` entry without it |
| S14-R-20 | Line order within a batch is ascending (entry kind, GL account code, dimension set SHA-256, counterparty, origin period, contract, legacy key); `line_no` follows it. JE numbers are allocated gaplessly per entity when the run is calculated (T-SL-08; DB-16) |

#### 14.3.3 Batches, chunks and external ids

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-21 | One batch per (run, transaction currency) (T-SL-07). Chunks take whole entries in S14-R-20 order until the adapter's line limit would be exceeded (`integration_connection` configuration; CSV has no limit). An entry larger than the limit is re-summarised at the `CONTRACT_ACCOUNT_DIMENSIONS` grain and split by contract, which keeps every chunk balanced [J: per-contract lines of one entry kind balance on their own] |
| S14-R-22 | `external_id` = `erev:<tenant code>:<run_no>:<batch_no>:<chunk_no>` (T-SL-07; NetSuite `eid:`, QuickBooks Online `requestid`; REQ-JE-012). Approval writes one outbox message per batch with `dedupe_key = external_id` (ADP-30), so a repeated export creates no second ERP document (CTL-021) |

#### 14.3.4 Delta and legacy views

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-23 | `DELTA` journal lines = primary-book lines plus LEGACY-book lines per grouping key, with no negation (JET-15; D-89 L7-6-Q-8); CHK-020 is the unit test. The legacy gross and adjustment report views (report `legacy_je_summary`; REQ-JE-007, REQ-JE-008) are computed from subledger lines by DEVIATIONS PJR-2 to PJR-5 over an effective-date window |

#### 14.3.5 Currency conversion residue

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S14-R-24 | When an adapter needs a batch in a currency other than the line currencies, each summarised line is converted at the batch rate with `round_half_up`; the difference Σ converted debits − Σ converted credits posts as one `ROUNDING` line in that batch (D-16; research 06 §9.5). The line is reported on the run and expected to be 0 or a few minor units |
| S14-R-25 | **25-7 revenue attribution (D-91; L5-5-Q-5; rev pending END-4b).** The contract-level `deposit_to_revenue_cum` and `deposit_to_revenue_time_cum` are apportioned to the contract's `IN_SCOPE_606` obligations with `largest_remainder` over the posted inception allocation A_p; when Σ A_p = 0 over the resolved SSP (both D-77: decision 5 of this file, decision 6 of POLICIES); when that sums to 0 over the stated prices; otherwise `TOTAL_WEIGHT_ZERO` (fail closed; the last two are new in D-91). Each share is node `deposit_revenue_share:<obligation>:<period>` (formula `step1.deposit_revenue_share.v1`; input the stage 02 node; params `keys`, `weights`, `key`, `basis`), emitted by stage 09 (which holds the recognitions and the allocated state) and cited by the stage 14 part target and by the stage 09 STEP1_MET netting (ENGINE_SPEC S02-R-04). A foreign-currency release takes the stage 12 `fx_deposit_to_revenue_functional` amount (S12-R-20) split over the attribution weights by S14-R-10 (D-91 (iv), (ix)); its `TIME` share (the dated-point releases, node `deposit_revenue_time_share:<obligation>:<period>`) takes the stage 12 `fx_deposit_to_revenue_time_functional` amount — the dated releases at their own release-date conversion — split over the same weights, so a dated release at one spot and an event release at another keep their functional amounts (D-94 (6); Codex D1-R04) |
| S14-R-26 | **Opening baseline deemed posted (rev 1.12; ENB-9; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S07-R-05, S07-INV-01).** A role key whose subject carries a stage 07 `OpeningBaseline` with cutover date D (an obligation with a baseline; a `<contract>@<entity>` subject when every obligation of the contract carries a baseline with the same D) emits no delta for a period ending on or before D, whatever the period's state and posting class: the cumulative target of that period is the baseline the ERP already holds, so it is deemed posted, and the first later period's delta is measured from it (`period_target` of the first period after D = its cumulative target − the baseline). A closed pre-cutover period therefore carries nothing into the first open period (no `LATE_EVENT` carry of the baseline revenue), a repeat compute posts nothing new, and the `cutover-no-postings` control holds. **Onboarding differences (rev 1.16; `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S07-R-07).** Under `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` the period containing D posts, for every role key of the subject, the S07-R-07 difference = the role target at D − the imported role amount (the baseline member of the role's family mapped through the family's Table 14-A template: `revenue_cum` through JET-02 principal / JET-04a, `billed_cum` through JET-03 invoice with tax 0 where the ledger holds `ENGINE`-mode lines of the contract dated on or before D (on the S10-R-03 date) (S10-R-03; otherwise JET-03 has no difference), `deposit_liability` through the JET-01b parts when the payload carries the member — an absent member with a non-zero recomputed target records `ONBOARDING_MEMBER_MISSING`, `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S07-R-07 rev 1.18 —, 0 for JET-04b and JET-07c), less what is already posted for D's period, as a delta dated D with `reason_code = ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` and class `EVENT`, assigned by RCP-04 (S14-R-06) when D's period is closed; the recomputed cumulative is then deemed posted as above. A role key of a family outside S07-R-07 (JET-05c, JET-06, JET-09 to JET-17) is deemed posted at D without a difference; a role key whose imported amount the template cannot map raises `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (fail closed). **Explicit eligibility (rev 1.17; Codex C5-PH2-R1, C5-PH2-R2).** The imported-amount resolution classifies every role key of a `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` subject at D as *eligible* (with its imported amount — a zero import is eligible and posts its whole recomputed cumulative, JET-04b and JET-07c), *deemed posted* (a family outside S07-R-07; a JET-03 key without recomputed `ENGINE` lines; an absent optional member with a zero target) or *refused* (`ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED`, `ONBOARDING_MEMBER_MISSING`); the difference is computed for eligible keys only and is never inferred from the presence of a key or a non-zero amount, and a key shared by an eligible and a deemed-posted part measures the eligible part's target only. **Component owners (rev 1.18; S10-R-13 keys `<group>@<entity>/<KIND>/<source>`).** Every refund-liability component resolves to the opening of its owner before the cutover, eligibility and refusal checks (D-98 35a; ENG-C5 phase 3): `RETURN`, `CONCESSION` and `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY` to the obligation their source names; `TERMINATION` (source = the `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` event key) and `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` (source = the estimate key, whose head is the CV-21 encoded contract id) to the `<contract>@<entity>` opening of the contract the source names at the component's entity — the source head is the CV-21 encoded contract id and is matched against the encoded ids of the openings' contracts exactly, never against a raw id (a raw id can equal another contract's encoded form, `K%2FA` against `K/A`; Codex C5-PH3-R1, D-98 candidate 53) — the opening that exists when every obligation of the contract at that entity carries a baseline with one cutover and one method; the role keys keep the component subject and the difference node names the owner (param `owner`). A component whose owner cannot be resolved while its contract carries openings — a mixed contract (an obligation without a baseline, or differing cutovers or methods) for a contract-owned kind, or an obligation-owned component whose obligation carries no baseline — raises `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (reason `component_owner_unresolved`; detail `kind`, `contract`, `entity`, `owner_candidates`) for its parts in periods ending on or before the latest candidate cutover; the rev 1.18 interim containment, which refused the three kinds outright, is withdrawn. The per-obligation return assets carry the obligation subject and need no resolution. An absent `billed_cum` member with a non-zero JET-03 target where `ENGINE` lines are recomputed through D records `ONBOARDING_MEMBER_MISSING` as the deposit member does (D-98 candidate 35, P2-Q-5). Business combination CHK-121 (ONB-CHK-121): ASC606 revenue 120,000.00 through the 2025-12-31 cutover deemed posted, January 2026 posts Dr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` / Cr `REVENUE` 10,000.00 against the opening liability 240,000.00 − 130,000.00 = 110,000.00; IFRS15 7,500.00 against 90,000.00 − 7,500.00 = 82,500.00 |
| S14-R-27 | **Template reason codes and reason variants (D-98 candidate 19 (supervisor ruling on lane ENG-C8 Q-1, 2026-09-19)).** A Table 14-A part whose row names a `reason_code` (JET-09e `TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`, S11-R-13; JET-09f `CLAWBACK`, S11-R-12) contributes a *tagged* share to each of its role keys; every other share is untagged. The role key and its cumulative target are unchanged (S14-R-04); for a role key with a tagged share, `role_targets` also publishes one `posting_target` node per *variant* — each template reason and the untagged remainder (§14.6) — whose amounts sum to the role key's target. In a postable period (`open`, `closing`, `reopened`) of a subject without a new void, the delta of a class is derived per variant: the variant's period movement less the posted amounts of that origin and class whose `reason_code` is the variant's (the untagged variant: the lines without a `reason_code`), provided every posted line of the role key at that origin and class is attributable (its `reason_code` is absent or a Table 14-A reason). Each variant delta carries the variant's `reason_code` (None for the untagged one) and is therefore its own entry under S14-R-12: at the CHK-112 termination the acceleration 18,000.00 (`TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`) and the month's amortisation 1,000.00 (no reason) are two `CONTRACT_COST_AMORTIZATION` entries whose role totals are the 19,000.00 posted before. Otherwise the role key posts one delta as before: a closed-period carry keeps `reason_code = LATE_EVENT` with its origin (S08-R-08; a late-placed acceleration or clawback is not re-tagged), a new void keeps `VOID` (S14-R-08), and a role key whose posted lines at that origin carry `LATE_EVENT`, `VOID` or any other non-template reason posts its whole delta untagged, because those lines cannot be attributed to a variant. The variant deltas sum to the role key's delta, so totals at role and account grain never change. `PostedAmountInput.reason_code` carries the line's `reason_code` (ENGINE_SPEC §0.4; 05 RCP-05). No E-110 or T-SL-04 enumeration changes: `subledger_line.reason_code` is free text |

### 14.6 Trace nodes and formula ids

| Measure | Subject | Period | Formula id |
|---|---|---|---|
| `posting_target` | `<subject>/<entry kind>/<role>[:<purpose>]` (encoded components) | period | `post.role_target.v1` (inputs: the part target nodes) |
| `posting_target` (variant; S14-R-27) | `<subject>/<entry kind>/<role>[:<purpose>]/<reason>` — `<reason>` is the template reason (`TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`, `CLAWBACK`) or `UNTAGGED`; published only for a role key with a tagged share | period | `post.role_target.v1` (inputs: the variant's part target nodes) |
| `posting_delta` | as above plus `/<posting class>` (a variant delta: the variant subject plus `/<posting class>`) | posting period | `post.delta.v1` (inputs: `posting_target` of the role key or variant, source ref to posted totals) |
| `account_resolution` | line key | posting period | `post.account_resolution.v1` (source ref: mapping rule key or override) |

Intent lines carry `trace_node_id` = their `posting_target` node, so the reverse drill of RCP-27 reaches stage 09 to 12 nodes from a journal line; they carry `source_event_keys` (S14-R-13a) so the out-of-period register attributes a posted delta to the events that produced it without a per-event split (rev 1.29). Both lines of an opposed delta (S14-R-10; rev 1.65) carry the `posting_target` node of their role, and each has its own `account_resolution` node; the `posting_delta` node stays one per delta.

## 15. Stage 15: close projections and disclosures

Stage 15 produces the close datasets and disclosures: revenue waterfall; contract balances and their rollforward with catch-ups separated by cause; RPO with time bands, exemptions and rollforward; disaggregation; the contract-cost rollforward; revenue from the opening contract liability and from obligations satisfied in prior periods; lock snapshots; and the variance between two closes. The engine part computes per-group measures that need engine state. The platform part aggregates persisted engine outputs and the subledger at close cutoff, so every figure is reproducible from immutable records (REQ-RPT-002, REQ-RPT-003).

### 15.1 Inputs and outputs

| Layer | Inputs | Outputs |
|---|---|---|
| Engine (`s15_disclosures/`) | Stage 09 schedules and obligation measures; stage 10 presentation; stage 11 cost assets; stage 08 prior-period targets (`decompose_prior_period`, `ENGINE_SPEC.md` §8); resolved POL-190 to POL-204; horizon | `DisclosureState`: per obligation RPO, band placement and exemption flags at the version date; `prior_period` (rev 1.19, D-97 (19)): `targets`, one `revenue_prior_period` target per measured obligation and period from the inception period through the horizon, and `sums`, one `revenue_prior_period_sum` target per contract, performing entity and period (§15.5); `disaggregation`: `rows` per entity, posting period, dimension code and value over the `REVENUE` lines of the posting intents, `totals` per entity, period and dimension, and the `dimensions` seen (S15-R-15, S15-R-16, S15-INV-03); disaggregation tags on revenue intents; `expedients_used` facts; `contract_version.rpo_amount` (gross of the S15-R-09 exemptions) and `rpo_after_exemptions` (the net; rev 1.26, D-98 91a) |
| Platform (`erev_api/domain/reports/`, `erev_api/domain/close/snapshots.py`) | `schedule_line`, `obligation_version`, `contract_version_balance`, `cost_asset_version`, `subledger_line` with seal ranges, `calc_trace`, previous `lock_snapshot` datasets; `known_at` cutoff of the close run | Report runs (T-RPT-02) for the report codes of T-RPT-01; `disclosure_snapshot` rows (E-65); `lock_snapshot` datasets (E-64) and the manifest hash on `period_lock`; variance and re-lock diff reports |

Every platform dataset reads the latest `contract_version` per group with `known_at ≤ cutoff_known_at` (T-ENG-02 release rule) and the subledger lines sealed through the run's chain range. "As locked" runs read the lock snapshot instead (T-CLS-05).

### 15.2 Pseudocode

#### 15.2.1 Revenue waterfall (REQ-RPT-004; report `revenue_waterfall`)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-01 | Rows: contract, obligation, product or revenue category (report parameter). Columns: periods, quarters or fiscal years of the entity calendar. Measures, each stated at the as-of period (rev 1.127; item RPT-ASOF-FIGURES-1; supervisor ruling R-116 (c), pending the accountant — candidate AD-63): **recognised** = Σ `REVENUE` subledger lines by posting period (all reason codes, including late-event carries) for the periods up to the as-of period; **scheduled** = Σ schedule lines of kind `REVENUE` for periods after the as-of period, for an obligation whose remainder at the as-of has a scheduled part (S09-R-45) — those lines are that part, and a version in which they do not add up to it is refused by name; **awaiting trigger** = the obligation's awaiting-trigger amount at the end of the as-of period (one column, not by period): allocated − recognised − scheduled at that date, read from the period states of the version through the S15-R-07a reader (04 API-C-10, the remainder at the cut). An obligation without a scheduled part states no scheduled amount: the later lines of its version — a transfer still to come, amounts recognised by events dated after the as-of — are inside its awaiting-trigger amount. A date beyond the last period a version measures is read at that period. Before rev 1.127 the awaiting column was `awaiting_trigger_amount` of the obligation version, the amount at the version's date d_v, and recognised had no bound: a computation recognises through every open period, so revenue recognised since d_v stood in recognised and again in awaiting trigger (a usage obligation dated nine open periods back: 59,835.62 + 0.00 + 79,780.82 = 139,616.44 for an allocation of 80,000.00), and at an as-of before the last posted period the periods in between stood in recognised and in scheduled for every pattern |
| S15-R-02 | Tie-outs printed on the report (REQ-RPT-003): recognised for each period = the revenue journal total of that period (CTL-019; S14-INV-06); for every included obligation recognised to date + scheduled + awaiting trigger = allocated amount (PROP:P7; DB-17) — at every as-of, by the measures of S15-R-01 (rev 1.127) |

#### 15.2.2 Contract balances and rollforward (606-10-50-8 to 50-10; REQ-RPT-005, REQ-RPT-006, REQ-RPT-007; POL-126)

```python
def rollforward_contract(c, e, book, period) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Path attribution per (group, contracting entity, book); aggregated per (entity, book, currency) by summing lines."""
    opening = presented_at(c, e, book, period.start - timedelta(days=1))      # previous lock snapshot or version at cutoff
    layers = [("OPENING", opening.CL)] if opening.CL else []
    asset = {"UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE": opening.UR, "CONTRACT_ASSET": opening.CA}
    out: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    for fl in control_role_flows(c, e, book, period):          # subledger CONTRACT_LIABILITY lines excluding JET-06 kinds,
                                                                # and the billing that posts no line (S15-R-03a),
                                                                # ordered (effective_date, chain_seq, entry_no, line_no)
        if fl.is_credit:                                        # billing, deposits, noncash, interest expense, RL release, negative relief
            settled = min(asset["UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE"] + asset["CONTRACT_ASSET"], fl.amount)
            left = settled
            for role in ("UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE", "CONTRACT_ASSET"):                 # receivable-like balances settle first
                take = min(asset[role], left); asset[role] -= take; left -= take
                out[f"{role}.transferred_to_receivables_or_settled"] -= take
            rest = fl.amount - settled                                              # remainder enters the liability
            if rest:
                layers.append(("NEW", rest)); out[f"CL.increases.{fl.kind}"] += rest
        else:                                                   # revenue relief, RL increase, contra, interest income, credit memo
            need = fl.amount
            for i, (tag, bal) in enumerate(layers):             # FIFO within the contract (POL-126)
                take = min(bal, need); need -= take; layers[i] = (tag, bal - take)
                if take:
                    label = (("CL.revenue_from_opening_balance" if tag == "OPENING" else "CL.revenue_from_other_increases")
                             if fl.is_revenue else f"CL.decreases.{fl.kind}")
                    out[label] -= take
            if need:
                role = right_class_role(fl.obligation)          # ALG-03 class of the revenue's obligation
                label = f"{role}.revenue_in_excess_of_billing" if fl.is_revenue else f"{role}.increases.{fl.kind}"
                asset[role] += need; out[label] += need
    closing = presented_at(c, e, book, period.end)
    for role in ("UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE", "CONTRACT_ASSET"):
        out[f"{role}.reclassified_between_asset_captions"] += getattr(closing, short(role)) - asset[role]
    return out
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-03 | **Lines.** Contract liability: opening; increases by kind (`BILLING`, `DEPOSIT_TRANSFER`, `NONCASH`, `FINANCING`, `REFUND_RELEASE`, `NEGATIVE_REVENUE`); revenue recognised from the opening balance; revenue recognised from other increases; other decreases by kind (`REFUND_LIABILITY`, `CONTRA`, `FINANCING`, `CREDIT_MEMO`); business combinations; FX (functional and reporting views only); closing. Contract asset and unbilled receivable: opening; revenue recognised in excess of billing; increases by kind; transferred to receivables or settled; reclassified between the two captions; business combinations; FX; closing (REQ-RPT-006; GLOSSARY). Memo lines "of which" give the period's revenue by cause (S15-R-04) |
| S15-R-03a | (rev 1.76; supervisor ruling R-72 (a), 2026-09-30) **Flows that post no line.** The flows of the path are the control-role flows of S12-R-03. Every one of them is a subledger line of the role except billing: an invoice or a credit memo posts a line in `ENGINE` mode only (JET-03; POL-004), and under `ERP` — the registry default — it is ingested for the position and never posted, so a path over the lines alone leaves the billing of the period as `other` and fails the tie-out in every period that holds an invoice. The billing of a period that is not on the role is the delta of the stage 10 node `billed_unconditional_cum` of the member contract and entity (S10-R-08 B_u; §10.5; read as S15-R-07a reads a balance — the latest period node at or before the period, 0 before the first) less the `BILLING` and `CREDIT_MEMO` lines of the role in the period. Where that is not zero and the role holds no such line in the period, the period's billing documents enter the path at their effective dates: the kept `BILLING_RECORDED` lines of S10-R-07 and the `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` events, without voided events and voided contracts, recorded by the cutoff — an invoice as a credit of kind `BILLING`, a credit memo as a debit of kind `CREDIT_MEMO`. On one date a document enters before the lines of that date ([J] a posted line carries the amount of its computation and is dated by it; the document's date is the invoice's). With such lines in the period the billing is on the role already and no document enters. The documents enter as recorded and nothing is plugged: a difference between them and the delta stays `other` and fails the tie-out (S15-R-07). No figure is derived a second time — the amount to explain is the engine's stored node and the documents are the stored events the engine read (rulings R-35, R-69). The billing events consumed are members of the run's source binding (S15-R-24). **Presentation (ruling R-72 (c)).** The report shows the kinds of S15-R-03 under the lines of 03 REQ-RPT-006 by the table "kind → line" of `SCREENS_B.md` RPT-03; a line per kind is not built in 1.0 |
| S15-R-04 | **Revenue by cause (memo lines).** For each contract and period: `LATE_EVENT` = revenue relief posted in the period with an origin period before it; every other cause (`NORMAL`, `MODIFICATION`, `TP_CHANGE`, `CATCH_UP`, `RETURN`, `BREAKAGE`, `ROYALTY`) = Σ of the period's `REVENUE` schedule lines of that line type in the contract version at cutoff (§9.2.10). Catch-ups from modifications, transaction-price estimates and measure-of-progress estimates are therefore disclosed separately, as 606-10-50-10(b) requires. The memo lines sum to the revenue lines of the rollforward and do not enter the tie. [J] Posted entries aggregate causes (S14-R-12), so the cause split comes from the version's decomposition (OQ-B-24) |
| S15-R-05 | **Revenue from the opening liability (POL-126 `FIFO_WITHIN_CONTRACT`).** The path consumes the opening liability layer before any new layer, so the line equals min(opening presented liability, revenue relief of the period) for the contract (POL-126 formula). `FIFO_WITHIN_POB` runs the same path per obligation with obligation-attributed billing |
| S15-R-06 | **Business combinations (PT-11 step 4).** Balances established by `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` with `reason = BUSINESS_COMBINATION` in the period enter as the business-combinations line at their acquisition-date presented amounts; they create no journal lines |
| S15-R-07 | **Ties.** Opening + lines = closing for every balance, entity, book and currency (PROP:P6; S15-INV-01); closing = contract balances report closing (CTL-030); in functional view, closing = GL balance when a trial balance exists (REQ-RPT-003). An unexplained difference is shown as `other` and fails the tie-out (REQ-RPT-006) |
| S15-R-07a | (rev 1.60; supervisor ruling R-16, 2026-09-29) **Presented balance at a period end (`presented_at`; the version-at-cutoff source of S15-R-03 to S15-R-07, of the contract balances report and of every other platform read of a member contract's period balance).** For a member contract c in contracting entity e, the balance of a T-CON-09 measure m at the end of period p is read from the calc trace of the contract version released at the cutoff (§15.1): the value of the node `m:<subject>:<p′>` of the latest period p′ ≤ p that carries one — 0 when p precedes the first node, the last node's value when p follows it. `<subject>` is the engine's contract-and-entity subject key: the contract's external id and the entity code EACH encoded by the one CV-21 table and joined with `@` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` CV-21, CV-50; `contract_entity_subject_key`); a reader never joins a key from the raw identifiers. A measure without a node under the subject is one the engine does not publish per member contract (§10.5: the current parts are measured per group and entity; `accounts_receivable` exists in `ENGINE` mode only), so its T-CON-09 column is the column default 0. The T-CON-09 row holds the balances at the version's LATEST period — the last period end of the subject's member-balance nodes — so a column without period nodes answers only when it is 0, or when p is at or after that latest period. Every other case is REFUSED BY NAME and no figure is served in its place: (a) a member row whose subject carries no balance node in its version's trace; (b) a non-zero column without period nodes asked for an earlier period. The refusal is problem `validation-failed` with one error per member contract and entity — `field` `balances[<external id>@<entity code>]`, `rule_id` `S15-R-07a`, the message naming the contract, the entity, the contract version and the period — and it refuses the whole read: a report run ends `FAILED` with the problem, the dataset freeze of a lock refuses and writes nothing, the dashboard read answers the problem. [J] The T-CON-09 row is one period, so answering it for an earlier period states a later balance as an earlier one; and a reader that finds no node of a member has lost the key, not found a zero. The defect this rule closes: the lookup was joined from the raw external id and the raw entity code, missed every identifier holding `%` `/` `@` `#` or `:`, and answered the latest balance for every earlier period — the rollforward opened at the closing, showed the period's revenue as `other` and failed S15-R-07 |

#### 15.2.3 RPO, time bands and practical expedients (606-10-50-13 to 50-15; POL-197 to POL-201)

```python
def rpo_obligation(o: ObligationMeasures, as_of: date, bands_months: tuple[int, ...]) -> RpoRow:
    included = o.satisfaction_status in ("UNSATISFIED", "PARTIALLY_SATISFIED")
    total = o.allocated_amount - o.revenue_cum if included else 0
    exempt = exemptions(o)                                       # S15-R-09; list of (exemption, excluded amount)
    excluded = sum(amount for _, amount in exempt)
    placed = defaultdict(int)
    bounds = [dates.add_months(as_of, b) for b in bands_months]  # POL-201 default [12, 24]
    for line in future_schedule_lines(o, after=as_of):           # scheduled amounts by period end
        placed[band_index(line.period_end, bounds)] += line.amount
    awaiting_band = band_index(o.end_date, bounds) if o.end_date else 0
    placed[awaiting_band] += o.awaiting_trigger_amount
    return RpoRow(total=total, excluded=excluded, bands=scale_for_exemptions(placed, total, excluded), exemptions=exempt)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-08 | RPO of an obligation = allocated constrained transaction price − cumulative revenue for obligations unsatisfied or partially satisfied at the as-of date (REQ-RPT-009; GLOSSARY: scheduled + awaiting trigger before exemptions). (rev 1.127; item RPT-ASOF-FIGURES-1; supervisor ruling R-116 (c), candidate AD-63) **Measured at the date.** Both terms are those of the as-of date d: the remainder of the obligation at d, its scheduled part plus its awaiting-trigger part, read from the period states published at the last performing-entity period end on or before d (the basis of S15-R-12; the S15-R-07a reader; 04 API-C-10) — never the schedule lines after d plus the version's stored `awaiting_trigger_amount`, which is the amount at the version's date and states again whatever was recognised since (measured: 79,780.82 for a remainder of 20,164.38, and 99,945.21 at an earlier date for an allocation of 80,000.00). `contract_version.rpo_amount` sums the included obligations **before** the S15-R-09 exemptions (rev 1.26; D-98 candidates 28, 91, 91a): the gross contractual remaining amount of S09-R-18, so EX42 contract A under POL-198 carries 12,000 − 3,000 = 9,000, never 0. The exempt amount is carried separately, by the `rpo_excluded` nodes and the §15.2.7 `RPO` dataset's Σ excluded, and `DisclosureState.rpo_after_exemptions` names the net amount (gross = net + exempt); the time bands (S15-R-10) and the report's included / excluded split stay net. (rev 1.161; item RPT-RPO-ROLLFWD-1; supervisor ruling R-121 (g)) **At a look-back the remainder at the date decides which obligations are in**: an obligation that is satisfied at its version's date d_v and was not yet at d has a remainder at d and is included with it — in the report and in the contract read's `rpo` (04 API-C-10 rev 1.213), which until then left it out (measured: 108,710.14 beside 118,430.14 for one contract at one date). An obligation stage 15 leaves out for another reason — a contract that is not a contract in the book, a cancelled obligation — is out at every date |
| S15-R-09 | **Exemptions.** POL-197 `APPLY`: every obligation of a contract whose original expected duration (inception to the latest obligation end date at inception) is 12 months or less. POL-198 `APPLY`: `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` obligations. POL-199 `APPLY` (ASC606 only): the `ROYALTY` component; the guarantee stays (50-14B). POL-200 `APPLY` (ASC606 only): VC allocated entirely to a wholly unsatisfied obligation or series increment under 32-40, using the targeted VC quota in force at the date measured (d_v for the RPO row; each end of the S15-R-12 rollforward), read from `AllocatedState.targeted_vc_quota_history` (stage 05 at inception, stage 08 after each reallocating version; ENGINE_SPEC S05-R-14, S08-R-07; OQ-B-21; rev 1.7, D-91). The excluded amount is min(remaining RPO, max(0, Σ a_posted of the quotas in force)); a negative quota exempts nothing (50-14A(b) omits consideration from RPO and never adds to it). The `rpo_excluded` node records `quota_as_of` and `quota_nodes`. An exempt amount is listed with the exemption, nature of the obligation, remaining duration and a description of the excluded consideration (50-15; POL-197 engine effect) |
| S15-R-10 | **Bands (POL-201).** Band k covers period ends in (as_of + b_(k−1) months, as_of + b_k months], with b_0 = 0 and the last band open-ended. Scheduled amounts are placed by their period end; awaiting-trigger amounts by the obligation's end date, or in the first band when it has none [J: event-driven amounts have no schedule; the contractual end date is the latest expected satisfaction]. (rev 1.127) The two amounts are those of the as-of date (S15-R-08): the scheduled part as the schedule lines of the later periods (S15-R-01), the awaiting-trigger part of that date. When a partial exemption applies, the bands of the obligation are apportioned over the non-exempt total with `largest_remainder` (weights = placed amounts). Σ bands = RPO after exemptions (PROP:P7) |
| S15-R-11 | The 50-15 narrative dataset lists the constrained-out amount (`contract_version.vc_excluded_amount`) and every exempt amount (REQ-TP-003) |
| S15-R-12 | **RPO rollforward (REQ-RPT-010; V9).** Opening RPO (previous lock snapshot) + new contracts (allocation of groups activated in the period) ± modifications (Σ allocation changes of segments with cause `MODIFICATION`, `TERMINATION`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISE` effective in the period) ± transaction-price and estimate changes (causes `TP_CHANGE`, `ESTIMATE_CHANGE`, and the movement of the expected-return reduction between segment boundaries) ± late events (allocation and revenue changes with an origin period before the period) − revenue recognised in the period on included obligations − cancellations (terminated remaining allocation) ± exemption movements (the exempt amount at each end follows S15-R-09 read at that end's date, the previous snapshot date for `OPENING` and the period end for `CLOSING`, never the latest quota; rev 1.10, D-91) ± FX (reporting views) = closing RPO. The unexplained difference is shown, and a non-zero difference fails the tie-out and raises a V9 warning line on the report. **Basis (D-91).** The RPO of an obligation at a date d is the S15-R-08 measurement: the allocation of the `FIXED` segment in force at d less the S09-R-23 reduction ρ(d) = round(r × (Y + E)) of a returnable obligation under POL-053 `REDUCE_CONTRACT_QUANTITY` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` S04-R-08b: the segments keep the gross allocation), less the stage 09 `revenue_cum` target; ρ(d) and R(d) are read from the stage 09 states published at the last performing-entity period end on or before d (C-04; S12-R-17), and a segment boundary inside the period is measured on its date. The restated opening, the closing and the terminated remainder are on this basis, so a segment's line takes the movement of the returns-adjusted allocation at its boundary (a new contract enters at the constrained transaction price allocated, 04 DB-17 V1; when booking precedes the first transfer, E is capped at units kept, ρ at the inception boundary is 0, `NEW_CONTRACTS` equals the gross allocation and the first-transfer reduction is a between-boundary movement). The between-boundary movement of ρ — estimate revisions (ALG-06 §2.7.2 step 1), returns in excess of E (step 6) and window expiry (S09-R-26) — is a change in the estimate of variable consideration (606-10-55-25; 32-6; 32-14; 32-42 to 32-44; POLICIES ALG-06 §2.7.1 [J]) and is carried on the transaction-price-and-estimate-changes line with sign −Δρ; actual returns within E move no line. An obligation that is not included (cancelled; S09-R-46) contributes nothing to any line after its cancellation boundary. **Eligibility interval (D-91 (vii); D-94 (4)).** The obligations of a contract whose book status is `NOT_A_CONTRACT` are outside RPO for as long as that status holds, on every line: a segment boundary while excluded (on its date or the day before) emits nothing; a contract that becomes eligible inside the period enters on `NEW_CONTRACTS` at the first eligible date at its allocation net of the returns reduction and of the 25-7 revenue attributed (S14-R-25), with the revenue recognised from that date on `REVENUE`; a contract that loses eligibility inside the period (a 606-10-25-5 reassessment) leaves on `CANCELLATIONS` at its remainder on the S15-R-08 basis, net of the 25-7 revenue attributed, with reason `STATUS_25_5` on the row so the preparer can reclassify (presentation flagged for G12: `EXEMPTIONS` or a dedicated line are the alternatives); both may happen in one period, and the period still reconciles without suppressing any revenue or the unexplained line. Revenue recognised remains the movement of the stage 09 targets (S14-INV-06). **The report's late-events line (rev 1.161; item RPT-RPO-ROLLFWD-1; supervisor ruling R-121 (g)).** The platform's rollforward (RPT-07) reads the opening from the version at the day before the range and the revenue line and the closing from the version at the range end (S15-R-24a (b)). `LATE_EVENTS` is the revenue the closing version states to the day before the range less the revenue the opening version states to that day — the revenue change with an origin before the period — and is zero when one version is read at both ends. Until rev 1.161 the report had no such line and the amount stood as unexplained (measured: −6,480.00 for a progress report of a locked period, posted in the next period with the locked one as its origin). The line follows the versions' dates and not the ledger: an event that arrives while the earlier period is still open is posted in that period, and the report shows its revenue as a late event of the period its version is effective in — and the RPO of the earlier date without it. That is a known limitation; its remedy is a reader of the ledger's posting and origin periods, an item of its own. V9 alone does not evidence the closing; S15-INV-08 does. Realised `PERIOD_VC` amounts (S09-R-02) are not yet in this basis (D-91 deferral) |

#### 15.2.4 Revenue from obligations satisfied in prior periods (606-10-50-12A; IFRS 15.116(c); POL-204)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-13 | The engine aggregates, per obligation and period t starting at s, the stage 08 targets of `decompose_prior_period` (`ENGINE_SPEC.md` §8; ALG-10 §2.11.3): Σ over allocation or measure changes k effective in t of round(E_after,k(s) − E_before,k(s)), plus late-event carries with an origin period before t (ALG-09 step 7). E_after,k(s) is the exact cumulative revenue at s measured with the allocation and progress totals after change k. For a pure transaction-price change this equals round(ΔX × f(s)) of ALG-10 §2.11.3; a 25-13(a) boundary contributes 0 (CV-63). Stage 15 calls `s08_estimates_late_events.decompose_prior_period(ctx, st.allocated, period_key, tb)` for every period through the horizon (§0.4); `ENGINE_SPEC.md` S08-R-14 publishes the measure-change case (OQ-B-20 resolved by D-76; POLICIES ALG-10 §2.11.3 rev 1.2) |
| S15-R-14 | Report `revenue_from_prior_period_obligations` sums the nodes `revenue_prior_period:<obligation>:<period>` of the traces referenced by the period's subledger lines (05 §3.6.8). Journal lines are not split (ALG-10 §2.11.3) |

#### 15.2.5 Disaggregation (606-10-50-5 to 50-7, 55-89 to 55-91; REQ-RPT-011; POL-191)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-15 | Stage 14 writes on every `REVENUE` line the disaggregation dimension values of its obligation: the template `disaggregation` mapping (T-REF-23), product family and revenue category (T-REF-20), contract region, channel and contract type (T-CON-01), performing entity, and the derived `timing_of_transfer` ∈ {`POINT_IN_TIME`, `OVER_TIME`} from `satisfaction_pattern` (E-19). Stamping point (rev 1.19, D-97 (19)): stages 05 and 06 stamp `ObligationState.dimensions` when they build the obligation (`state.disaggregation_tags`, from the canonical bundle); stage 14 copies them onto lines whose `account_role` is `REVENUE` only, never onto another role; the disclosure reads the lines, not the obligation |
| S15-R-16 | Report `disaggregation` groups the period's `REVENUE` lines (including JET-13 performing-side, JET-05c, JET-14 release and JET-01b 25-7 lines) by the selected dimensions per entity, book and period. Tie-out: total = revenue journal total (CTL-028). Under POL-191 `ELECT` (nonpublic) the pack still shows timing of transfer plus the qualitative factors (50-7). A `REVENUE` line without a value for a dimension falls in that dimension's `-` bucket (rev 1.19, D-97 (19)); S15-INV-03: for every dimension, Σ rows = the period's `REVENUE` journal total per entity |

#### 15.2.6 Contract-cost rollforward (340-40-50-2, 50-3, 50-5; REQ-CST-006; POL-196)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S15-R-17 | Per entity, book and cost kind (`OBTAIN`, `FULFILL`): opening carrying (previous snapshot), additions (JET-09a, 09a′), clawbacks, amortisation (JET-09b), acceleration on termination (JET-09e, `reason_code = TERMINATION_ACCELERATION`; the rollforward classifies an acceleration by `reason_code = TERMINATION_ACCELERATION` OR by the JET-09e template row, and a clawback by `reason_code = CLAWBACK` OR by the JET-09f template row, so a late-placed or voided movement whose line carries `LATE_EVENT` or `VOID` is still classified; D-98 candidate 19 (supervisor ruling on lane ENG-C8 Q-1, 2026-09-19)), impairment (JET-09c), impairment reversal (JET-09d, IFRS15), closing. Tie-out: opening + movements = closing = Σ `cost_asset_version.carrying_amount` (S11-INV-06). The expedients-used dataset (E-65 `EXPEDIENTS_USED`) lists the costs expensed under POL-140 with reason `EXPEDIENT_ONE_YEAR` and the use of 606-10-32-18 (POL-046) and the RPO exemptions (50-22; 340-40-50-5) |

#### 15.2.7 Lock snapshots (REQ-CLS-010; T-CLS-04, T-CLS-05; E-64)

| Snapshot kind (E-64) | Source report code | Row key (sort order) | Control totals |

| `CONTRACT_BALANCE_ROLLFORWARD` | `contract_balance_rollforward` | (entity, book, currency, balance, line code) | Σ opening, Σ closing per balance |
| `RPO` | `rpo` | (entity, contract, obligation) | Σ total, Σ per band, Σ excluded |
